### [1] [The microscopic origin of the hysteretic hydrogen adsorption in Double Wall Carbon Nanotubes bundles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20022)
*Carlos Cabrillo,Ricardo Fern\'andez-Perea,Felix Fern\'andez-Alonso*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文通过中子衍射和数值模拟阐明双壁碳纳米管（DWCNT）在超临界温度下滞后氢吸附的微观物理起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前观察到DWCNT在50 K、77 K和150 K下具有显著滞后氢吸附行为，具有实际意义，但其物理机制尚不清楚。

Method: 结合中子衍射（ND）信号的理论建模与基于DWCNT束沿管轴平移对称性的二维数值模拟，定量分析材料纳米结构对氢负载的响应。

Result: 发现随氢负载增加，DWCNT束结构发生膨胀；滞后吸附由两种微观机制导致：一是结构静态的、依赖H₂负载的间隙位间有效能垒；二是结构形变相关的机制，即束膨胀/收缩导致间隙间通道随H₂负载增加/减少而打开/关闭。

Conclusion: 研究识别了DWCNT中滞后氢吸附的两个微观起源，为理解超临界温度下碳纳米管储氢的滞后行为提供了结构—能量层面的解释。

Abstract: In a recent study we reported large hysteretic hydrogen adsorption in samples made out of Double Wall Carbon Nanotubes (DWCNT) at supercritical temperatures (50 K, 77 K and 150 K), a physical behavior of practical interest. Here, we center our efforts in elucidate its physical origin by means of neutron diffraction (ND) and computer simulations. After a detailed theoretical modeling of the ND signal we were able to quantitatively study how the nanostructure of the material reacts to the hydrogen loads. An expansion of the bundle structure with increasing hydrogen load was found. Taking advantage of the translational symmetry of a DWCNT bundle along the nanotube axis, a two dimensional numerical analogue of the system helped us to clarify the microscopic origin of the hysteresis. Two microscopic mechanisms are identified as responsible for the metastable hydrogen adsorption, one structurally static in character, namely, H$_{2}$-load dependent effective energy barriers between interstitials, the other related to the structural deformation, namely, the opening/closing of the channels between interstitials with increasing/decreasing H$_{2}$ loads owing to the concomitant bundle expansion/contraction.

</details>


### [2] [Multiple moir\'e polarization textures in twisted trilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20793)
*Haoshen Ye,Ying Zhou,Shuai Dong*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 在扭转三层范德华材料中引入相对莫尔相位作为额外结构自由度，可突破扭转双层极化织构拓扑固定的限制，并通过层间滑动调控净极化织构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭转双层中的莫尔调制堆叠与继承的滑动铁电性会产生非平庸极化织构，但对给定材料其极化织构拓扑基本由固有扭转堆叠景观决定，缺乏可调性。

Method: 以具有不同对称性的滑动铁电体 CrI3 和 BN 为代表，分析扭转三层中相对莫尔相位如何重塑三层堆叠景观，并研究层间滑动对净极化织构的调控；将多重莫尔极化织构归因于两个双层子系统原始莫尔图案的干涉。

Result: 相对莫尔相位可改变三层堆叠景观并调节净极化织构；在 CrI3 与 BN 中均可产生多重莫尔极化织构，其形成源于两个双层子系统莫尔图案的干涉。

Conclusion: 扭转三层中的相对莫尔相位提供了超越扭转双层固有堆叠景观的结构自由度，为滑动铁电体中莫尔极化织构的拓扑调控与设计提供了新途径。

Abstract: Twisted van der Waals layers exhibit nontrivial polarization textures from moir\'e-modulated stacking and the inherited sliding ferroelectricity. However, in any twisted bilayer, the topology of polarization texture is essentially fixed due to the inherent twist-stacking landscape for a given material. Here, this constraint is overcome in some twisted trilayers, where the relative moir\'e phase emerges as an additional structural degree of freedom. Using CrI$_3$ and BN as representative sliding ferroelectrics with distinct symmetries, it is clarified how the relative moir\'e phases reshape the trilayer stacking landscapes and tune the net polarization texture by layer sliding. The multiple moir\'e polarization textures can be attributed to the interference of original moir\'e patterns of the two bilayer subsystems.

</details>


### [3] [Large-scale Monte Carlo/Molecular Dynamics simulations with Atomic Cluster Expansion potentials in LAMMPS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21147)
*Lorenzo Piersante,Anirudh Raju Natarajan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种用于LAMMPS中ACE势MCMD模拟的MPI/OpenMP混合并行方案，使机器学习势在大尺度微观组织模拟中变得实用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数百千原子尺度的MCMD模拟通常依赖经验势，因为现有实现中机器学习原子间势在该尺度上进行MCMD不切实际。

Method: Monte Carlo移动通过局部能量变化评估并按MPI进程并行执行，分子动力学按OpenMP线程并行；固定CPU数时MC壁时可随MPI进程数成比例降低，较大模拟盒可减小自由能计算的统计误差。

Result: 在强非谐Hf-Ti体系中计算HCP和BCC相自由能，并在670,000原子的纳米晶Cu中模拟Zr偏聚；Cu-Zr的ACE势与经验势预测相似晶界成分，但晶内Zr溶解量不同。

Conclusion: 该方案使基于机器学习原子间势的MCMD模拟在微结构长度尺度上具有实际可行性。

Abstract: Monte Carlo/molecular dynamics (MCMD) simulations are widely used to study the properties of materials at the mesoscale. Simulations with hundreds of thousands of atoms usually rely on empirical potentials because MCMD with machine-learning interatomic potentials is impractical at this scale in existing implementations. Here, we introduce a hybrid MPI/OpenMP scheme for MCMD with Atomic Cluster Expansion (ACE) potentials in LAMMPS. Monte Carlo moves are evaluated from local energy changes and carried out in parallel over MPI ranks, while molecular dynamics is parallelized over OpenMP threads. At a fixed number of CPUs, the wall time of the Monte Carlo moves decreases by a factor equal to the number of MPI ranks. Larger simulation cells also reduce the statistical errors of free energy calculations because thermal fluctuations decrease with system size. We demonstrate the scheme by calculating free energies of the HCP and BCC phases in the strongly anharmonic Hf--Ti system and by simulating zirconium segregation in a nanocrystalline copper sample of 670,000 atoms. For Cu--Zr, an ACE potential and an empirical potential predict similar grain boundary compositions but different amounts of zirconium dissolved in the grains. The scheme makes MCMD simulations with machine-learning interatomic potentials practical at the length scales of microstructure.

</details>


### [4] [Atomistic Insights into the All-Optical Switching of GdCo Alloys with Minimal Gd Concentration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21410)
*Paul-Iulian Gavriloaea,Jackson L. Ross,Richard F. L. Evans,Roy Chantrell,St\'ephane Mangin,Oksana Chubykalo-Fesenko*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 原子自旋动力学模拟表明，在Pt/Co/Pt中少量界面Gd即可实现单脉冲全光螺旋度无关切换；磁化补偿并非必要条件，关键控制参数是Gd与热浴的有效自旋-热浴耦合强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期实验在仅含少量界面Gd的Pt/Co/Pt异质结构中观察到全光螺旋度无关切换（AO-HIS），挑战了AO-HIS通常需Gd基亚铁磁体接近磁化补偿的传统认识。作者旨在检验有效界面GdCo合金图像，并厘清物种相关自旋-热浴耦合如何决定反转所需的最低Gd浓度。

Method: 采用原子自旋动力学模拟，在均匀加热模型下比较两种体系：Gd局限于表面层的三单层Co薄膜，以及三维均匀合金化GdCo模型；通过改变Gd与Co相对热浴耦合强度及Gd浓度，分析瞬态反转行为、切换速度和随机性。

Result: 当Gd与热浴耦合显著弱于Co时，可在远离补偿点获得稳健的瞬态toggle-like反转；在λCo=0.02和λCo=0.001及选定脉冲参数下，总Gd浓度低至1.5 at.%仍可反转。提高Gd含量会加速反转，符合Gd作为角动量储库的解释；而稀释的界面Gd导致更慢、更随机的动力学。

Conclusion: 磁化补偿不是超快AO-HIS的根本必要条件，有效Gd自旋-热浴耦合是重要控制参数。与实验定量比较还需显式考虑光吸收、叠层相关热流、偶极场以及稳定的终态切换判据。

Abstract: Single-pulse all-optical helicity-independent switching (AO-HIS) is most commonly observed in Gd-based ferrimagnets near magnetization compensation. Recent experiments, however, demonstrated switching in Pt/Co/Pt-based heterostructures containing only a small amount of Gd at a Co/Pt interface. Here, we use atomistic spin-dynamics simulations to test an effective interfacial GdCo-alloy picture and to determine how species-dependent spin-bath coupling controls the minimum Gd concentration required for reversal. We compare a three-monolayer Co film with Gd confined to the surface layer and a three-dimensional uniformly alloyed GdCo model. Within the present uniform-heating model, robust transient toggle-like reversal is obtained far from compensation when the Gd coupling to the thermal bath is substantially weaker than that of Co; for {\lambda}Co = 0.02 and {\lambda}Co = 0.001, reversal occurs down to 1.5 at. % total Gd for selected pulse parameters. Increasing the Gd content accelerates reversal, consistent with the interpretation of Gd as an angular-momentum reservoir, whereas dilute interfacial Gd produces slower and more stochastic dynamics. These results show that compensation is not a fundamental requirement for ultrafast AO-HIS and identify the effective Gd spin-bath coupling as a key control parameter. Quantitative comparison with experiment will require explicit optical absorption, stack-dependent heat flow, dipolar fields, and a stable-final-state switching criterion.

</details>


### [5] [Lower Bound of Thermal Conductivity in Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18795)
*Yan Gao,Yafei Ding,Hongbin Xu,Zhaoxuan Tang,Fuqing Duan,Wenmei Luo,Zhensheng Zhang,Xiaohang Zhang,Guimei Zhu,Yahui Xue,Baowen Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作开发高效图案化与转移工艺，制备1–30 μm悬空双层石墨烯热桥，测得室温热导率约570 W/(m K)，且不随长度变化；结合HNEMD模拟指出仅约0.01单层非晶碳覆盖即可抑制ZA声子，从而给出由非晶表面散射主导的稳健热导率下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯本征热导率因物理与计量复杂性争议很大；热桥法测量可靠但样品制备困难，且表面污染与基底对ZA声子的影响机制尚不明确。

Method: 开发高效图案化与转移技术，系统制备并表征不同长度（1–30 μm）的悬空双层石墨烯热桥；开展严格表面清洁与无定形氮化硅基底对照；采用均匀非平衡分子动力学（HNEMD）模拟研究非晶碳覆盖对声子输运的影响。

Result: 含不可避免聚合物残留的悬空双层石墨烯表现出长度无关的室温热导率约570 W/(m K)，在250–350 K内近乎恒定，并对严格清洁和无定形SiN基底不敏感；HNEMD显示约0.01单层非晶碳覆盖即可显著抑制面外弯曲声学（ZA）声子。

Conclusion: 研究挑战了刚性基底比表面污染更强抑制ZA声子的主流观点，指出非晶表面散射主导一个稳健的热导率下界；该下界反映真实器件条件下的热传导，为声子输运基础研究和石墨烯热管理集成提供参考。

Abstract: The intrinsic thermal conductivity ($\kappa$) of graphene remains highly controversial because of physical and metrological complexities. Although the thermal bridge method enables reliable measurements, sample fabrication remains challenging. Here, we develop a highly efficient patterning and transfer technique to systematically fabricate and characterize suspended bilayer graphene (BLG) thermal bridges with lengths of 1-30 $\mu$m. Suspended BLG with unavoidable polymer residues exhibits length-independent thermal conductivity of approximately 570 W/(m K) at room temperature, remaining nearly constant from 250 to 350 K and robust to rigorous surface cleaning and amorphous silicon nitride substrates. Homogeneous non-equilibrium molecular dynamics (HNEMD) simulations show that amorphous carbon coverage of only approximately 0.01 monolayer is sufficient to suppress out-of-plane flexural acoustic (ZA) phonons. These findings challenge the prevailing view that rigid substrates suppress ZA phonons more strongly than surface contamination and identify a robust lower bound of $\kappa$ governed by amorphous surface scattering. This lower bound reflects heat conduction in graphene under realistic device conditions and provides a reference for fundamental phonon transport studies and the integration of graphene into thermal management applications in electronic devices and chips.

</details>


### [6] [A Practitioner's Guide to Materials Theory and Computation in the Age of Big Data and Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18797)
*Jack Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该专著以势能面和统计采样为统一主线，实践导向地系统概述计算材料科学，从经验力场、密度泛函理论、多体电子结构到高通量工作流、材料数据库和人工智能，并强调原子表示、图神经网络与生成模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算材料科学方法日益多样，原子模拟、高通量计算和人工智能快速发展，但从业者难以形成对其理论基础的连贯理解，因此需要一部面向实践、教学式且统一的计算材料科学综述。

Method: 基于作者研究经验，围绕势能面与统计采样，系统讨论经验力场、密度泛函理论、多体电子结构方法、统计采样和性质预测；进一步分析高通量工作流、材料数据库及人工智能的概率基础，并重点阐述原子表示、图神经网络和生成模型，以及对称性、群论、微分几何和随机过程的作用。

Result: 专著将经典物理理论与新兴数据驱动方法相连接，给出计算材料科学的统一视角，并识别面向物理知情、高效和可靠材料发现的机遇与挑战。

Conclusion: 势能面与统计采样可作为统一理解计算材料科学的核心框架，有助于弥合理论方法与数据驱动建模，并推动可靠、高效和物理知情的材料发现。

Abstract: Computational modelling has become a cornerstone of materials science, with advances in atomistic simulations, high-throughput computation, and artificial intelligence transforming how materials are understood, discovered, and designed. These developments build upon generations of theoretical innovations that have translated complex mathematical and physical principles into practical predictive tools. However, the growing diversity of computational methods presents challenges for practitioners seeking a coherent understanding of their theoretical foundations. Drawing upon the author's research experience, this monograph provides a practitioner-oriented, pedagogical overview of computational materials science, unified through the concepts of potential-energy landscapes and statistical sampling. It explores how these concepts underpin structural stability, phase behaviour, and electronic and magnetic properties, through a systematic treatment of empirical force fields, density-functional theory, many-body electronic-structure methods, statistical sampling, and property prediction. It further examines high-throughput workflows, materials databases, and the probabilistic foundations of artificial intelligence. Particular emphasis is placed on atomistic representations, graph neural networks, and generative models, highlighting the roles of symmetry, group theory, differential geometry, and stochastic processes in modern materials modelling. By connecting established physical theories with emerging data-driven approaches, this monograph offers a unified perspective on computational materials science and identifies opportunities and challenges toward physics-informed, efficient, and reliable materials discovery.

</details>


### [7] [Predictive sufficiency as an information microscope for time-resolved coherent scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18884)
*Christian Gutt,Amir Tosson,Nimmi Das Anthuparambil,Jovita Lukasik,Michael M\"oller*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出“信息显微镜”框架，用学习到的散斑序列表示检验所选XPCS观测量是否保留描述后续散射动力学所需信息，并用XPCS Alphabet寻找可实验测量、可解释的新观测量，以恢复丢失信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统XPCS主要用强度关联函数平均值描述弛豫，但会掩盖散斑涨落在时间上和倒空间中的演化，因此需要判断常用观测量是否丢失了关键动力学信息。

Method: 构建基于散斑序列学习表示的信息显微镜，检测当前观测量未包含的信息；再用XPCS Alphabet搜索实验可及的观测量来恢复这些信息，并在模拟Bragg散射、受控SAXS模型和二维玻璃形成体系中验证。

Result: 在Bragg散射中，散斑活动性与各向异性揭示关联形变和间歇滑移；在SAXS模型中，散斑活动性涨落对合作粒子运动的时空范围敏感；在二维玻璃形成体系中，保留时间分辨散斑关联和径向强度可恢复平均弛豫函数丢失的信息，且这些关联涨落在caging阶段增强，但不依赖单一主导关联模式。

Conclusion: 该工作表明学习可指导相干散射的物理分析，同时确保所得XPCS观测量保持可测量性和可解释性。

Abstract: X-ray photon correlation spectroscopy (XPCS) measures dynamics through the evolution of coherent speckle patterns, most commonly summarized by intensity correlation functions. These averages describe relaxation but can conceal how speckle fluctuations evolve in time and across reciprocal space. Here we introduce an information microscope that tests whether a chosen set of XPCS observables retains the information needed to describe subsequent measured scattering dynamics. A learned representation of the present speckle sequence indicates when information remains outside the chosen description; an XPCS Alphabet then searches for experimentally accessible observables that recover it. In simulated Bragg scattering, speckle activity and anisotropy reveal signatures of correlated deformation and intermittent slip. In a controlled SAXS model, fluctuations of speckle activity provide sensitivity to the spatial extent of cooperative particle motion. In a two-dimensional glass former, retaining time-resolved speckle correlations and radial intensity recovers information lost in the average relaxation function. The fluctuations of these correlations become stronger during caging, without selecting a single dominant correlated mode. This approach uses learning to guide the physical analysis of coherent scattering, while keeping the resulting XPCS observables measurable and interpretable.

</details>


### [8] [On-demand valley control in a WSe2/CrPS4 heterostructure via optically programmed magnetic order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18906)
*Muhammad Hassan Shaikh,Daria Blach,Harishankar Jayakumar,Brayan Eraso Solarte,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Yafei Ren,Archana Raja,Chitraleema Chakraborty*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作利用光可调磁序在WSe2/CrPS4异质结中实现了谷对称性破缺的可逆光开关，并将磁对称性、谷极化和激子弛豫集成于同一平台进行动态调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维半导体中谷自由度的光学控制对可扩展谷电子学至关重要；磁邻近效应虽可解除谷简并，但静态界面缺乏器件所需的动态调控能力。

Method: 构建WSe2/CrPS4异质结，采用偏振分辨与时间分辨光致发光谱，通过激发能相对于配体-金属电荷转移（LMCT）能隙的位置调控CrPS4磁序，并观测WSe2 A激子及低能激子（XLE）衰减动力学。

Result: 低于LMCT能隙激发时，CrPS4保持面外Néel矢量，在界面产生净面外自旋并实现自旋极化电荷转移，从而在无外磁场下形成稳健的A激子衰减偏振隙；高于LMCT能隙激发则偏振隙消失，表明Néel矢量转至面内，实现可逆切换并直接证明自旋极化电荷转移。相同光激发还可将XLE衰减动力学调控约42%，可能源于界面耦合改变。

Conclusion: 研究建立了首个由光动态调控磁对称性、谷极化与激子弛豫的多功能平台，为可重构谷电子学器件提供了新途径。

Abstract: The ability to optically control the valley degree of freedom in two-dimensional semiconductors is critical for scalable valleytronics. While magnetic proximity effects can lift valley degeneracy, static interfaces lack the dynamic control needed for devices. Here, we demonstrate on-demand optical switching of valley symmetry breaking in a WSe2/CrPS4 heterostructure by exploiting light-tunable magnetic order. Using polarization- and time-resolved photoluminescence, we show that excitation below the ligand-to-metal charge transfer (LMCT) gap preserves an out-of-plane N\'eel vector in CrPS4, generating a net out-of-plane spin at the interface and enabling spin-polarized charge transfer. This yields a robust polarization gap in the WSe2 A-exciton decay dynamics without an external magnetic field. Excitation above the LMCT gap extinguishes the polarization gap, indicating a rotation of the N\'eel vector to in-plane. This reversible switching provides direct evidence of spin-polarized charge transfer. Furthermore, the same optical excitation enables optical control of the decay dynamics of the low-energy excitons (XLE) by ~42%, which is potentially attributed to the modification of interfacial couplings. Our work establishes a first unique multifunctional platform where magnetic symmetry, valley polarization, and exciton relaxation are integrated and dynamically controlled by light.

</details>


### [9] [Adaptive nonorthogonal atomic orbitals refine charge transfer and bonding descriptors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19023)
*Emily Oliphant,Emmanouil Kioupakis,Wenhao Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文将COGITO从平面波DFT映射到自适应原子轨道所得到的高维成键信息，压缩为可解释、可迁移的共价成键、电荷转移和晶体依赖原子化学描述符，并用于区分成键类型与分解能带色散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一性原理计算隐含成键与电荷转移信息，但将周期性电子结构转化为可解释且可迁移的描述符仍很困难；静态或正交轨道基方法在稳定性和区分不同成键机制方面存在不足。

Method: 作者基于COGITO工具，将平面波DFT投影到可解释的最小自适应原子轨道基，再把高维成键信息约化为保留实空间相互作用来源的化学描述符；并与静态或正交轨道基方法比较，进一步将能带色散分解为按距离分辨的贡献。

Result: COGITO描述符在短程和长程成键分析中更稳定，更能区分离子键、共价键和金属键；还能可靠分解能带色散，并验证和补充硅低对称性导带底最小值的轨道起源。

Conclusion: 自适应COGITO描述符能够编码电子结构，为揭示导致电子性质的轨道相互作用和电荷转移提供了结构化路径。

Abstract: First-principles calculations implicitly encode bonding and charge transfer, but converting periodic electronic structure into interpretable and transferable descriptors remains challenging. Recently, we introduced Crystal Orbital Guided Iteration To atomic-Orbitals (COGITO), a tool for mapping plane-wave DFT back to an interpretable, minimal basis of adaptive atomic orbitals. Here, we show how the high-dimensional bonding information from COGITO can be reduced into chemically useful descriptors of covalent bonding, charge transfer, and crystal-dependent atomic chemistry without collapsing the real-space origin of the interactions. Compared to methods that use static or orthogonal orbital bases, COGITO descriptors of short- and long-range bonding show more stability and a better ability to distinguish between ionic, covalent, and metallic bonding regimes. Lastly, COGITO reliably decomposes band dispersion into range-resolved contributions, allowing us to verify and add to the orbital origins of silicon's low-symmetry conduction band minimum. Overall, the adaptive COGITO-based descriptors encode electronic structure, offering a well-structured route to expose the orbital interactions and charge transfer responsible for electronic properties.

</details>

*Haofu Wen,Xiaodong Guo,Xiaokang Xu,Peng Di,Xuebiao Ji,Ruoqi Wang,Shijun Yuan,Kunquan Hong,Qionghua Zhou,Qian Chen,Taishi Chen,Jinlan Wang*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单极反常能斯特热电器件依赖金属互连，导致严重焦耳耗散并降低封装密度，限制输出电压；因此需要无需无源金属桥的本征双极反常能斯特方案。

Conclusion: 该机制为紧凑、单片横向热电器件提供新路径，有望实现无界面寄生耗散的伏级电压输出。

Abstract: Unipolar anomalous Nernst thermopiles are critically plagued by metallic interconnects, which induce heavy Joule dissipation and compromise the module's packing density, ultimately capping voltage generation. Here, we circumvent these limitations by establishing an intrinsic bipolar anomalous Nernst effect (BANE) paradigm, enabling seamless series integration analogous to Seebeck n-p pairs without passive metallic bridges. Focusing on flat-band topological systems, through machine learning assisted screening across multiple target properties, we identified 249 BANE materials, exhibiting near-room-temperature polarity switching. Guided by this predictive framework, we experimentally realized this phenomenon in single crystals of the Weyl ferromagnet, Co$_2$TiGe. Strikingly, while its intrinsic anomalous Hall conductivity (AHC) remains rigorously temperature-invariant up to the Curie temperature, the anomalous Nernst signal undergoes a sharp polarity inversion across $T \approx 225$ K. First-principles calculations attribute this behavior to the interplay between flat bands and Weyl nodes, where thermal depopulation shifts the chemical potential across the band edge, inverting the sign of $\partial \sigma_{xy} / \partial \epsilon$ via the Mott relation. This work opens an avenue toward compact, monolithic transverse thermopiles capable of volt-scale generation free from interfacial parasitic dissipation.

</details>

*Zipeng Xu,Kento Katagiri,Tatiana Pikuz,Wanghui Li,Anirudh Hari,Dorian P. Luccioni,Sophie E. Parsons,Yuichi Inubushi,Kohei Miyanishi,Hirotaka Nakamura,Tadashi Togashi,Makina Yabashi,Toshinori Yabuuchi,Norimasa Ozaki,Leora E. Dresselhaus-Marais*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冲击后失效预测需要理解压缩阶段形成的结构在卸载时如何演化；金刚石中晶体塑性与局域非晶化均可协调冲击剪切，但三维缺陷组织及其对拉伸损伤局域化的影响尚不清楚。

Method: 在X射线自由电子激光上开展多泵浦-探测延迟的相位衬度增强射线照相，并结合流体力学计算和分子动力学模拟；通过绕冲击轴旋转观察方向分析投影特征角度，模拟压缩与卸载过程。

Result: 线性特征首先出现在塑性波前之后，而波前附近材料仍高度压缩；投影特征角度倾向于有限{111}片层并沿接近<112>的面内方向伸长。分子动力学显示压缩时形成非晶带与堆垛层错互连网络，优先位于面内剪切牵引较大的{111}变体上，其投影可定性再现旋转导致的特征可见度下降；模拟卸载时拉伸在这些预存非晶带内启裂。

Conclusion: 压缩阶段形成的晶体学组织化缺陷网络为卸载阶段拉伸损伤提供优先位点；该路径可能也适用于其他强共价固体。

Abstract: Predicting failure after shock loading requires understanding how the structures created during compression evolve as the material unloads. In diamond, both crystal plasticity and localized amorphization have been reported to accommodate shock-induced shear. However, it remains unresolved how the resulting defect structures organize in three dimensions during compression and how this organization influences where tensile damage localizes during release. Here we couple phase-contrast-enhanced radiography at an X-ray free-electron laser with simulations to address this question in single-crystal diamond. By combining radiographs at multiple pump-probe delays with hydrodynamic calculations, we show that linear features first become visible behind the plastic wavefront while material near the front remains highly compressed. By rotating the viewing direction about the shock axis, we find that the projected feature angles favor finite $\{111\}$ sheets elongated along in-plane directions close to $\langle112\rangle$. Molecular dynamics simulations show that an interconnected network of amorphous bands and stacking faults forms during compression, preferentially on $\{111\}$ variants with larger in-plane shear traction. Projections of this network qualitatively reproduce the observed loss of feature visibility with rotation. During simulated release, tension opens local cracks within these pre-existing amorphous bands. These results support a pathway in which the crystallographically organized defect network formed during compression provides preferred sites for tensile damage during release. This pathway may also operate in other strongly covalent solids.

</details>


### [12] [SPARC: Evidence-guided adaptive model composition for materials discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19050)
*Sissi Feng,Yuchen Han,Runze Zhang,Jiaru Bai,Brandon Sutherland,Ali Shayesteh Zeraati,Jason Hattrick-Simpers,Varinia Bernales,Al\'an Aspuru-Guzik,Yang Bai*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: SPARC 是一个闭环实验框架，通过折外预测评估竞争模型族，并用稀疏后验组合按证据支持的预测贡献分配模型影响，同时以预测离散度和残差定义信任来指导探索与测量验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环实验不仅需选择下一个实验，还需判断哪些已有测量和模型关系有足够证据支持并影响决策；随着实验证据积累，这种支持应被持续修正。

Method: SPARC 用折外预测评估竞争模型族，使每个模型在未参与拟合的观测上受检验；通过稀疏后验组合依据折外证据支持的预测贡献分配模型影响，并保留多个模型族的互补贡献。候选相关的预测离散度和残差变异性定义信任，与预测响应共同调节探索；独立诊断对已有测量进行优先级排序以开展前瞻性验证，并同时报告模型导出的信任。

Result: 在八个已发表材料数据集上，SPARC 在六个数据集上取得高于文献报道的 R^2，相对提升最高达 77%，且未使用若干已发表流程所用的密度泛函理论或实验衍生描述符。在自动化碱性析氧反应 campaign 中，11 个 SPARC 选择的实验有 8 个过电位低于 300 mV，最佳为 242 mV；初始 125 个实验中仅 18 个达到该水平。重复前被优先验证的测量平均偏差为 76 mV，匹配对照为 27 mV。

Conclusion: SPARC 将实验证据转化为修正模型影响的依据，用于指导探索并识别值得验证的测量。

Abstract: Closed-loop experimentation requires more than selecting the next experiment. It must also determine which existing measurements and model-derived relationships are sufficiently supported to influence that decision. Here we introduce SPARC (Sparse Predictive Aggregation of Regression Components), a framework that continually revises this support as experimental evidence accumulates. Competing model families are evaluated through out-of-fold predictions, so that each model is tested on observations not used in its corresponding fit. Sparse posterior composition assigns model influence according to predictive contributions supported by the out-of-fold evidence, retaining complementary contributions from several model families. Candidate-dependent predictive dispersion and residual variability define trust, which modulates exploration alongside the predicted response. Independent diagnostics also prioritize existing measurements for prospective verification, with model-derived trust reported alongside them. Across eight published materials datasets, SPARC achieved higher $R^2$ than the corresponding literature-reported values in six, with relative gains of up to 77% without the density-functional-theory- or experiment-derived descriptors used by several published pipelines. In an automated alkaline oxygen-evolution-reaction campaign, 8 of 11 SPARC-selected experiments reached overpotentials below 300 mV, with a best value of 242 mV, compared with 18 of 125 initial experiments. Measurements prioritized for verification before repetition subsequently deviated by 76 mV on average, compared with 27 mV for matched controls. SPARC therefore turns experimental evidence into a basis for revising model influence, directing exploration and identifying measurements that warrant verification.

</details>


### [13] [Interplay of interfacial energetics and selective Ni segregation in the morphology of co-precipitates in low-alloy steels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19060)
*Jia-Hong Ke,Anshul Kamboj,Mukesh Bachhav*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文用量子级界面能计算与蒙特卡洛模拟统一量化低合金钢中Cu-rich与Mn-Ni-Si-rich析出相的界面偏聚和共析出行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CRP与MNSP常形成核壳或附着形貌，但控制其稳定性与形貌的界面能及偏聚机制尚缺乏定量认识。

Method: 采用Density-functional-theory训练的cluster expansion结合lattice Monte Carlo，在同一Hamiltonian下计算Fe/CRP、Fe/MNSP和Cu/MNSP界面，并进行Gibbs吸附分析。

Result: 界面能排序为γ_Cu/MNSP < γ_Fe/CRP ≲ γ_Fe/MNSP，黏附功为正、铺展参数为负，支持Mn-Ni-Si在CRP上部分覆盖；仅Ni偏聚至Fe/CRP界面，Mn和Si单独偏聚弱但在Ni存在时富集并与Ni共偏聚；Ni形成约单原子层界面层，过剩量7.4–9.6 atoms/nm^2，具B2-like局域有序，能量降低随Ni覆盖度增加；1 at.%基体Ni时Fe/CRP自由能降低约0.15 J/m^2。

Conclusion: 这些界面系数与偏聚趋势将偏聚、界面能和共析出联系起来，可用于低合金钢的成分优化。

Abstract: Coupled precipitation and interfacial segregation in low-alloy steels change precipitate stability and morphology, which influence strengthening and embrittlement. Cu-rich precipitates (CRPs) and Mn-Ni-Si-rich precipitates (MNSPs) frequently form core-shell or appendage morphologies, but the interfacial energetics controlling this change are not well quantified. We combine a density-functional-theory-trained cluster expansion with lattice Monte Carlo simulations to evaluate the Fe/CRP, Fe/MNSP and Cu/MNSP interfaces within one Hamiltonian. The three coefficients based on excess internal energy follow gamma_Cu/MNSP < gamma_Fe/CRP <~ gamma_Fe/MNSP and give a positive work of adhesion and a negative spreading parameter, consistent with favorable Mn-Ni-Si attachment to a CRP and partial coverage. Only Ni segregates to the Fe/CRP boundary. Mn and Si segregate weakly on their own but become more enriched when Ni is present and co-segregate with Ni at the boundary. Ni forms an interfacial layer about one monolayer thick with an excess of 7.4-9.6 atoms/nm^2, develops B2-like local order and shows an energy reduction that increases with Ni coverage. At 1 at.% matrix Ni, a Gibbs adsorption analysis gives a Fe/CRP free-energy reduction of about 0.15 J/m^2. These interface coefficients and segregation trends connect segregation, interface energy and co-precipitation and support compositional optimization in low-alloy steels.

</details>


### [14] [A mesoscale damage model for void nucleation and growth in shock loaded microstructure informed by computer vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19159)
*Abhijith Thoopul Anantharanga,Brandon Runnels*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作将基于计算机视觉的孔洞形核倾向预测与连续损伤模型耦合，用于钽板撞击层裂模拟，并证明微观结构代表性对自由表面速度预测至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延性材料高应变率层裂由孔洞形核、长大和合并控制，但损伤模型中的形核判据多为随机且常忽略微观结构；需要发展具有微观结构意识的形核模型。

Method: 用晶粒取向和晶界能预测易感场，以局部最大主应力作为力学门槛控制形核概率；将其与晶体塑性模型耦合到拉格朗日冲击模拟中，并选择性解析微观结构以降低计算成本；在196 m/s飞片速度下标定。

Result: 196 m/s下模拟自由表面速度与实验在冲击到达、峰值状态、卸载和回拉信号上吻合，偏差主要限于后期振荡；无需改参数即可迁移到214 m/s并准确复现FSV；合成微观结构对比表明FSV显著依赖所纳入的微观结构，只有代表性微观结构给出正确预测；孔洞主要在晶界和三叉晶界形核。

Conclusion: 将微观结构感知的形核概率与损伤/晶体塑性框架结合，可提高层裂模拟的预测能力，并表明必须采用代表性微观结构才能可靠预测自由表面速度。

Abstract: Spallation in ductile materials under high strain rates occurs through the process of void nucleation, growth, and coalescence. Void nucleation is known to depend on factors ranging from microstructural to stress state. The criteria for void nucleation in damage models are stochastic, and microstructural awareness is often neglected. Recently, it was shown that nucleation propensity can be estimated using computer vision. In the present work, this is integrated with a continuum damage model framework for comprehensive simulation of spallation in tantalum under plate impact. Void nucleation probability is governed by a susceptibility field, predicted from grain orientations and grain boundary (GB) energy, with a mechanical barrier set by the local maximum principal stress. This is coupled to a crystal plasticity model within a Lagrangian shock simulation, with microstructure selectively resolved to reduce computational cost. The model is calibrated at a flyer velocity of 196 m/s, where the simulated free surface velocity (FSV) matches experimental measurements of shock arrival, peak state, release and the pullback signal measured in flyer impact experiments, with some deviations confined to the late time ringing. With no further parameter changes, the model transfers to a 214 m/s case and accurately reproduces the FSV. To demonstrate the microstructure-awareness of the nucleation model, a selection of synthetic microstructures is presented for comparison. In the resolved microstructure, voids nucleate predominantly (though not exclusively) at GBs and triple junctions. It is shown that the FSV differs significantly based on the incorporated microstructure, yielding the correct FSV prediction only in the case of representative microstructures.

</details>


### [15] [Exploring Energy Landscapes for Functional Materials: Ga vacancies in $\beta$-Ga$_{2}$O$_{3}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19365)

Result: 揭示了复杂空位构型与迁移路径；温度依赖分析表明最可能迁移序列可随温度改变，其机制由网络的全局性质而非单纯最高能垒决定；可视化、平均首次通过时间和热容将景观组织与弛豫时间尺度和热力学可观测量联系起来。

Conclusion: 该接口将能量景观方法扩展到 ASE 兼容原子模型，为结合全局优化、过渡态搜索、动力学网络分析和热力学表征与最新机器学习原子间势提供了通用框架。

Abstract: Energy landscape exploration requires extensive energy and force evaluations, making efficient access to interatomic potentials an important practical challenge. We present a persistent socket interface that connects the landscape exploration tools in the GMIN, OPTIM, and PATHSAMPLE programs to potentials available through the Atomic Simulation Environment (ASE). The persistent design keeps both the calculator and socket connection active throughout the search, avoiding repeated initialisation. We apply this framework to understand and predict the properties of Ga vacancies in $\beta$-Ga$_2$O$_3$ using two NequIP-based potentials, and construct kinetic transition networks describing complex vacancy configurations and migration pathways. Temperature-dependent analysis of the dominant paths reveals how the most probable migration sequence can change, with the mechanism governed by global properties of the network, rather than simply the highest energy barrier. Visualisation, mean first-passage times, and heat capacities enable us to connect the organisation of the landscape to relaxation time scales and thermodynamic observables. The interface extends energy landscape methods to ASE-compatible atomistic models, providing a general framework for combining global optimisation, transition state searches, kinetic network analysis, and thermodynamic characterisation with recent developments in machine-learned interatomic potentials.

</details>


### [16] [Porosification of the dielectric layer in alternating-current electroluminescence devices enables high-resolution tactile imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19384)
*Mingwei Gu,Licheng Li,Zeyuan Zhou,Ying Yang,Zixiang Nie,Xingyu Ma,Junshuai Chen,Lin Zheng,Jinhui Gu,Jia-Sen Xie,Ziling Wei,Mengxing Huang,Jin Ge*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉触觉传感器依赖 bulky 光学架构，占用空间大，限制灵巧机械手的功能设计；因此需要更薄、更紧凑且高分辨的触觉成像方案。

Method: 构建ACEL自发光触觉成像器件：可压缩多孔介电层在压力下改变电容并调控发光，形成非线性电容依赖的发射窗口以实现可调压力—光转换；通过孔径工程抑制局部发光缺陷；连续电致发光界面直接呈现接触图案，无需电场计算重建，并可层压至CMOS图像传感器。

Conclusion: 该自发光触觉成像方法可减少高分辨率触觉成像所需空间，为灵巧机械手提供更大设计自由度，并展示出紧凑、高分辨接触感知的应用潜力。

Abstract: Vision-based tactile sensing enables high-resolution contact perception, yet bulky optical architectures consume valuable space and constrain the functional design of dexterous robotic hands. Here we introduce a self-emissive tactile imaging strategy based on alternating-current electroluminescence (ACEL) devices, in which a compressible porous dielectric encodes contact pressure into spatial emission patterns without external illumination or discrete sensing pixels. We identify a nonlinear capacitance-dependent emission window that provides a basis for tunable pressure-to-light conversion. Pore-size engineering further suppresses local emission deficiencies, reducing spatial intensity variation by nearly ninefold and enabling faithful imaging of contact features down to 100 {\mu}m. The continuous electroluminescent interface also reproduces complex contact patterns without computational reconstruction of electrical fields. Direct lamination of the ACEL device onto a complementary metal-oxide-semiconductor (CMOS) image sensor is expected to enable compact contact imaging in an assembly less than 5 mm thick, substantially thinner than conventional vision-based tactile sensors. This approach could reduce the space required for high-resolution tactile imaging, offering greater design freedom for dexterous robotic hands.

</details>

Motivation: 气固反应动力学对热化学储热等应用很重要，但材料开发中的实验评估仍是瓶颈，需要能原位、化学选择性监测小样品反应的方法。

For CaO, some differences were observed between the FTIR and TG results, possibly because CaO is sensitive to differences in gas-phase conditions, particularly the initial increase in humidity, between the two measurements. The spectra distinguished molecular-water uptake in La$_2$(SO$_4$)$_3$ and CaSO$_4$ from hydroxide formation in CaO. They also detected carbonate formation during CaO hydration. Thus, \textit{in situ} time-resolved FTIR separately monitored individual chemical reactions, whereas TG recorded only their combined effect on the sample mass. Result: 多数相分离体系界面能为正，多数化合物有序体系为负，反映异类原子成键有利；例外表明界面化学偏好可不与体相行为一致，共格应变可反对有序化，稳定化合物可采用不同于计算超晶格的结构。界面能与晶格错配无明确相关性，而共格应变能随错配增大；相分离体系最低界面能多沿最密排取向，有序体系无此趋势。与已报道界面能和二元相图定性一致，但定量一致性受参考态、结构约束和温度影响。

Most phase-separating systems exhibit positive interfacial energies, whereas most compound-ordering systems exhibit negative values, reflecting favorable unlike-atom bonding. Exceptions show that the chemical preference at a particular interface need not coincide with bulk phase behavior: coherency strain can oppose ordering, and stable compounds can adopt structures distinct from the calculated superlattices. No clear correlation of interfacial energy with lattice mismatch is observed, whereas coherency strain energy increases with mismatch. For phase-separating systems, the lowest interfacial energies generally occur along the closest-packed orientations, while this trend is absent for ordering systems. Comparisons with reported interfacial energies and binary phase diagrams show qualitative consistency, although quantitative agreement varies with reference states, structural constraints, and temperature. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散衰减与波状振荡虽常用于表征热传导，却不能唯一识别热流输运机制；需要区分本构响应中的不同热流通道，并将其与可观测的温度涨落联系起来。

Method: 建立两级谱理论：先在有限波矢下将本构响应分解为弛豫通道和流式通道，以极点与热流留数表征；再通过能量守恒将二者联合响应与平衡温度涨落关联；并对17 K的NaF进行第一性原理计算。

Result: NaF计算显示，Guyer--Krumhansl约化中产生微扰非局域修正的流式诱导模式耦合，会非微扰地将孤立动量组织弛豫通道转变为占主导的复共轭流式对，破坏G--K的单弛豫通道本构结构；但振荡温度动力学仍可在其预测振荡窗口的短周期边界以下存在。更短周期下，热流权重在多个碰撞耦合流式通道间重分配，并趋近独立阻尼的声子流式；升温可消除孤立动量组织扇区，而振荡温度动力学仍持续。

Conclusion: 流体力学本构描述的失效可反映热流输运通道的重组，而非振荡温度动力学的消失；与温度测量吻合不足以验证约化理论的本构结构。

Abstract: Diffusive decay and wave-like oscillations in temperature dynamics are commonly used to characterize heat conduction, yet these signatures do not uniquely identify the underlying heat-current transport mechanisms. Here we reveal a spectrum of constitutive heat-current channels whose microscopic organization can change without a corresponding qualitative change in observable temperature dynamics. We develop a two-level spectral theory that first resolves the finite-wave-vector constitutive response into relaxational and streaming channels characterized by their poles and heat-current residues, and then connects their combined response to equilibrium temperature fluctuations through energy conservation. First-principles calculations for NaF at 17~K reveal that the same streaming-induced mode coupling responsible for the perturbative nonlocal correction in the Guyer--Krumhansl (G--K) reduction drives a nonperturbative transformation of the isolated momentum-organized relaxational channel into a dominant complex-conjugate streaming pair. This transformation breaks down the single-relaxational-channel constitutive structure of G--K, while oscillatory temperature dynamics persist below the short-period boundary of its predicted oscillatory window. At shorter periods, heat-current weight redistributes among multiple collision-coupled streaming channels before approaching independently damped phonon streaming. Increasing temperature provides a distinct route beyond hydrodynamics by eliminating the isolated momentum-organized sector, even as oscillatory temperature dynamics persist. Thus, the breakdown of a hydrodynamic constitutive description can reflect a reorganization of heat-current transport rather than the disappearance of oscillatory temperature dynamics, and agreement with temperature measurements alone does not validate the constitutive structure of a reduced theory.

</details>

*Changzhi Ai,Xueping Qin,Dimosthenis Sokaras,Frank Abild-Pedersen,Johannes Voss*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: IANN 是一个开源 PyTorch 框架，将多种等变图神经网络 MLIP 架构统一到同一数据对象、训练器和部署流程中，并发布大规模 PaiNN 基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 MLIP 虽接近 DFT 精度且成本更低，但各先进架构以独立代码库发布，数据格式、配置、训练循环和 MD 接口不统一，导致同一数据集上的架构比较及训练模型的生产部署需大量重工程。

Result: 可在百万级结构公共数据集上以相同条件比较各架构；发布基于 MPtrj、Alexandria、OMat24、MatPES、OC20、OC22、OC25、MPr2SCAN 和 MatPESr2SCAN 共 4190 万条精选 DFT 结构训练的 PaiNN 基础模型，覆盖 PBE、RPBE 和 r2SCAN 泛函。

Conclusion: IANN 通过统一训练、比较和部署路径显著降低 MLIP 软件碎片化带来的重工程成本，支持从工作站到多节点 GPU 的一致运行，并促进自主代理集成。

Abstract: Machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) now approach density functional theory (DFT) accuracy at a fraction of its cost, but the practical benefit is limited by software fragmentation: each state-of-the-art architecture is released as a separate codebase, with its own data format, configuration system, training loop and molecular-dynamics interface. Comparing two architectures on the same dataset, or moving a trained model into a production simulation, therefore requires substantial re-engineering. We introduce IANN (InterAtomic Neural Network), an open-source PyTorch framework that reimplements equivariant graph neural networks - PaiNN, NequIP, MACE, Allegro, EquiformerV2, EquiformerV3 and UMA - behind a unified data object, a unified trainer and a single deployment route, so that the architecture is a one-word change in a configuration dictionary. Forces, stresses and virials are obtained for every architecture from one analytic differentiation of the energy with respect to the edge vectors of the atomic graph, exact under periodic boundary conditions. Distributed training is configured automatically from the SLURM, PBS or MPI environment, so the same script runs unchanged from a workstation to a multi-node GPU allocation. Self-describing checkpoints serve directly as ASE calculators, as ensembles reporting uncertainties, or as TorchScript models run inside LAMMPS through bundled C++ pair styles with single- and multi-GPU inference. Trained on a common dataset of millions of structures, the architectures can be compared under identical conditions. We further release PaiNN foundation models trained on 41.9 million curated DFT structures from MPtrj, Alexandria, OMat24, MatPES, OC20, OC22, OC25, MPr2SCAN and MatPESr2SCAN, spanning the PBE, RPBE and r2SCAN functionals. Additionally, IANN provides an agent interface to facilitate integration with autonomous agents.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁磁与反铁磁Kitaev耦合对磁阻挫和磁相的影响不同，但二者在同一材料中的共存此前未被探索；作者旨在寻找其微观实现机制。

Abstract: Kitaev couplings with ferromagnetic (FM) or antiferromagnetic (AFM) character shape magnetic frustration and phases differently in real materials, yet their coexistence within a single material remains unexplored. Here, we propose Jahn-Teller distortion as a route to realizing such coexistence on inequivalent bonds. These opposite signs are robust against variations in Hubbard $U$, functionals, and strain. These findings not only establish Jahn-Teller distortion as a microscopic route to mixed-sign Kitaev exchange, but also open a new avenue for exploring novel magnetic phases.

### [23] [Metallic or insulating magnetic phases of transition-metal oxides in light of the Slater vs Mott controversy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19888)
*Xiuwen Zhang,Jia-Xin Xiong,Alex Zunger*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作用参数无关的类平均场DFT表明，即使磁性基元被量子势垒电子隔离、缺乏长程有序，仍可出现本征带隙，因此AFM/PM相带隙不必源于长程有序或强关联U。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁d电子氧化物中反铁磁长程有序相与顺磁无序相的绝缘/金属性起源长期存在Slater-Mott争议：Slater观点依赖长程有序导致能带排斥成隙，若顺磁相无长程有序则应金属化，但这与大量实验冲突；Mott-Peierls-Hubbard观点强调局域强关联U，但U在周期体系中难以测量或计算，常被当作可调参数。

Method: 采用参数无关的类平均场密度泛函理论（DFT），允许能量降低的自发对称破缺；将局部磁性基元视为宏观AFM或PM相的构筑单元，并通过量子势垒将其彼此电子隔离，例如把磁性NiO6八面体局域嵌入宽禁带MgO中，以检验无长程有序时的电子结构。

Result: 发现这些被电子隔离、长程有序消失的局部磁性基元仍表现出本征带隙；因此AFM或PM相中的带隙形成并不需要长程有序，也不需要以强关联U为核心解释。

Conclusion: AFM/PM磁d电子氧化物的带隙可在类平均场能带图像中通过对称破缺理解，而非必须归因于Slater型长程有序或Mott型强关联；这为Slater-Mott争议提供了参数无关的新视角。

Abstract: Magnetic d-electron oxides usually have a long-range-ordered ground state and an above-ground-state disordered para phase. The historical Slater-Mott controversy still reverberating to date pertains to understanding the origin of the insulating vs metallic nature of these two phases. Slater suggested that the insulating character evolved from the long-range order (LRO) antiferromagnetic (AFM) cell-doubling, leading to energy band repulsion and thus to forming a band gap. The reliance on LRO to explain gapping suggests that the absence of LRO in the spin-disordered para-phase would deprive the system from band repulsion, thus predicting a metallic paramagnetic (PM) phase. This, however, conflicts with numerous current observations. The alternative Mott-Peierls-Hubbard view abandons the mean-field-like band structure view emphasizing instead the local interelectronic repulsion U as a strong correlation signature of gapping both at the presence or absence of LRO. However, U is not an easily measured or calculated quantity in periodic systems, a situation often leading in practice to treating U as an adjustable parameter. The current work uses a parameter-free mean-field-like approach (Density Functional Theory, DFT), allowing energy-lowering symmetry breaking. This is done by considering local magnetic motifs as building blocks of the macroscopic AFM or PM phases. It is found that even when such motifs are isolated electronically from each other (i.e. vanishing LRO) by embedding them in quantum barriers (such as magnetic NiO6 octahedra surrounded locally by wide-gap MgO) they exhibit an intrinsic band gap. Thus, the origin of the band gaps in AFM or PM phases need not be LRO or strong correlation and can be understood within the mean-field-like band structure approach with symmetry breaking.

</details>

Abstract: Extreme ultraviolet (XUV) second harmonic generation (SHG) combines interface selectivity with core-level element specificity but has so far been limited to linear absorption edges. Abstract: Machine learning of $GW$ in solids has largely focused on prediction of scalar quasiparticle energy corrections. Abstract: Electronic transport in polyanionic battery cathodes proceeds through small-polaron hopping between transition-metal sites and is commonly assumed to be tightly coupled to the motion of charge-compensating mobile ions. Using olivine Li$_x$MnPO$_4$ as a prototypical platform, we use first-principles molecular dynamics with extended Hubbard functionals to follow, in real time and at finite temperature, the adiabatic evolution of Mn oxidation states and atomic sublattices. We find that polaron hopping and cation migration are dynamically decoupled: Mn$^{2+}$/Mn$^{3+}$ valence exchanges occur frequently while the intercalated ions remain confined to their sites, and hopping is gated by the local oxygen framework. Freezing the six oxygen atoms of an individual MnO$_6$ octahedron excludes that site from the hopping network, while increasing the fictitious oxygen mass slows down electronic transport. Each valence exchange coincides with a concerted breathing of the donor and acceptor octahedra ($\Delta V \simeq 2$ $\r{A}^3$), with their volume asymmetry crossing zero at the transition, consistent with the Holstein-Marcus coincidence condition. The hopping rate yields an activation energy of $E_a = 0.20 \pm 0.03$ eV, closely matching the elastic energy scale of the two coordination cages ($\lambda_V/4 \approx 0.20$ eV). These results identify the oxygen coordination cage, not the mobile cation sublattice, as the dynamical gate for small-polaron transport: a site can accept the electron only if its cage can breathe, and the barrier is governed by cage elasticity. Selective constraints and mass scaling applied to first-principles dynamics provide a general route to link electronic and lattice degrees of freedom in redox-active materials, opening the door to framework engineering in intercalation and mixed ionic-electronic conductors.

Abstract: Two-dimensional van der Waals heterostructures (vdWHs) based on transition metal dichalcogenides (TMDs) have emerged as promising platforms for next-generation low-power electronics and spintronics. Here, we investigate the structural, electronic, and spin properties of MoTe$_2$/HfS$_2$ vdWHs using first-principles density functional theory calculations. Our results reveal an intrinsic type-III (broken-gap) band alignment, making this heterostructure highly attractive for tunnel field-effect transistor (TFET) applications. We further show that external vertical electric fields, twist engineering, and both out-of-plane and in-plane strain provide efficient means to tune the band alignment, charge transfer, and Rashba parameters. In particular, electric fields and mechanical strain can drive reversible transitions between type-II and type-III band alignments while simultaneously modifying the Rashba spin texture and the band-to-band tunneling (BTBT) window. The twisted heterostructure with $\theta = 21.79^\circ$ exhibits similar tunability, including strain-induced transitions between distinct band-alignment regimes. These findings establish MoTe$_2$/HfS$_2$ vdWHs as versatile multifunctional platforms combining electrically tunable Rashba spin splitting with controllable type-II/type-III band alignment, offering promising opportunities for next-generation TFETs and spintronic devices.

The band gap, determined from the PL spectra, decreases with increasing temperature at low temperatures, reaches a minimum near 50 K, and then increases at higher temperatures. This behavior contrasts with the monotonic decrease typically observed in conventional semiconductors. The observed temperature dependence is well reproduced by a Bose-Einstein oscillator model with two characteristic phonon energies of approximately 8.9 meV and 1.2 meV. Furthermore, first-principles phonon calculations for the optimized crystal structure revealed peaks in the phonon density of states near these two characteristic energies. These results suggest that electron-phonon interactions play an important role in the anomalous temperature dependence of the optical band gap in TiNCl, with low- and higher-energy phonons contributing to the low-temperature decrease and the higher-temperature increase, respectively.

Abstract: Machine-learning force fields are capable of simulating billions of atoms, but usually require high-performance supercomputing clusters. We show that a universal graph neural network force field, ALIGNN-FF, evaluates energies and forces of more than one billion atoms on desk-side computers such as the NVIDIA GB10, which has 128 GB of unified memory, by using a cube-and-halo decomposition. The halo contains every atom within the model's 20 {\AA} receptive field, which makes the result exact. One GB10 holds 216,000 atoms with forces as a single graph. The cube-and-halo decomposition removes this limit, enabling an evaluation of 2 billion atoms on nine GB10s. We also demonstrate three case studies: a billion-atom polycrystal, a 102-million-atom microelectronic gate stack and a 1.07-million-atom satellite tobacco mosaic virus in water. This brings single-point evaluations at experimentally relevant sizes within reach of one research group. The code is available at: https://github.com/atomgptlab/scalingmat.

</details>

Under OER conditions, however, the highly active as-grown state is metastable: near-surface N depletion and Fe0 oxidation/removal accompany rapid initial activity decay toward a quasi-steady state. pH-dependent kinetics and post-OER anion redistribution are consistent with possible lattice-oxygen participation. In this representation, the kagome-lattice problem maps onto a triangular lattice of trimers, while the nearest- and next-nearest-neighbor hoppings of the original lattice generate complex hopping amplitudes between neighboring trimers. This formulation provides a transparent picture of how spin chirality and SO coupling can be recast as an effective Zeeman field that lifts the degeneracy of the $m= \pm 1$ states, breaks time-reversal symmetry, and thereby gives rise to weak orbital ferromagnetism and the AHE. The resulting phase diagram includes metallic phases, topologically trivial insulating phases, as well as several topological phases characterized by nonzero Chern numbers and the quantum anomalous Hall effect. We further identify an alternative route to time-reversal-symmetry breaking and orbital magnetization, driven by an emergent gauge field arising solely from complex inter-trimer hoppings and requiring no effective Zeeman field.

Concurrently, magnetic reconstruction profoundly reshapes the reciprocal-space topology, increasing the number of Weyl-point pairs from 42 in the collinear ferromagnetic state to 115 in the non-coplanar state. Abstract: Dissociative water adsorption is a key step in heterogeneous and electrochemical catalysis, but on finite Ni nanosystems the OH$^*$ and H$^*$ fragments can occupy many combinations of surface sites, making exhaustive density functional theory (DFT) screening impractical. We introduce a graph neural network (GNN) framework with an adsorbate-free representation: candidate O- and H-binding sites are defined by sets of Ni atoms (\textit{anchors}), and each site pair is encoded as a graph constructed solely from these anchors and their local Ni environment. Using only MACE-relaxed bare Ni geometries as input, separate GNNs predict the DFT-reference adsorption energies of OH$^*$+H$^*$ configurations and whether the proposed binding sites would be retained upon DFT relaxation, eliminating the need to construct or relax adsorbates during screening. Trained across Ni nanoclusters and Ni(111)/Ni(100) surfaces, the energy model achieves a mean absolute error of 0.031 eV on the internal validation set and demonstrates transferability to unseen Ni$_{55}$, Ni$_{91}$, and Ni$_{130}$ clusters and larger surface cells, particularly for relative-energy ranking, although quantitative accuracy varies across systems. These independent tests also support the retention classifier's use as a conservative filter for selecting configurations likely to preserve their proposed binding sites. Application to Ni nanoparticles containing up to $\sim$3000 atoms identifies favorable co-adsorption motifs and shows that lower O-site coordination generally stabilizes the dissociated state, whereas H-site coordination has a weaker influence. The framework thus offers an efficient route to screening adsorption energetics, with potential extensions to other metals and adsorbate combinations.

Abstract: The low-voltage plateau-like capacity of hard carbon anodes is widely regarded as a key signature of sodium storage in closed pores, often defining the performance of Na-ion batteries. Nucleation can be accelerated by introducing defects at the pore walls, although an excessively high defect concentration slows bulk diffusion. This work shows that not all closed pores contribute equally to Na storage: some may be thermodynamically favorable but kinetically inactive under realistic charge/discharge rates. Because of this, the inverse design of hard carbon electrodes should target a kinetically accessible pore-size and defect-density window that balances high plateau-like capacity with fast-charging performance.

Abstract: Density-functional calculations feed materials databases and machine-learning models, and are declared converged when energy changes and residuals fall below thresholds. A variational formulation of self-consistency in the space of Kohn-Sham Hamiltonians yields a descent probe that exposes lower states accessible from a returned solution, and a frozen-orbital Janak test detects such an inconsistency. </details>

Abstract: Quantum calculations are expected to broaden the range of strongly correlated phenomena in solids accessible to first-principles calculations. The quantum Krylov diagonalization method, or in a broader sense, the quantum subspace expansion method (QSE) offers an advantage over other variational methods in terms of numerical stability. In this work, we propose a boson representation of electronic systems with the aim of reducing QSE quantum circuit depth, where electron-hole pair bosons serve as the fundamental degrees of freedom. We benchmark the boson representation in comparison with a corresponding fermionic QSE and CCSD for an equidistant and a dimerized configuration of a periodic hydrogen chain \ce{(H2)_n}. Despite the drastic approximation we reveal the boson QSE scheme provides practically useful accuracy in the ground state energy. Our results support its applicability to realistic quasi-two- and three-dimensional electronic systems.

Abstract: We present a transferable Density Functional Tight Binding (DFTB) model for bismuth with explicit spin-orbit coupling that enables exploration of the structural degrees of freedom governing topological properties with minimal computational resources. We have used our DFTB model to run high-throughput calculations at a scale that would be significantly more expensive with standard Kohn-Sham Density Functional Theory (DFT) to determine a strain-buckling map of electronic and topological properties for bismuthene, where we identify regions of band gap closure and band inversion as a function of structural parameters. We show that our approach yields an accurate band structure for the rhombohedral phase, where small band gaps exist at or near time-reversal-invariant momentum (TRIM) points. We then demonstrate the transferability of our model to bismuth properties not included in our training data through determination of the rhombohedral phase phonon density of states and energetic ordering of additional bismuth solid phases. Finally, we have validated our topological predictions through comparison to DFT results for the optimized bilayer geometries, a band inversion order parameter, and the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ invariant as a function of monolayer unit cell length. Our efforts establish DFTB as a rigorous approach for topological characterization while yielding approximately three orders of magnitude increase in computational efficiency relative to DFT. Our model can thus serve as a computational tool for topological materials discovery by enabling large-scale computational screening to address the potentially complex relationship between physical-chemical properties and the resulting topological classification.

Method: 从文献汇编 21 种金属、氧化物、半导体、聚合物、陶瓷和碳材料的波长相关折射率与消光系数，保留各文献来源及原始条件；用物理计算链计算不同入射角、板厚和温度下的反射率、透射率、吸收率和发射率，并结合 AM0 太阳光谱计算聚光太阳辐射下的吸收、反射和透射通量。

Conclusion: SpaderOptics 为太阳-材料相互作用提供了一致、可追溯的光学与热辐射性质数据库，但适用范围目前限于 AM0 条件。

Abstract: Solar-matter interactions are important in many scientific and engineering applications. Modeling them needs consistent optical and thermal-radiative properties over the full solar spectrum. Here we present SpaderOptics, a database for 21 materials: metals, oxides, semiconductors, polymers, ceramics, and carbon. It compiles wavelength-dependent refractive indices and extinction coefficients from the literature. Each literature source is kept separate, together with its original conditions. One physics-based calculation chain turns these data into reflectivity, transmissivity, absorptivity, and emissivity for different incidence angles, slab thicknesses, and temperatures. Combined with the Air Mass Zero (AM0) solar spectrum, the properties give absorbed, reflected, and transmitted solar flux for concentrated sunlight. Equilibrium temperatures are given for both a theoretical lumped body and for a slab body, in which thermal conduction is considered. All data are limited to the solid state, up to a material-specific upper temperature limit. Currently, the database applies to extraterrestrial (AM0) sunlight only. A graphical App is also provided for exploring and visualizing the database.

</details>

Abstract: Cryogenic atom probe tomography (cryo-APT) is increasingly being used to map the three-dimensional distribution of highly reactive species in heterogeneous materials, including those found in battery systems. However, site-specific preparation of localised features and liquid-solid interfaces remains challenging, particularly when regions of interest (ROIs) are concentrated along electrode edges that are difficult to access using conventional mounting geometries. Here, we present a cryo-APT workflow incorporating an upright-mounting procedure that enables targeted specimen preparation of localised regions using plasma focused ion beam (PFIB) microscopy and milling under cryogenic conditions. We first demonstrate the approach using an electrolyte-wetted graphite electrode, successfully preparing and analysing a specimen containing both an edge-localised electrolyte deposit and the underlying graphite and Cu current collector. We then apply the workflow to a cycled graphite electrode, targeting Li dendrites concentrated around the electrode perimeter and successfully preparing and analysing the targeted Li-rich region. Beyond battery systems, this approach offers potential for site-specific cryo-APT of edge-localised features across a broader range of material systems, where conventional specimen preparation geometries may limit access to ROIs.

Abstract: The electronic structure of a magnetic crystal depends jointly on its spin configuration and the on-site Hubbard correction U, requiring a separate DFT+U calculation for each combination. We introduce U-HamGNN, an equivariant network that takes these two inputs directly and predicts spin-resolved Hamiltonians across diverse crystal chemistries. Abstract: Predicting correlations between source generations can support uncertainty estimation and variance reduction in Monte Carlo transport.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Atomistic-continuum simulations of complex wrinkling deformations in large-area multilayer two-dimensional (2D) materials are computationally expensive. We address these challenges using two-dimensional functional principal component analysis (FPCA). The proposed FPCA representation reduces deformation fields containing millions of degrees of freedom to latent spaces with only tens of dimensions while preserving the continuity of the deformed surface. In addition, prescribed homogeneous boundary constraints are satisfied exactly through an appropriate choice of basis functions in the FPCA representation. A neural network is then trained to predict the principal components from the system and loading parameters. The resulting ML-predicted deformation fields are used as high-quality initial configurations for the atomistic-continuum solver, which subsequently performs energy minimization to obtain equilibrium solutions. The framework is demonstrated for multilayer graphene subjected to uniaxial compression, biaxial compression, and combined biaxial compression and simple shear. For the most computationally intensive large-strain cases, the proposed approach achieves speedups of at least 6.8 times while converging to final energies comparable to, and in some cases lower than, those obtained using conventional atomistic-continuum initialization.

Abstract: Simulating hypersonic flows in thermochemical nonequilibrium requires solving the Navier-Stokes equations for reacting, possibly ionised mixtures. Compact high-order computations of viscous two-temperature air flows on unstructured grids have not gone beyond P = 2. We present an implicit Flux Reconstruction (FR) method for such flows, built on three ingredients. First, the solver stores and updates the logarithms of the partial densities and temperatures, making every state admissible without a positivity limiter and, for ionised mixtures, imposing charge neutrality consistently with the implicit update. Second, shocks are captured by subcell order blending, extended to steady implicit computations on curved Gauss-Legendre meshes, with second-order subcell blending in subsonic regions and an automatic freeze of the blending near convergence. Third, a pseudo-transient Newton method with a physicality-limited update and automatic CFL control reaches steady state from the free stream with the same settings at every order. The design order is verified up to sixth order on a manufactured solution for five-species air with finite-rate chemistry and vibrational relaxation. Steady viscous solutions up to sixth order are obtained for five- and eleven-species air on the High Enthalpy shock tunnel G\"ottingen (HEG) cylinder and for eleven-species air on the RAM-C II vehicle. To our knowledge, this work gives the first design-order verification of a high-order method with finite-rate air chemistry and vibrational relaxation, and the first high-order finite-element-type computations of viscous eleven-species, two-temperature air. On the HEG cylinder, the stagnation-point heat flux is within 2% of finite-volume references and within 6% of the measurements, with a comparable number of degrees of freedom to second-order finite-volume computations but on meshes with 20 to 40 times fewer elements.

</details>

Abstract: Equation-derived neural integration can execute prescribed dynamics without training, while informative ensemble learning can estimate uncertain parameters and identify governing interactions without requiring differentiability. As a first step toward flood modeling applications, we present $\Phi$SpIN-SW, its prescribed-physics realization for the rotating shallow-water equations. Compatible C-grid operators on spherical Voronoi meshes define a computational graph that retains non-polynomial operations and advances the state through recurrent neural integration compiled with JAX. Analytical benchmarks characterize numerical errors, and global and coastal experiments closely reproduce reference solutions generated using the Model Prediction Across Scales (MPAS) model. At approximately 14~km global resolution, A100, H200 and B200 GPUs attain 9-, 19- and 34-fold higher timestep throughput than a 96-core MPAS-SW CPU reference under matched workloads. Conclusion: 卷积化公式适合Neural Physics实现和GPU加速，为洪水建模及大规模地球物理模拟提供了一种可扩展方法。

Abstract: This paper presents a method for solving the shallow water equations on highly distorted structured grids using convolution-based operators following the Neural Physics approach. By mapping distorted physical grids to a uniform computational domain with Jacobian transformations, the governing advection and diffusion operators are reformulated in a structured convolutional form. This enables the use of efficient stencil-based operations and, following the Neural Physics concept, we do this through convolutional layers whose weights are determined by the chosen numerical methods rather than by training.
  The method introduces consistent mass-lumped formulations, conservative and non-conservative advection forms, and a diffusion operator, all expressed as convolutions. A nonlinear Petrov-Galerkin stabilisation is incorporated to control numerical oscillations on distorted meshes. Two approximations for the Laplacian operator are also proposed to accelerate pressure and diffusion solves. The framework is validated using the Carlisle 2005 flood event, demonstrating accurate predictions of water depth and velocity on distorted meshes for both linear and quadratic discretisations.
  The results show that distorted structured grids can retain conservation, stability and accuracy, whilst adapting the mesh statically. The convolutional formulation makes the method well suited to Neural Physics implementations and GPU acceleration, offering a scalable approach for flood modelling and large-scale geophysical simulations.

*Jingyang Huang,Sihong Shao,Yuxuan Zhang*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 分析表明粒子湮灭不仅控制粒子增长，还将随时间指数增长的方差界替换为线性增长界，从而缓解数值符号问题；频繁湮灭限制方差但可能增加累积偏差；数值实验验证了理论方差行为和偏差-方差权衡，所提调度位于兼顾精度与效率的经验Pareto前沿低成本区域。

Abstract: Particle annihilation (PA) plays an indispensable role in alleviating the numerical sign problem in long-time simulations with the Wigner branching random walk (WBRW) method. However, the effect of PA frequency on long-time accuracy remains poorly understood, leaving its choice largely empirical. To provide systematic guidance for PA scheduling, we establish an overall error estimate for PA through a uniform mesh, separating WBRW variance, resampling variance and squared bias. The analysis shows that, beyond merely controlling particle growth, PA replaces the exponential-in-time variance bound with a linear-in-time bound, thereby alleviating the numerical sign problem. It also reveals bias-variance trade-off induced by PA frequency: frequent PA limits variance but can increase accumulated bias. More importantly, building on the newfound error structure, we propose a variance-constrained cost optimization strategy for adaptive PA scheduling across several PA methods. An intrinsic-time reparameterization provides a common clock for variance control, PA scheduling, and branching dynamics under time-dependent potentials. Numerical experiments validate the theoretical variance behavior and bias-variance trade-off, and results of our schedule populate the low-cost regime of the empirical Pareto frontier objecting to balance accuracy and efficiency.

</details>

Abstract: FiuMD is a GPU molecular dynamics engine that advances many independent biomolecular simulations as a single batch, even when the systems differ in size, topology, force-field parameters, periodic box, timestep, or thermostat parameters. All systems are concatenated into global tensors, so the classical interaction terms and a shared TorchMD-Net neural network potential are evaluated for the whole batch at once, while each system keeps its own integration and thermostat. FiuMD started as a fork of TorchMD and reworks its system representation, force evaluation, and integration to handle such heterogeneous batches. We test it with coarse-grained C$\alpha$ models of chignolin, Trp-cage, BBL, and $\lambda$-repressor. In double precision, forces within a heterogeneous batch agree with isolated FiuMD and TorchMD evaluations to within the precision of repeated evaluations, with a maximum relative difference of $7\times10^{-16}$ across 60 systems. Over 1000 steps without a thermostat, batched trajectories deviate by at most $2.5\times10^{-11}$ Angstrom from isolated ones, even when periodic images lie within the neural network cutoff. With a different target temperature for each system of the ensemble, the mean and width of each system's kinetic temperature match the values expected from its own thermostat. We show that when individual simulations are too small to fill the GPU, batching can improve throughput several-fold over TorchMD. On a single GPU, batching four different protein systems achieves a 4.6-7.8-fold speedup per step compared with sequential TorchMD simulations. Compared with sequential homogeneous FiuMD batches, heterogeneous batching provides up to a 4-fold speedup for smaller batches while maintaining comparable performance for larger ones.

Abstract: Discrete element method (DEM) simulations are widely used to investigate granular and particulate systems, but their application often requires specialized expertise in particle physics, numerical modelling, and simulation post-processing. Large language models (LLMs) offer new opportunities to simplify these workflows by enabling users to interact with simulation tools through natural language. This study offers GranularGPT, an LLM-assisted agentic framework for end-to-end DEM simulation workflows, from natural-language problem descriptions to simulation execution and automated post-processing. The framework enables users to generate DEM simulation cases, execute and monitor simulations, and automatically extract and report physical and process-level metrics. Evaluation across 60 normal-condition cases covering 12 DEM scenarios and parameter variations achieve a simulation success rate of 91.7%. In the error-handling evaluation, all 10 repairable errors are detected and 9 are successfully resolved, while all 5 non-repairable cases are correctly identified and reported. The framework automatically extracts a range of DEM-relevant metrics, including particle size distributions, packing and density measures, velocity profiles, discharge rates, mixing indices, contact force characteristics, and thermal quantities. GranularGPT provides a foundation for improving the accessibility and efficiency of DEM simulation workflows while reducing the specialized expertise required for their operation.

</details>


### [56] [Parametrised-Quadratic Interface Reconstruction and Geometric Velocity Extension in the Volume-of-Fluid Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20802)
*V M S K Minnikanti,Wenyi Yan,Shyamprasad Karagadde*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于局部参数曲线界面重构的保守VOF框架PI-VOF，用于固定笛卡尔网格上的几何界面传播，并实现近二阶界面演化与尖锐Stefan条件耦合。

Abstract: A conservative Volume-of-Fluid (VOF) framework using local parametric-curve interface reconstruction (PI-VOF) is developed for geometric interface propagation on fixed Cartesian grids. While VOF methods achieve conservative and second-order accurate interface advection under prescribed velocity fields, normal-velocity-driven interface propagation requires the interfacial velocity to be extended to neighbouring staggered-grid locations.
  Existing studies on interface evolution employ auxiliary distance functions, kernel smoothers, or high-resolution VOF in multiple grids to compute propagation velocities and address first-order-accuracy limitations. In contrast, PI-VOF directly employs higher-order parametric interface geometry for local normal-velocity extension, aiming to achieve second-order accurate interface evolution on fixed Cartesian grids. The resulting velocity field is employed in conservative geometric advection using a directionally split Eulerian-implicit and Lagrangian-explicit (EI-LE) scheme, while the reconstructed interface provides the geometric information required for curvature evaluation and the ghost-fluid finite-difference discretisation of sharp interfacial Dirichlet conditions. Static reconstruction and reversible-vortex advection tests demonstrate higher-order interface geometry and approximately second-order transport convergence, with mass conservation to the chosen tolerance on refined grids. Prescribed normal-velocity and curvature-dependent propagation tests establish near second-order convergence. Coupled Stefan benchmarks involving capillarity and attachment kinetics demonstrate approximately second-order thermal convergence and decreasing interface errors under mesh refinement. The results establish a geometric formulation connecting conservative VOF transport, local normal-velocity extension, and sharp Stefan conditions for evolving interfaces.

</details>


### [57] [Local topology hallucinations in universal machine-learned interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21489)
*Xiaoqing Liu,Yangshuai Wang,Lei Shen*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 论文揭示通用机器学习原子间势存在“局部拓扑幻觉”：不同模型极小值可能弛豫到同一参考势能面盆地，因而模型间一致并不能证明预测的亚稳态彼此不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测亚稳态要求机器学习势保留参考势能面的极小值与鞍点；若曲率符号或盆地拓扑预测错误，模型可能虚构出并不存在的亚稳态。

Method: 作者定义局部拓扑幻觉为不同模型极小值弛豫到同一参考盆地，比较MACE、CHGNet、M3GNet与DFT的曲率符号，并在LiGe2S中检验模型极小值、鞍点及前瞻性方向测试。

Conclusion: 仅凭模型间一致性无法确认预测极小值是不同亚稳态；需借助参考势能面或DFT验证曲率符号与盆地拓扑。

Abstract: Predicting metastable states with universal machine-learned interatomic potentials requires that they preserve the minima and saddles of the reference potential-energy surface. Here we identify local topology hallucination, in which distinct model minima relax into the same reference basin. This failure arises from mode-selective curvature-sign errors shared by MACE, CHGNet and M3GNet. Curvature signs predicted by two of the three models agreed with density-functional calculations in 55 of 57 positive but only 5 of 56 negative cases. In LiGe$_2$S, two MACE minima connected by a verified saddle relaxed into one density-functional basin. Reference relaxation likewise returned nine of ten selected model minima to their reference basins. A directional test with two displaced reference calculations rejected 9 of 12 prospectively tested model instabilities. Model agreement alone therefore cannot establish that predicted minima are distinct metastable states.

</details>

Abstract: Optical cavities offer opportunities to control quantum materials, but radiative leakage and vibrational relaxation call for microscopic theories that treat interactions and dissipation on equal footing. We develop a Keldysh-Lindblad nonequilibrium Green's function framework for interacting electrons coupled to quantized photon and phonon fields. The formulation accommodates boson-number-nonconserving Hamiltonians and dissipative channels that combine electronic transitions with bosonic processes. We derive the mixed electron-boson diagrammatic expansion and the corresponding Kadanoff-Baym equations, retaining environmental noise, initial correlations, and interaction-induced memory. The equal-time equations govern the electronic density matrix and the bosonic covariance. As an illustration, we construct jump operators that preserve a semiconductor with filled valence bands and an empty photon field. Within a mean-field treatment, this state remains stationary although its two-time propagators exhibit dissipative broadening. The framework opens new avenues for studying correlated dynamics in lossy cavities and vibrational environments.

Abstract: Accurately accounting for nuclear quantum effects (NQEs), including zero-point energy and nuclear tunneling, is essential for a reliable prediction of the structural, thermodynamic, and dynamical properties of chemical systems, particularly those involving light atoms. However, incorporating NQEs could render simulations of large-scale chemical systems computationally prohibitive, particularly when combined with expensive electronic-structure calculations. One potential avenue for reducing cost is to use bead-Fourier path integrals (BF-PIs). In Paper I, we introduced the BF centroid molecular dynamics (BF-CMD) method, in which BF-PIs were used to compute an effective potential for CMD, and in Paper II, we introduced BF ring polymer MD (BF-RPMD), taking the BF-PI and using it for approximate real-time dynamics. Both papers featured a series of model systems and were able to reduce the number of beads needed to converge dynamical properties by a factor of 2 to 4, depending on the system. In this work, we report the first implementation and application of the BF-PI framework to atomistic simulations using liquid bulk water and hexagonal ice as test cases. As in our previous papers, we find that adding just a single Fourier component to the path yields a 4- and 8-fold reduction in the number of beads while achieving results that agree with reference PI simulations for liquid water and ice, respectively. We also demonstrate that we can reduce simulation costs by up to 5.5x compared to standard PIs by reducing the number of force calculations and normal-mode transformations.

</details>

*Motoyuki Shiga*

Abstract: Brownian chain molecular dynamics (BCMD) [M. Shiga, J. Comput. Chem. 43, 1864 (2022)] was introduced as a practical path-integral-based approach to approximate quantum vibrational dynamics in molecular and condensed-phase systems. Its equations of motion combine Newtonian dynamics of the centroid variables with overdamped Brownian dynamics of the non-centroid path variables. Here, we introduce a path-resolved quasi-density-operator (path-QDO) representation by extending the QDO construction underlying centroid molecular dynamics to the full discretized imaginary-time path. For two position-diagonal observables, this representation gives an exact expression for the Kubo-transformed correlation function in the fine-discretization limit, without requiring either observable to be linear. BCMD can be viewed as a phenomenological, time-homogeneous Markov approximation to the exact reduced propagation in these path variables, although its generator is not derived from the path-QDO formulation. We also formulate and validate a multiple-time-step algorithm that allows pseudo-Hamiltonian errors in the deterministic propagation to be controlled by separating the Brownian refresh interval from the inner integration time step. We test the path-QDO representation on one-dimensional models and present BCMD infrared spectra of q-TIP4P/F ice, ambient water, and hot water, comparing them with published results from other approximate quantum-dynamics methods.

</details>

TL;DR: GPRidge 是一个基于高斯过程（GP）的库，用于加速最小能量路径和第一阶鞍点计算，提供编译语言接口和 Python 绑定。它利用 GP 预测迭代调整原子笛卡尔坐标，并将路径计算得到的 GP 能面和实测能量/力用于鞍点局部精修；新评估会更新代理模型并检查候选几何。分子体系接受条件为实测力收敛且独立 Hessian 仅有一个负特征值，若出现第二个负特征值则触发逃逸并重新精修。该方法支持分子的联合逆距离 GP 和周期体系的局部 GP 残差。

  <summary>Details</summary>
