Method: 使用 GP 预测引导坐标迭代调整；将拟合的 GP 能量面与实测能量、力传递给局部鞍点精修；新评估用于改进代理模型并检查候选几何。分子计算以实测力收敛和独立 Hessian 指数一为接受标准，第二个负特征值触发逃逸与再精修；周期体系采用局部 GP 残差。

Result: 在匹配的 Baker-Chan 反应上，GPRidge 比 CI-NEB 和 OCI-NEB 分别少用 7.8 倍和 7.0 倍能量-力评估，并将含验证的 CI-NEB 时间减少 41%。20 个 Birkholz-Schlegel 鞍点全部通过独立指数一检查，而同一路径极大值启动的经典 dimer 方法为 17 个；在这 17 个反应上，GP dimer 阶段少用 6.4 倍评估。72 个 Pt(111) 路径均满足 OptBench 力判据，七聚体和岛重构问题的中位评估次数分别为 30 和 23，而 CI-NEB 为 778 和 443。

Conclusion: GPRidge 能高效且可靠地加速最小能量路径与鞍点搜索，显著减少能量-力评估并提高鞍点验证通过率，适用于分子反应和周期表面体系。

Abstract: GPRidge, a Gaussian-process (GP) library for accelerating calculations of minimum energy paths and first-order saddle points with compiled-language and Python bindings, is presented. An evaluation controller uses GP predictions to iteratively adjust the Cartesian coordinates of the atoms. The fitted GP energy surface and measured energies and forces pass from the path calculation to local refinement of the saddle point. New evaluations improve the surrogate and check the candidate geometry. For the molecular calculations, acceptance requires measured-force convergence and an independent Hessian with only one negative eigenvalue. A second negative eigenvalue triggers an escape and renewed refinement. The controller supports a joint inverse-distance GP for molecules and local GP residuals on periodic systems. Applications to the 24 Baker-Chan and 20 Birkholz-Schlegel molecular reactions using the Universal Model for Atoms (UMA-S-1.1), and to 72 Pt heptamer and island rearrangements on Pt(111) using a Morse potential, are presented. On matched Baker-Chan reactions, GPRidge uses 7.8- and 7.0-fold fewer energy-and-force evaluations than climbing-image nudged elastic band (CI-NEB) and off-path climbing-image nudged elastic band (OCI-NEB), respectively, reducing validation-inclusive CI-NEB time by 41%. All 20 Birkholz-Schlegel saddle points pass the independent index-one check, compared with 17 for the classical dimer method started from the same path maxima; on those 17 reactions, the GP dimer stage uses 6.4-fold fewer evaluations. All 72 Pt(111) paths meet the OptBench force criterion, with median evaluation counts of 30 and 23 for the heptamer and island problems, against 778 and 443 for CI-NEB.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FLiBe中氚可溶为T2或TF，二者溶解度差异大；渗透实验给出接近HF的溶解度和与T2压力平方根成正比的通量，通常被解释为T2解离，需厘清微观机制。

Result: 模型复现实验密度和中子结构因子，但近液相线时过流；T2和TF溶解度与实验一致且均不解离；TF溶解度更高，除非盐强还原否则持有大部分溶解氚，恒氧化还原电位下其浓度随T2压力平方根变化；He不改变T2溶解度，He气泡按Henry常数吸收T2并在表面富集TF；T2扩散略快于实验，TF和T-比T2慢2–3倍。

Conclusion: 渗透实验的平方根压力依赖不必然意味着T2解离，而可由TF主导溶解及氧化还原平衡解释；该势为FLiBe中氚化学与气泡行为提供微观预测工具。

Abstract: Tritium produced in molten 2LiF-BeF$_2$ (FLiBe) may dissolve as T$_2$ or as TF, which differ greatly in solubility. Permeation experiments give a solubility close to that of HF and a flux proportional to the square root of the T$_2$ pressure, which has usually been taken as evidence of dissociation. We have developed a fixed-charge classical potential for FLiBe containing atomic tritium, T$_2$, TF, the hydride ion T$^-$ and helium, fitted only to periodic DFT calculations for hydrogen. The nuclei are classical, so the solubilities apply to all hydrogen isotopes. The host reproduces the measured density and neutron structure factor but is too fluid close to the liquidus. The calculated solubilities of T$_2$ and TF agree with experiment, and neither molecule dissociates in the melt. Because TF is much more soluble, it holds most of the dissolved tritium unless the salt is strongly reducing, and at constant redox potential its concentration varies with the square root of the T$_2$ pressure, which accounts for the permeation results without dissociation. Helium does not change the solubility of T$_2$: a helium bubble in the melt takes up T$_2$ as its Henry constant predicts, while TF is enriched at the bubble surface. T$_2$ diffuses somewhat faster than measured, and TF and T$^-$ two to three times more slowly than T$_2$.

</details>

Abstract: Machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs) are trained on energies, forces and stresses, yet their frozen representations can predict other materials properties. We probe ORB-v3 and UMA-S across layers, read-outs and equivariant channels for nine properties of 154,875 Materials Project crystals. Both models outperform composition-only descriptors on all targets, including five outside their training objectives; errors in physical units help assess potential screening applications. Optimal depth varies by property, consistent with a physical-scale ordering. Random-weight controls reveal strongly property-dependent benefits of pretraining. UMA-S's rotation-invariant angular channels improve crystal-system classification, while higher scores from its full tensor disappear under rotation; matched-model controls do not isolate equivariance as the explanation. Accuracy varies across different probes, but nearest-neighbour prediction often approaches XGBoost performance, even though materials do not form distinct clusters according to their properties. We also test whether a linear predictor trained on one model's embeddings can predict properties using the other model's embeddings after a learned linear transformation. Predictive accuracy is largely preserved, despite differences in which atoms the two models place close together. These results guide the reuse of frozen MLIP representations for materials prediction.

</details>


### [65] [Variational and geometric time-dependent density-functional theory of electronic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21431)
*\'Eric Canc\`es,Th\'eo Duez,Jari van Gog,Asbj{\o}rn B{\ae}kgaard Lauritsen,Mathieu Lewin,Jakob Oldenburg,Julien Toulouse*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该文从几何视角统一比较变分TDDFT与几何TDDFT，并为后者建立TDKS方案、精确条件和算子可逆性基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TDDFT的核心难题是构造能重现给定含时电子密度的多电子波函数；其几何解释自然导致以实局部势约束密度的变分TDDFT和以虚局部势或Hermitian非局域势约束密度的几何TDDFT两种表述。

Method: 针对连续电子体系发展统一几何框架，系统比较两种表述，建立相应的含时Kohn-Sham方案，推导几何TDDFT的精确条件，并用泛函分析讨论两种表述所涉算子的性质及可逆性充分条件。

Result: 给出包括因果性、时间反演对称性、均匀坐标缩放、平移不变性、能量与动量运动方程、单电子自相互作用和绝热极限在内的精确条件，并对部分论述提供严格数学证明及算子可逆性条件。

Conclusion: 该统一视角阐明了变分TDDFT与几何TDDFT的数学和概念关系，并为几何TDDFT中近似方法的发展奠定基础。

Abstract: A central problem in time-dependent density-functional theory (TDDFT) is to construct a time-dependent many-electron wave function that reproduces a prescribed time-dependent electron density. This problem admits a natural geometric interpretation, which in turn leads to at least two distinct formulations of TDDFT. The first is the usual variational formulation of TDDFT, in which the density constraint is enforced with a real-valued local potential. The second is geometric TDDFT, introduced in recent works, in which the same density constraint is enforced with an imaginary local potential, or equivalently with a Hermitian nonlocal potential. In this work, we develop this unified viewpoint for continuous electronic systems and systematically compare the two formulations. We formulate the corresponding time-dependent Kohn-Sham schemes and derive a number of exact conditions for geometric TDDFT, including conditions associated with causality, time-reversal symmetry, uniform-coordinate scaling, translational invariance, energy and momentum equations of motion, one-electron self-interaction, and the adiabatic limit. The paper also contains a mathematical section where we provide a fully rigorous justification of some parts of the text. We particularly discuss the mathematical properties of two operators underlying the two formulations of TDDFT and establish sufficient conditions for their invertibility. We explain the functional-analytic subtleties underlying the geometric interpretation of TDDFT and the two formulations of TDDFT. This unified perspective clarifies the mathematical and conceptual relation between variational and geometric TDDFT and provides a foundation for the development of approximations within geometric TDDFT.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子模拟器是量子算法开发与原型验证的基础，但软件栈及 CPU/GPU 后端的选择通常缺乏面向实际应用的证据。

Method: 在量子硬件无关条件下，系统比较 CUDA-Q 与 Qiskit 在 CPU/GPU 后端、单/双精度下的表现；测试覆盖 GHZ 态制备、QFT/QFT† 镜像电路、VQE 能量估计（UCCSD 与深度缩减的 LCNot-UCCSD）以及最多 32 量子比特氢链直接采样。

Result: GPU 加速双精度通常带来 2×–10× 加速，最宽电路可达 100× 以上，并在固定计算内存下决定最大可模拟规模；CPU statevector 后端和 Qiskit Aer 在少量子比特及宽而浅电路上仍有竞争力甚至更优。该指南使 QSCI(LCNot-UCCSD)-RBM 工作流从 CPU 上 16 量子比特活性空间扩展到 GPU 上 32 量子比特（10 电子、16 轨道），完成 N2/cc-pVDZ 的 20 点势能面扫描。

Conclusion: 基于应用负载的原则性模拟器选型可直接扩展科学计算可达范围，并加速量子算法原型开发。

Abstract: Quantum computing simulators underpin the development and prototyping of quantum algorithms, yet the practical choice between competing software stacks and their CPU and GPU backends is rarely guided by application-oriented evidence. We present a systematic, quantum hardware-agnostic evaluation of two widely used stacks, CUDA-Q and Qiskit, selected as the simulators closest to production execution paths, across CPU and GPU backends, and across single- and double-precision arithmetic. The evaluation spans foundational utility circuits (GHZ-state preparation and QFT/QFT$^\dagger$ mirror circuits) through applied quantum chemistry (Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE) energy estimation with standard UCCSD and a depth-reduced LCNot-UCCSD ansatz, and direct sampling on hydrogen chains up to 32 qubits). The results yield concrete selection guidance: GPU-accelerated double precision delivers consistent speedups, typically $2\times$ to $10\times$ for tractable application workloads and substantially larger at the widest circuits up to $100\times$ or more, and governs the maximum simulable system size under fixed compute memory, while CPU statevector backends and Qiskit Aer remain competitive, and at times preferable, at low qubit counts and wide but shallow circuits. We demonstrate the practical payoff of this guidance by extending an in-house developed QSCI(LCNot-UCCSD)-RBM electronic-structure workflow, previously constrained to a 16-qubit active space on a CPU, to a 32-qubit (10 electrons, 16 orbitals) active space with GPU acceleration, completing a 20-point potential-energy-surface scan of N$_{2}$ in the cc-pVDZ basis. This end-to-end evaluation establishes that the principled simulator selection translates directly into expanded scientific reach and faster algorithmic prototyping.

</details>


### [67] [Vibe synthesis: hierarchical agentic decision-making for intent-driven multistep molecule synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18772)
*Jie Chen,Guikun Chen,Feng Wan,Ning Xu,Wen Liu,Yixiang Ruan,Yiyi Qiu,Haohua Li,Wei Wang,Yi Wang,Xiaoxue Wang,Peng Wang,Jiazhou Zhu,Jun Xuan,Chengxi Li,Jianhong Xu,Wenguan Wang,Yiming Mo*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出意图驱动的VibeSyn系统，用分层智能体将化学目标与实验证据转化为自适应合成决策，并在复杂多步合成中实现高度自主执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂分子合成需随证据累积不断修正多步决策，但现有AI工具和自动化平台多依赖预定义任务与硬编码流程，难以支持长周期、化学依赖的自适应合成。

Method: VibeSyn采用分层智能体架构，将化学家定义的分子目标与不断积累的实验证据转化为可执行决策，并通过计算工具和实验室自动化完成实验操作；在三个自主性递增的演示中评估，包括基于证据的实验决策、10步长春花碱全合成及含非天然氨基酸环肽的结构到化合物合成。

Result: 在长周期合成任务中，主动人工输入和简单确认分别仅占记录工作流动作与结果的不到7.5%和5.2%，系统能够自主导航不断演化的实验轨迹。

Conclusion: VibeSyn展示了化学家定义合成意图、智能体驱动实验室自主完成目标化合物合成的范式，指向高度自主的长周期多步合成未来。

Abstract: The ability to construct complex molecules underpins advances in medicine and the molecular sciences. Yet long-horizon multistep synthesis requires decisions to be revised as evidence accumulates across chemically dependent operations, a capability not readily supported by artificial-intelligence tools and automated platforms organized around predefined tasks and hard-coded workflows. Here we propose and demonstrate a vibe synthesis system (VibeSyn), an intent-driven architecture in which hierarchical agents translate chemist-defined molecular objectives and accumulating evidence into adaptive decisions executed through computational tools and laboratory automation. We evaluate VibeSyn in three demonstrations of increasing scope and autonomy, progressing from evidence-grounded experimental decisions to adaptive execution of a ten-step total synthesis of catharanthine and structure-to-compound synthesis of cyclic peptides containing unnatural amino acids (uAAs). Across the long-horizon campaigns, active human input and simple confirmation account for less than 7.5% and 5.2%, respectively, of recorded workflow actions and outcomes. VibeSyn points towards a future in which chemists define synthetic intent and agent-directed laboratories autonomously navigate the evolving experimental trajectory required to deliver the target compound.

</details>


### [68] [Adiabatic Electronic Momentum and the Continuity Equation with Nonlocal Effective Core Potentials in an Electronic Phase-Space Hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18950)
*Bismark Ninson,Zhen Tao*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用相空间电子哈密顿量方法研究有效核心势（ECP）的非局域分量对绝热电子动量和电流密度连续性方程的影响，并分析“移动ECP”对绝热电子输运的含义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Born-Oppenheimer分离下，绝热电子动量和电流密度描述核运动诱导的电子运动；但重原子计算常用ECP替代核心电子，其非局域分量会给电子动量和连续性方程带来额外贡献，需要明确其作用。

Method: 采用相空间电子哈密顿量方法，在小分子移动核体系中正确计入ECP非局域分量对相应电子算符的贡献，计算绝热电子动量与电子电流密度散度，并考察移动ECP（使非局域赝势随核运动以恢复伽利略协变性）的影响。

Result: 该方法在正确计入非局域贡献后可有效描述绝热电子动量和电流密度散度；非局域修正在ECP中心原子运动时重要，否则较小，体现电子响应与ECP的空间局域性。对于具有时间反演对称性的非简并体系，移动ECP在核速度二阶以内不改变时间反演偶可观测量在坐标空间的绝热演化，但可改变电子动量算符及其对核动力学的反馈。

Conclusion: ECP非局域效应在涉及ECP中心原子运动时对电子动量/电流描述不可忽略；移动ECP对可观测绝热输运至O(v^2)无修正，但会影响电子动量算符和核动力学反馈。

Abstract: Adiabatic electronic momentum and current density provide fundamental information about electronic motion induced by nuclear motion under the Born-Oppenheimer separation. In practical electronic structure calculations, core electrons in heavy atoms are commonly replaced by effective core potentials (ECPs), whose nonlocal components introduce additional contributions to electronic momentum and the continuity equation. Here, using a phase-space electronic Hamiltonian approach, we investigate these nonlocal components on adiabatic electronic momentum and the continuity equation in small molecules with moving nuclei. We demonstrate that this approach efficiently describes adiabatic electronic momentum and the divergence of electronic current density when the nonlocal contributions to the corresponding electronic operators are properly accounted for. We find that the nonlocal correction to electronic momentum is important when the ECP-centered atoms move but is small otherwise, reflecting the spatial locality of the electronic response and ECP. We further examine the implications of moving ECPs, which account for the motion of nonlocal pseudopotentials with the nuclei to restore Galilean covariance, for adiabatic electronic transport. We show that, for nondegenerate systems with time-reversal symmetry, moving ECPs do not modify the coordinate space adiabatic evolution of time-reversal even observables through second order in nuclear velocity, although they can alter the electronic momentum operator and its feedback on nuclear dynamics.

</details>


### [69] [Latent Molecular Rollout Towards Causal World Models: Direction is Learnable, Amplitude Control is Readout-Dependent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19501)
*Rui Wang*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在8B Qwen3上构建潜在分子rollout系统，用iBonD中9,202个水相实验pKa对训练，系统审计分子潜在接口的预测保真、方向/幅度可控性与解码结构迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子世界模型应预测结构变化如何改变物理性质，而非仅对静态分子做标签分配；作者希望评估潜在分子接口是否具备因果控制能力，并区分常被混淆的预测、方向学习和幅度校准等能力。

Method: 在8B Qwen3骨干上构建潜在分子rollout系统，使用iBonD的9,202个水相实验pKa对训练；去除文本捷径后，比较连续潜在推断与直接分子特征读出；用目标条件策略评估位移方向与幅度校准，并测试输入槽编辑、最终状态注入及生成微调对解码结构的控制迁移。

Result: 连续潜在推断测试MAE为2.087，显著优于直接特征读出（MAE差-0.276，95% CI [-0.352,-0.203]，p<0.001）；目标条件策略能可靠学习位移方向，中位余弦0.577，43个留出对中62.8%超过0.3阈值；幅度结论依赖读出方式：冻结线性读出相关性近零（-0.020），非线性梯度读出恢复相对幅度排序（相关性0.585，符号准确率0.744-0.767）但绝对尺度压缩；在80个测试源上，输入槽编辑或最终状态注入均未产生有意义的结构级变化；生成微调在内部oracle评估下有相对提升，但单样本设置未超过简单常数基线。

Conclusion: 固定规模潜在分子接口的能力边界被明确刻画：方向控制可稳健实现，但校准幅度控制和向解码结构的迁移仍是通向因果分子世界模型的开放挑战。

Abstract: A molecular world model should predict how structural changes alter physical properties, not merely assign labels to static molecules. We build a latent molecular rollout system on an 8B Qwen3 backbone, trained on 9,202 aqueous experimental pKa pairs from iBonD, and assess its progress toward causal control by auditing three often-conflated capabilities: predictive fidelity through the intended molecular pathway, directional learnability versus amplitude calibration of target-conditioned displacements, and transfer of latent control to decoded structures.
  After removing textual shortcuts, continuous latent inference achieves a test MAE of 2.087, significantly outperforming direct molecular feature readout (MAE difference -0.276, 95% CI [-0.352, -0.203], p<0.001). A target-conditioned policy reliably learns displacement directions, with a median cosine of 0.577 and 62.8% of 43 held-out pairs exceeding the 0.3 threshold.
  Amplitude conclusions are strictly readout-dependent. A frozen linear readout yields near-zero amplitude correlation (-0.020), while a nonlinear gradient-based readout recovers relative amplitude ordering (correlation 0.585, sign accuracy 0.744-0.767) with compressed absolute scale.
  At the decoded structure level, neither input-slot editing nor direct final-state injection produces meaningful changes across 80 test sources, bounding the tested control surfaces rather than indicating a universal decoder bottleneck. Generation fine-tuning shows relative improvement under internal oracle evaluation but does not surpass a simple constant baseline in single-sample settings.
  These results delineate the capability frontier of fixed-scale latent molecular interfaces: directional control is robustly achievable, but calibrated amplitude control and decoded structural transfer remain open challenges on the path to causal molecular world models.

</details>


### [70] [Affordable Analytic Gradients for G0W0-BSE Excited States for Multiple GW Variants via Back-Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19547)
*Qirui Gao,Igor Ying Zhang,Xin Xu*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该工作推导、实现并验证了CD-GW、AC-GW、CD-WAC-GW及基于其构建的BSE的解析核梯度，并将其用于激发态几何优化与非绝热分子动力学模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GW和BSE方法可高精度描述激发态，但缺乏高效解析核梯度限制了激发态结构优化和非绝热动力学应用；因此需要为不同GW自能变体及BSE开发可验证的解析梯度。

Method: 将全部梯度表示为与SCF计算中原始量导数相关的一组covector的收缩，并通过GW和BSE方程的反向传播过程推导这些covector；实现CD、AC、CD-WAC三种GW自能及BSE的核梯度，并用中心有限差分验证正确性。

Result: 有限差分确认所有梯度实现正确；使用AC-GW-BSE梯度优化了CO、供体-受体染料DA1和TICT模型DMABN的激发态几何，结果与实验或高水平计算一致；利用SHARC对DMABN进行TSH非绝热分子动力学模拟；计时和内存缩放测试表明GW与BSE梯度计算分别渐近标度为N^4和N^3。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了可验证且可扩展的GW/BSE解析核梯度方法，能够支持激发态几何优化和NAMD模拟；相关源码包括REST及支持REST后端的修改版SHARC已在Gitee公开。

Abstract: We derive, implement and validate analytic nuclear gradients for three variants of $GW$ self-energies---the contour-deformation (CD), the analytic continuation (AC), and the contour deformation with $W$-analytic-continuation (CD-WAC)---and for the Bethe--Salpeter equation (BSE) built on top of them. All gradients are expressed as contractions of a set of covectors connected to derivatives of primitive quantities in SCF calculations. The derivation of these covectors is a back-propagation procedure through the $GW$ and BSE equations. Central finite differences confirm the correctness of all implemented gradients. With the AC-$GW$-BSE gradient we optimize the excited-state geometries of carbon monoxide, a donor-acceptor dye labelled as DA1, and DMABN, a typical model system for twisted intramolecular charge-transfer phenomena (TICT), and have obtained results that successfully reproduce observations from experiments or high-level calculations. We also performed trajectory surface hopping (TSH) simulations of nonadiabatic molecular dynamics (NAMD) of DMABN using the SHARC NAMD package, showing the capacity of our method to simulate NAMD problems. We performed timing and memory scaling tests, demonstrating that the gradient calculations of both $GW$ and BSE scale asymptotically as $N^4$ for timing and $N^3$ for memory. All source codes used in the present work, including the REST program package and the modified SHARC NAMD package that supports REST as the quantum chemistry backend, are available on Gitee https://gitee.com/restgroup/rest and https://gitee.com/qirui-gao-2025/sharc

</details>


### [71] [Rovibrational state-specific quasi-classical trajectory calculation and master equation analysis of the CNO system in high-energy collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20147)
*Clement H. B. Civrais,Breno R. L. Galvao,Sung Min Jo,Marco Panesi*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该研究基于态分辨准经典轨迹与主方程分析，系统考察高超音速热条件下NO+C、CN+O和CO+N的CNO体系碰撞过程，揭示非平衡能量弛豫、解离与交换反应机制及QSS假设适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNO体系决定CN、CO、NO等辐射组分生成与消耗，对燃烧、天体物理和高超音速应用重要；需厘清各碰撞体系的状态特异性动力学及常用近似（如能量模式解耦和QSS）的可靠性。

Method: 采用振转态分辨的准经典轨迹计算，结合主方程分析，研究NO(X^2Π)+C(^3P)、CN(X^2Σ^+)+O(^3P)以及CO(X^1Σ^+)+N(^4S,^2D)体系在高超音速热条件下的非弹性、解离和交换过程。

Result: 非弹性过程主要为低能态转动激发和高能态振转激发；多数体系转动与振动弛豫时间相当且温度依赖异常，说明能量模式解耦假设不成立。解离由高振转态控制，更依赖态特异能量亏损而非振动特异动力学。T=10,000 K时，各体系72%–93%的双原子组分消耗处于QSS区；较低温度下大量消耗发生在QSS区外，表明QSS仅适用于中高温。交换反应不建立QSS，故QSS建模无效。NO+C低温主要生成CN+O、高温倾向CO+N并优先生成N(^4S)；CO+N通道比例低于现有激波管测量，但热交换速率系数可重现激波管测量和高精度计算。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了CNO体系非弹性、交换和解离过程之间的一致性相似性，为发展降阶模型提供了物理基础；同时表明能量模式解耦和QSS假设需谨慎使用，尤其交换反应及低温条件不宜采用QSS近似。

Abstract: The CNO system governs the formation and depletion of the CN, CO and NO radiative species relevant to combustion, astrophysical and hypersonic applications. The NO($X^2\Pi$) + C($^3P$), CN($X^2\Sigma^+$) + O($^3P$) and CO($X^1\Sigma^+$) + N($^4S$, $^2D$) collisional systems are investigated using rovibrational state-specific quasi-classical trajectory calculations and master equation analysis under hypersonic thermal conditions. Inelastic processes proceed primarily through rotational excitation of low-lying states and rovibrational excitation of high-lying states. For most systems, rotational and vibrational relaxation times exhibit comparable scales and unconventional temperature dependences, invalidating the assumption of decoupled energy modes. Dissociation is governed by high-lying rovibrational states and correlates strongly with the state-specific energy deficit rather than vibrational-specific dynamics. At $T$ = 10,000 K, 72 to 93% of the depletion of the diatomic species occurs within a quasi-steady-state (QSS) regime for all systems. At lower temperatures, a large portion of the depletion occurs outside the QSS regime, indicating that the QSS assumption holds only for moderate to high temperatures. No such regime is established for exchange reactions, demonstrating that the QSS assumption is invalid for modelling them. The NO + C system proceeds predominantly towards CN + O at low temperatures and towards the CO + N channels at high temperatures, preferentially forming N($^4S$). The fraction proceeding towards the CO + N channels remains lower than in available shock-tube measurements, while the thermal exchange rate coefficients reproduce shock-tube measurements and high-fidelity calculations. Finally, consistent similarities across inelastic, exchange and dissociation processes offer promising physical foundations for reduced-order model development.

</details>


### [72] [Oxirane Ring-Opening Dynamics Mediated By The First Singlet Excitation Using Time-Dependent \textit{GW} Molecular Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20723)
*Aaditya Manjanath,Ryoji Sahara,Thi Nu Pham,Yoshiyuki Kawazoe,Kaoru Ohno*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: TDGW-MD模拟表明，环氧乙烷经直接HOMO→LUMO（S1）激发即可发生不对称C–O键断裂开环；表面跳跃后，单重态路径经分子内氢转移生成乙醛，三重态路径经C–C键断裂生成甲醛和亚甲基。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前TDDFT研究认为S1激发非反应性、开环源于更高激发态，而约束DFT模拟则报道S2→S1跃迁后开环，存在争议；本文旨在从多体视角澄清环氧乙烷光致开环的初始激发与后续路径。

Method: 采用时间依赖GW分子动力学（TDGW-MD），基于扩展准粒子理论的多体方法模拟环氧乙烷光化学；在选定表面跳跃后，分别在破缺对称开壳层单重态和三重态势能面上进行条件轨迹模拟。

Result: 模拟发现直接HOMO→LUMO（S1）激发可引发不对称C–O键断裂开环；跳跃后单重态轨迹通过分子内氢转移生成乙醛，三重态轨迹通过C–C键断裂生成甲醛和亚甲基，产物与既往光解实验一致。

Conclusion: 在TDGW-MD框架下，最低单重激发态直接激发即可启动环氧乙烷开环，且后续自旋条件决定不同反应通道；研究展示了TDGW-MD从多体角度阐明光诱导反应机制的潜力。

Abstract: Oxirane (ethylene oxide, \ce{C2H4O}) is a prototypical system for investigating photoinduced bond cleavage and ring-opening dynamics. Previous time-dependent density functional theory studies identified the initial HOMO $\rightarrow$ LUMO (\ce{S1}) excitation as nonreactive and attributed ring opening to higher-energy excitations. Subsequent constrained density functional theory simulations, however, reported ring opening following an \ce{S2} $\rightarrow$ \ce{S1} transition. Here, we investigate photochemistry of oxirane using time-dependent $GW$ molecular dynamics (TD$GW$-MD), a many-body approach based on extended quasiparticle theory [\textit{J. Chem. Phys.} \textbf{160}, 184102 (2024)]. Our simulations reveal ring opening initiated by asymmetric \ce{C-O} bond cleavage following direct HOMO $\rightarrow$ LUMO (\ce{S1}) excitation. Following the selected surface hop, separate conditional trajectories on the broken-symmetry open-shell singlet and triplet potential energy surfaces lead to distinct products. The singlet trajectory yields acetaldehyde via intramolecular hydrogen transfer, while the triplet trajectory produces formaldehyde and methylene via \ce{C-C} bond fragmentation. These products are consistent with those identified in previous photolysis experiments. Our results demonstrate that direct excitation to the lowest singlet excited state can initiate ring opening within the present TD$GW$-MD framework. They further reveal distinct subsequent reaction pathways under the selected spin conditions. This study highlights the potential of TD$GW$-MD for elucidating photoinduced reaction mechanisms from a many-body perspective.

</details>


### [73] [Communication-Efficient Hamiltonian Evaluation for Massively Parallel Tensor-Product Bitstring Selected Configuration Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20808)
*Enhua Xu,Himadri Pathak,William Dawson,Takahito Nakajima*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出 FLIP 算法，用于 TBSCI 框架中的大规模哈密顿量评估，通过重排 CI 向量和激发链接数据消除 α 电子双激发的远程系数传输，并在 Fugaku 上实现超大规模 FCI 计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端规模 SCI 需要在超算上协同完成哈密顿量评估、分布式 CI 向量存储和进程间通信；已有 TBSCI 虽支持万亿行列式并行对角化，但通信开销仍限制强扩展与大规模应用。

Method: FLIP 重新组织 CI 向量布局与激发链接数据，将 α 电子双激发中原本需远程传输 CI 系数的操作改为本地系数访问，并从邻近 MPI rank 获取链接信息，从而减少通信等待。

Result: 在 Fugaku 的通信密集 FCI 基准中，32 次哈密顿量-向量乘法的最慢进程均无显式通信等待；N2/cc-pVTZ 的 2.62×10^12 个行列式在 18,000 至 81,000 节点（逾 380 万核）上达到 91.3% 强扩展效率；获得 N2/cc-pVTZ 的 FCI 参考势能曲线；六个键长下，按参考 SCI 波函数中集体权重选择的自旋比特串仅用少于 1% 的 FCI 行列式即给出与 FCI 相差 0.1 mEh 以内的变分 TBSCI 能量。

Conclusion: FLIP 有效提升了 TBSCI 的通信性能与强扩展性，并展示了张量积比特串表示的紧凑性；未来可通过局部构建双激发链接进一步降低通信量，并避免为大规模外部行列式空间的二阶微扰计算持久存储预计算双链接表。

Abstract: Extreme-scale selected configuration interaction (SCI) requires coordinated Hamiltonian evaluation, distributed CI-vector storage, and interprocess communication on massively parallel computers. Our previous work [J. Chem. Phys. 164, 194103 (2026)] introduced the tensor-product bitstring selected configuration interaction (TBSCI) framework, combining on-the-fly Hamiltonian evaluation with fully distributed CI-vector storage and enabling parallel diagonalization of trillion-determinant spaces on up to 54,000 Fugaku nodes. Here, we introduce FLIP, a new Hamiltonian evaluation algorithm within TBSCI that reorganizes the CI-vector layout and excitation-link data to replace remote CI-coefficient transfers for $\alpha$-electron double excitations with local coefficient access and link retrieval from nearby MPI ranks. In communication-intensive full configuration interaction (FCI) benchmarks on Fugaku, no exposed communication waiting time was recorded on the slowest process across all 32 measured Hamiltonian-vector multiplications. For N$_2$/cc-pVTZ with $2.62\times10^{12}$ determinants, the multiplication achieved 91.3% strong-scaling efficiency from 18,000 to 81,000 nodes (more than 3.8 million compute cores). We also obtained an FCI reference potential-energy curve for N$_2$/cc-pVTZ. At all six bond lengths, spin bitstrings selected by their collective weights in reference SCI wavefunctions yielded variational TBSCI energies within 0.1 m$E_h$ of FCI using fewer than 1% of the FCI determinants, demonstrating the compactness of the tensor-product bitstring representation. Finally, we discuss local construction of double-excitation links to substantially reduce communication volume and eliminate persistent storage of precomputed double-link tables for future second-order perturbation theory calculations with large external determinant spaces.

</details>


### [74] [MolReps: Taking SELFIES, Making SMILES, Teaching LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20848)
*Ken Deng,Garrett Morris*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出MolReps，用10种分子表示构建9,600个问答对，对文本和多模态LLM进行后训练，以提升分子表示理解与转换能力，并评估其在分子性质预测基准上的迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一分子可写成多种语法不同但等价的表示，LLM是否能识别并关联这些等价编码尚不明确，且分子表示理解对下游分子任务具有潜在价值。

Method: 构建MolReps流水线，覆盖10种分子表示，生成9,600个问答对，任务包括表示转换、归一化和2D结构识别；对MolQwen3-4B-Instruct和MolQwen3-VL-4B-Instruct进行后训练，并在分子重叠/分子不重叠测试集及MolRepBench上评估。

Result: 在分子重叠测试集上，InChIKey匹配率分别从4.01%提升至75.13%、从7.11%提升至73.14%；在分子不重叠测试集上，有效性分别从20.35%提升至58.12%、从17.19%提升至59.34%，但InChIKey匹配率仍低且分别从3.86%降至3.09%、从6.44%降至2.29%。在MolRepBench上，后训练模型相对基座模型平均相对提升27.84%。

Conclusion: 针对性后训练能显著增强LLM对分子表示的理解与转换能力，并改善下游分子性质预测表现；但对分子不重叠样本的精确分子身份匹配泛化仍有限，说明表示理解提升不等于跨分子精确识别。

Abstract: The same molecule can be expressed in multiple, syntactically distinct representations, yet it remains unclear whether large language models (LLMs) can recognize and relate these equivalent encodings. We introduce MolReps, a pipeline that generates 9,600 question-answer pairs across ten representations for conversion, normalization, and 2D structure recognition. Post-training on MolReps improves text-only and multimodal models, MolQwen3-4B-Instruct (MQ3-4B-I) and MolQwen3-VL-4B-Instruct (MQ3-VL-4B-I), from $4.01\%$ to $75.13\%$ and from $7.11\%$ to $73.14\%$ (InChIKey match) on the molecule-overlap test set, respectively. On the molecule-disjoint test set, post-training increased validity from $20.35\%$ to $58.12\%$ for MQ3-4B-I and from $17.19\%$ to $59.34\%$ for MQ3-VL-4B-I. However, InChIKey match remained low, decreasing from $3.86\%$ to $3.09\%$ and from $6.44\%$ to $2.29\%$, respectively. Furthermore, the resulting models achieve a relative $27.84\%$ improvement in average over their base model on MolRepBench, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating molecular property prediction. These results show the effectiveness of targeted post-training for enhancing molecular representation understanding and its potential to improve downstream molecular tasks.

</details>


### [75] [An Alternative Explicitly Correlated Approach for Describing Strongly Correlated Systems within a Single-Determinant Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21170)
*Stanislav Ked\v{z}uch,Shota Tsuru,Takahito Nakajima,Jozef Noga*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种在单行列式框架内结合单态配对CCSD与显式相关理论的单参考方法，用于强关联闭壳层分子，兼顾强、弱电子相关并避免势能面非物理伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联体系通常需要多参考方法，但希望发展单参考替代方案；同时需平衡强电子相关与弱电子相关，并改善基组收敛与势能面稳定性。

Method: 将单态配对CCSD（CCSD0）与F12显式相关理论结合：单态相互作用由标准显式相关项描述；正则化中遗漏的三重态相关通过新构造的显式相关赝激发算符恢复，该算符满足Kato尖点条件并利用完整虚空间按电子间距离恢复三重态配对相关。基于CCSD0与复分子轨道理论的结构对应，提出CCSD0-F12Pi及微扰三体方法。

Result: 在N2和CO2键断裂过程以及线性单态亚甲基和环丁二烯强关联区域进行评测，并与Mukherjee多参考耦合簇方法基准比较；结果表明新框架系统性地避免势能面非物理伪影，同时加速向完备基组极限收敛。

Conclusion: 该工作为闭壳层分子提供了一种可替代多参考方法的单参考框架，在强关联区域保持稳定性并改善显式相关收敛行为。

Abstract: We introduce an alternative explicitly correlated single-reference approach for strongly correlated systems within a single-determinant framework, providing a balanced description of strong and weak electron correlations by integrating singlet-paired coupled-cluster singles and doubles with explicitly correlated theory. While the singlet interactions are captured via standard explicitly correlated terms, the triplet correlation omitted during regularization is recovered through a newly formulated explicitly correlated pseudo-excitation operator that satisfies Kato's cusp condition and integrates a full virtual space to retrieve the missing triplet-paired correlations based on the interelectronic distance. We show that CCSD0 [J. Chem. Theory Comput. 11, 3171 (2015)] is structurally related to the complex molecular orbital framework, an orbital theory that addresses the stability dilemma by yielding an energy lower than that of restricted Hartree-Fock in the presence of imaginary spin-singlet instabilities, while the symmetry dilemma is resolved via projection [J. Chem. Phys. 69, 4535 (1978)]. Prompted by this structural correspondence, we introduce the explicitly correlated "CCSD0-F12Pi" and perturbative triples methodologies. The performance of these new single-reference frameworks has been evaluated for the bond-breaking processes of N2 and CO2, and benchmarked against Mukherjee's multireference coupled-cluster method for the strongly correlated regimes of linear singlet methylene and cyclobutadiene. Our results show that the new framework systematically avoids unphysical artifacts along the potential energy surfaces while accelerating convergence toward the complete basis set limit, offering a single-reference alternative to multireference methodologies for closed-shell molecules.

</details>


### [76] [Rotation-Invariant Machine Learning for Highly Symmetric Molecular Configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21308)
*Logan Bolton,Tomas Karella,Nicholas Lubbers,Nathaniel Philip Gorski,Emily Shinkle,Alice Elisabeth Anastasia Allen,Pieter J Swart,Roxana Bujack*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出轻量级n重对称构型基准以评估原子环境表示的表达力，发现不变与等变架构存在退化，并为HIP-HOP构建新不变基，在甲烷模拟中实现更低误差和3.2倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子机器学习可低成本近似量子计算，但精度强烈依赖三维原子环境表示；不变表示计算快，却在高对称环境中难以区分几何上不同的构型，因此需要评估并改进其表达力。

Method: 构建针对n重对称构型的轻量基准，暴露不变和等变架构中的表示退化；据此为HIP-HOP设计新的不变基，并在甲烷模拟数据集上评估。

Result: 扩展基在HIP-HOP各变体中误差最低，精度与EquiformerV3相近，同时推理速度快3.2倍。

Conclusion: 精心构造的不变表示能够缓解关键表达力限制，并在精度与计算效率之间取得更好平衡。

Abstract: Atomistic machine learning can approximate expensive quantum mechanical calculations at a fraction of their computational cost, but accuracy depends strongly on how the three-dimensional atomic environments are represented. Invariant representations enable fast computation but can fail to distinguish geometrically distinct configurations, especially in highly symmetric environments. We introduce a lightweight benchmark to evaluate expressivity on $n$-fold symmetric configurations, and use it to expose degeneracies in both invariant and equivariant architectures. Guided by these results, we construct a new invariant basis for HIP-HOP which resolves most of the identified failures. On a methane simulation dataset, the extended basis achieves the lowest error among the HIP-HOP variants and has similar accuracy to EquiformerV3 while running $3.2\times$ faster at inference. These results show that carefully constructed invariant representations can mitigate key expressivity limitations while balancing accuracy and computational efficiency.

</details>

### [77] [Random matrix discrepancy -- CLuP algorithm and parametric RDT approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18650)
*Mihailo Stojnic*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究比例渐近 regime 下随机矩阵 discrepancy 的信息论与算法性质：对 iid GOE 矩阵集合，在单位立方体顶点上最小化线性组合的 L2 范数 κ，并建立 RDT 下界、负 Hopfield 代理模型和 CLuP 算法来分析信息论极限与计算 gap。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是刻画随机矩阵 discrepancy 问题中 κ 的信息论最优值、算法可达性能以及二者之间是否存在计算 gap，并与对称二值感知机的向量类比现象进行对照。

Method: 作者提出 Random duality theory（RDT）框架及其 lifted 变体来推导 κ 的下界；引入负 Hopfield 模型作为 fully lifted RDT 的代理，在第 2、3、4 lifting level 上分析性能与计算 gap；同时设计 Controlled Loosening Up（CLuP）算法求解矩阵 discrepancy。

Result: RDT 下界与一阶矩方法给出的界精确匹配；负 Hopfield 代理在第 2 lifting level 上位于信息论下界与算法上界之间；第 3、4 lifting level 的 parametric RDT 预测低 κ 区域存在显著计算 gap，代理模型结果外推到矩阵 discrepancy 后与已有工作一致；CLuP 在低 α 区间接近信息论预测，在高 α 区间符合代理模型的算法预测。

Conclusion: 矩阵 discrepancy 的信息论与算法预测在整个 α∈(0,∞) 范围内与对称二值感知机的向量类比现象一致；结果表明计算 gap 随 α 变化，RDT、代理模型与 CLuP 为分析随机矩阵 discrepancy 提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We study information-theoretic and algorithmic aspects of random matrix discrepancy in proportional regime. The problem amounts to finding $\kappa=\min_{\x\in\mB^n}\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bar{A}_i\x_i\|_2$, where $\bar{A}_i\in\mR^{d\times d}$ is a collection of $n$ iid GOE matrices, $\mB^n$ are vertices of the unit cube in $\mR^n$, and $\alpha=\lim_{n\rightarrow \infty}\frac{n}{d^2}$ is a proportionality constant.
  Strong progress is made in several directions: (i) We develop a Random duality theory (RDT) framework and utilize its a lifted variant to establish lower bounds on $\kappa$, which precisely match the bounds obtained via the first moment in [81]. (ii) To address analytical challenges, we propose a negative Hopfield model as a proxy for implementation of a fully lifted RDT. On the 2nd lifting level, this model fits remarkably well between the information-theoretic lower bounds from [81] and the algorithmic upper bounds from [38]. (iii) On the 3rd and 4th lifting levels, parametric RDT concepts predict a sizeable computational gap for lower $\kappa$ values in the proxy model. Extending proxy information-theoretic fitting to algorithmic suggests a similar computational gap for matrix discrepancy in low $\kappa$ regime, aligning with [73]. (iv) We design a Controlled Loosening Up (CLuP) algorithm for matrix discrepancy. Its performance strongly matches our analytical results -- closely approaching information-theoretic predictions in lower $\alpha$ ranges (indicating a small or nonexistent computational gap) and following the proxy model's algorithmic predictions in higher $\alpha$ ranges (indicating a sizeable computational gap).
  Both the information-theoretic and algorithmic predictions match the corresponding phenomenologies of the symmetric binary perceptron (SBP) vector analogue in the entire $\alpha\in(0,\infty)$ range [1,2,14,31,51,92,111].

</details>


### [78] [Exact mobility edges in high-dimensional quasiperiodic networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19152)
*Jia-Hui Zhang,Shan-Zhong Li,Zhi Li*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文将一维隐藏自对偶思想推广到高维准周期网络，通过消去周期性子格点映射为有效高维广义Aubry-André模型，从而恢复隐藏自对偶并获得精确迁移率边。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受一维隐藏自对偶机制启发，作者希望为高维准周期网络建立统一理论框架，解决复杂高维模型中精确迁移率边难以构造的问题。

Method: 在周期网络作为键装饰连接、准周期势空间可分离的条件下，积分掉周期性子格点，将系统映射为有效高维广义Aubry-André方程；并以二维和三维模型为例进行数值验证。

Result: 该映射恢复了隐藏自对偶对称性，给出精确迁移率边；二维和三维模型的数值结果与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 研究建立了将高维准周期网络映射为高维Aubry-André模型的范式，为高维系统中精确迁移率边的构造与探索提供了系统路径。

Abstract: Inspired by the one-dimensional hidden self-duality [Phys. Rev. Lett. 134, 246301 (2025)], we establish a generalized theoretical framework for a specific class of high-dimensional quasiperiodic network models. Specifically, as long as the periodic network acts as bond-decorating links and the quasiperiodic potentials are spatially separable, the system can be mapped onto an effective high-dimensional generalized Aubry-Andr\'e equation by integrating out the periodic sites. This mapping restores the hidden self-duality symmetry in these complex models, thereby yielding exact mobility edges. Taking two-dimensional and three-dimensional models as examples, we demonstrate the consistency between the theoretical prediction and the numerical results. This paper establishes a clear paradigm for mapping high-dimensional quasiperiodic networks to high-dimensional Aubry-Andr\'e models, providing a systematic pathway for the construction and exploration of the exact mobility edge in high-dimensional systems.

</details>


### [79] [Velocity weakening and stick-slip in mean-field depinning with stress overshoots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19863)
*Adrien Rezzouk,Alberto Rosso*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文通过在弹簧驱动下引入瞬态应力过冲的全连接退钉扎模型，建立了力-速度特性、黏滑运动与准静态雪崩统计之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在厘清速度弱化、黏滑失稳与失控雪崩之间的关系，并理解弹性加载如何动态选择重入型力-速度关系中的分支。

Method: 构建强度为 M、弹簧刚度为 k0 的全连接退钉扎模型，结合精确稳态关系、线性稳定性分析、直接数值模拟和群体动力学进行分析。

Result: 当 M>1 时出现两个刚度尺度：k_w=M-1 处稳态特性首次出现速度弱化分支，k_q=(M-1)^2/(4M) 处准静态极限下出现有限幅度黏滑。二者之间形成中间区：准静态力收敛到稳态值，而有限速度驱动在临界速度以下产生黏滑；该区雪崩发生瞬态放大并出现有限的大尺寸非对称“ghost kings”。在 k_q 及以下，同样放大导致贯穿系统的“genuine kings”和有限准静态力循环。

Conclusion: 弹性加载会动态选择重入型力-速度关系的分支，从而统一解释速度弱化、黏滑与失控雪崩之间的联系。

Abstract: We study a fully connected depinning model with transient stress overshoots of strength $M$, driven through a spring of stiffness $k_0$. Combining exact stationary relations, linear stability analysis, direct simulations, and population dynamics, we connect the force--velocity characteristic to stick-slip motion and quasistatic avalanche statistics. For $M>1$, two distinct stiffness scales organize the response. At $k_0=k_{\rm w}\equiv M-1$, the stationary characteristic first develops a velocity-weakening branch, whereas $k_0=k_{\rm q}\equiv(M-1)^2/(4M)$ marks the onset of finite-amplitude stick-slip in the quasistatic limit. Their separation produces an intermediate regime in which the quasistatic force converges to a stationary value, whereas finite-velocity driving produces stick-slip below a critical velocity. Quasistatic avalanches in this regime undergo transient amplification and display an excess of large, asymmetric events---"ghost kings"---that remain finite. By contrast, at and below $k_{\rm q}$ the same amplification produces system-spanning "genuine kings" and a finite quasistatic force cycle. These results show how elastic loading dynamically selects among the branches of a reentrant force--velocity relation and clarify the connection between velocity weakening, stick-slip, and runaway avalanches.

</details>


### [80] [Localization transition in a spin chain with an exact Fock-space tree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21445)
*Bernhard Jobst,Simone Warzel,Alexander Altland,Frank Pollmann*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文引入一个无序自旋-1链模型，使单个畴壁在精确二叉树上运动，从而在无环、可处理的构型空间中研究具有强关联无序的多体局域化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体局域化常被视为多体构型图上的Anderson局域化，但强关联无序和密集环路使该图难以分析，因此需要一个既保留关联无序又可控的模型。

Method: 构造局域无序自旋-1链，其中单个畴壁探索精确二叉树构型；随机场在树上产生类似一般相互作用系统的强关联无序，且无环结构使问题可处理；结合强无序下的严格界和指数大树上的高效数值计算。

Result: 在强无序下严格限制了畴壁的扩散；在指数规模树上的数值结果表明，在有限无序强度处存在局域化转变。

Conclusion: 该无环二叉树模型为研究具有强关联无序的多体局域化提供了可控平台，并支持有限无序强度下的局域化转变。

Abstract: Many-body localization is often pictured as Anderson localization on the graph of many-body configurations. Strongly correlated disorder and a dense net of loops, however, make this graph hard to analyze. We introduce a local disordered spin-1 chain in which a single domain wall explores an exact binary tree of configurations. Random fields create strongly correlated disorder on this tree, as in generic interacting systems, while the absence of loops keeps the problem tractable. We rigorously bound the spreading of the domain wall at strong disorder, and efficient numerics on exponentially large trees indicate a localization transition at finite disorder strength.

</details>


### [81] [Connectivity from activity in spiking neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21592)
*Albert J. Wakhloo,Sebastian Mizera*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文提出一个统一的循环神经网络建模框架，将突触连接、单细胞响应与神经相关性用简单方程联系起来，并证明可由神经活动的高频相关性恢复网络连接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经科学中常需从可测的神经活动推断不可直接观测的突触连接；作者希望建立连接、单细胞响应和神经相关性之间的定量关系。

Method: 考虑处于平稳异步状态的网络，涵盖广泛的突触滤波器、脉冲非线性以及随机加结构化连接；推导出的方程按单突触、双突触和更高阶通路分解，并用实验可测的活动统计量（尤其是高频相关性）表示两个神经元间的有效单突触连接。

Result: 得到一套适用于多种循环神经网络模型的连接—响应—相关性方程，并给出按网络通路分解的表达式；该表达式表明有效单突触连接可由神经活动统计量刻画。

Conclusion: 神经活动的高频相关性包含连接信息，因此有可能从神经活动恢复网络连接。

Abstract: We show that a wide range of recurrent neural network models obey a simple set of equations tying synaptic connectivity and single-cell response properties to neural correlations. Our modeling framework considers networks in a stationary, asynchronous state and contains a broad class of synaptic filters, spiking nonlinearities, and random-plus-structured connectivity. The resulting equations naturally decompose into contributions from monosynaptic, disynaptic, and higher order paths through the network. We leverage this decomposition to express the effective monosynaptic connection between two neurons in terms of experimentally measurable statistics of the activity, and in particular, their high-frequency correlations. In this way, we show that it is possible to recover network connectivity from neural activity.

</details>

### [82] [Double quantum dots as dynamical impurities in one-dimensional transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21609)
*Yang-Zhi Chou*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究紧束缚链中单电子与电容耦合双量子点（可调谐两能级系统杂质）的散射，揭示Feshbach型完美反射及多杂质下由能级分裂调控的非单调透射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子点阵列中的动态两能级杂质会通过弹性、非弹性和虚跃迁过程影响电子输运；需要理解这些过程如何由栅压调节的能级分裂控制，并判断其可观测效应。

Method: 采用紧束缚链模型，将双量子点作为电容耦合的动态两能级系统杂质；对单个杂质精确求解单电子散射问题，并分析多个杂质在强反射链中的散射与干涉行为。

Result: 单杂质时出现Feshbach型完美弹性反射，共振条件可区分杂质初始两能级态；多杂质时透射在中等平均能级分裂处出现峰值。小分裂下与杂质交换能量会削弱背散射路径的相长干涉，导致透射初始上升；激发通道关闭后虚跃迁增强反射，使透射低于纯弹性值T0；大分裂下虚过程减弱，透射趋近T0。

Conclusion: 栅压控制的量子点阵列透射测量可检验这些预测，并为理解动态两能级杂质引起的散射、干涉与虚过程提供途径。

Abstract: We study single-electron scattering in a tight-binding chain capacitively coupled to double quantum dots, which act as dynamical two-level-system impurities with electrically tunable level splittings. For a single impurity, we solve the scattering problem exactly and show Feshbach-like perfect elastic reflection, whose resonant condition distinguishes the initial two-level state. For several impurities in a strongly reflecting chain, transmission peaks at an intermediate mean level splitting. At small splittings, energy exchange with the impurities can weaken constructive interference between backscattered paths, consistent with the initial rise in transmission. As excitation channels close, virtual transitions can enhance reflection and drive transmission below its purely elastic value $T_0$. At larger splittings, these virtual processes weaken and transmission approaches $T_0$. We discuss how gate-controlled transmission measurements in quantum-dot arrays could test these predictions.

</details>


### [83] [Magnon binding in a quantum Hall ferromagnet with a periodic electric field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18779)
*Manas Kaushik Das*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究了填充因子为1的量子霍尔铁磁体中，自旋波如何被周期静电势限制为一维通道，并由此调控同向传播磁振子之间的交换束缚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋波虽为中性，但具有垂直于动量的电偶极矩，可被周期静电势并行限制；作者关注这种准一维限制如何改变磁振子的交换吸引与光谱特征。

Method: 基于自旋波电偶极与周期静电势耦合形成平行通道的机制，计算横向波函数重叠对交换束缚的影响，并分析孤立谐波通道、耦合阵列及运动势中的波包行为。

Result: 通道越窄，横向波函数重叠越大，有效吸引越强；在孤立谐波通道中，从N个磁振子簇中移走一个所需能量领头阶为π(k_*ℓ)^4N(N-1)/4乘横向能级间距。预测了在已有磁振子基础上再加入一个磁振子的谱线频率与动量依赖；宽阵列中的隧穿可使束缚能指数小，而窄于横向对尺寸的条带在弱吸引下恢复一维束缚；运动势在群速度匹配时可携带单波包且扩散小于自由传播。

Conclusion: 空间限制是调控量子霍尔铁磁体中磁振子交换束缚和光谱的有力手段，并可在阵列与运动势中产生可观测的束缚、隧穿和波包输运效应。

Abstract: A spin wave in a filling-one quantum Hall ferromagnet is neutral but carries an electric dipole perpendicular to its momentum. A spatially periodic electrostatic potential therefore creates parallel channels for spin waves. We use this established mechanism to calculate how confinement changes the exchange binding of magnons travelling in the same direction. Narrower channels increase the overlap of their transverse wavefunctions and strengthen the effective attraction. In an isolated harmonic channel, the energy required to remove one magnon from a cluster of $N$ is $\pi(k_*\ell)^4N(N-1)/4$ times the transverse level spacing to leading order, where $k_*$ is the mean wave number along the channel and $\ell$ is the magnetic length. We predict the frequency and momentum dependence of the spectral line for adding a second magnon to one already present, both in an isolated channel and in a coupled array. Tunnelling in a wide array can make the binding energy exponentially small, while a strip narrower than the transverse pair size recovers one-dimensional binding at sufficiently weak attraction. We also find that a moving potential can carry a single wave packet with less spreading than free propagation when its initial group velocity matches the potential's speed.

</details>


### [84] [Nonlinear spin generation and spin photocurrent as a symmetry and magnetic domain probe in unconventional magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19012)
*Ying Xiong,Er-Ping Li,L. K. Ang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 非常规磁体无需自旋轨道耦合即可产生动量依赖的自旋劈裂；结合非线性自旋极化与自旋光电流，可诊断其自旋空间群对称性、磁畴和量子几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有平衡态自旋织构信息有限，需要超越平衡态的手段来探测非常规磁体的自旋空间群对称性、磁畴结构及量子几何。

Method: 建立机制分辨的二阶自旋光响应张量联合对称性分析，研究其频率依赖、磁宇称和弛豫标度，并对典型杂化宇称与奇宇称磁体模型进行数值演示。

Result: 表明非常规磁体是可由非偏振光诱导大磁宇称偶自旋光电流的天然平台，有望作为无偏振器自旋源；光学指纹将自旋空间对称性与自旋积累和输运的不同微观路径联系起来。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了用非线性自旋极化和自旋光电流联合诊断非常规磁体自旋空间群、磁畴与量子几何的光学分析框架。

Abstract: Unconventional magnets host momentum-dependent spin splitting without spin-orbit coupling. We demonstrate that the combination of nonlinear spin polarisation and spin photocurrent is a powerful diagnostic tool for probing the spin space group symmetry, magnetic domain, and quantum geometry in unconventional magnets, providing information beyond their equilibrium spin texture. We develop a mechanism-resolved joint symmetry analysis of second-order spin photoresponse tensors and analyse their frequency dependence, magnetic parity and relaxation scaling. We show that unconventional magnets are natural platforms to achieve large magnetic-even spin photocurrent induced by unpolarised light, making them promising candidates for polariser-free spin sources. We present numerical illustrations for prototypical hybrid parity and odd parity magnet models. Our work provides optical fingerprints connecting spin-space symmetry to distinct microscopic pathways of spin accumulation and transport.

</details>


### [85] [Chiral-phonon-mediated spin current injection into an Ising superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19180)
*Jiayan Zhang,Gaoyang Li,Yanxia Xing*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文理论提出并研究手性绝缘体/Ising超导体异质结中，由手性声子介导的自旋流注入机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索手性声子作为角动量载体向Ising超导体注入自旋流的可行性，并理解超导态及面内磁场对注入自旋流的调控。

Method: 采用非平衡格林函数形式，考虑温度梯度驱动非平衡手性声子，通过界面声子-自旋转换将声子角动量转移给电子自旋，推导注入自旋流表达式。

Result: 注入自旋流取决于两种相反圆偏振声子支贡献之差；在超导态中，自旋流密度随温度或面内磁场增强而增大；除超导转变处的拐点外，转变温度以下出现第二个拐点，其位置与声子诱导双准粒子激发开启一致，并在温度-磁场相图中追踪该拐点温度随磁场的变化。

Conclusion: 手性声子介导的自旋流注入可由温度和面内磁场调控，第二个拐点可作为声子诱导双准粒子激发开启的特征，为Ising超导体中自旋输运的探测与调控提供理论途径。

Abstract: We theoretically propose chiral-phonon-mediated spin current injection into an Ising superconductor in a chiral-insulator/Ising-superconductor heterostructure, where nonequilibrium phonons driven by a temperature gradient transfer their angular momentum to electron spins via interfacial phonon-spin conversion. Using the nonequilibrium Green's function formalism, we derive an expression for the injected spin current and show that it depends on the difference between the contributions from the two phonon branches with opposite circular polarizations. We further investigate the temperature dependence of the injected spin current density at different in-plane magnetic field strengths. In the superconducting state, the spin current density increases with temperature or in-plane magnetic field strength. In addition to the kink at the superconducting transition, a second kink appears below the transition temperature. The position of this kink is consistent with the onset of phonon-induced two-quasiparticle excitations as the superconducting gap decreases with increasing temperature. We further investigate the magnetic-field dependence of the kink temperature in a temperature--magnetic-field phase diagram.

</details>


### [86] [Thermal-energy relaxation in chiral quantum Hall edge mode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19259)
*Edvin G. Idrisov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究量子霍尔边缘电荷分数化区域中真实欧姆接触产生的非平衡态，并通过高电容、完美耦合的虚拟欧姆接触考察其热能量弛豫。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验常见假设：接触态是否总能仅由有效接触温度描述，并理解热欧姆接触下强热化是否足以使边缘态完全平衡。

Method: 理论分析真实欧姆接触（ROC）在电荷分数化区间产生的非平衡电子分布与电流噪声，并引入大电容、完美耦合的虚拟欧姆接触（FOC）研究沿量子霍尔边缘的热能量弛豫。

Result: 冷欧姆接触下，电子分布函数和电流噪声完全由平衡形式及费米-狄拉克分布描述；热欧姆接触下，即使热化很强，出射态也未完全平衡，分布函数和噪声仍保留初始非平衡态特征。

Conclusion: 热欧姆接触产生的边缘态不能仅用有效接触温度刻画，必须考虑完整的非平衡分布函数和噪声特征，这与常见假设相反。

Abstract: We study the nonequilibrium state generated by a real Ohmic contact (ROC) in the charge-fractionalization regime. We then investigate its thermal-energy relaxation along a quantum Hall edge mediated by a fictitious Ohmic contact (FOC) with large capacitance and perfect coupling to the edge. For cold Ohmic contacts, the electron distribution function and current noise are fully described by their equilibrium forms and the corresponding Fermi-Dirac distribution. In contrast, in the case of hot Ohmic contacts, strong thermalization effects do not drive the outgoing state into complete equilibrium. Both the distribution function and the noise retain signatures of the initial nonequilibrium state and therefore cannot be characterized solely by the effective contact temperature, contrary to common assumptions.

</details>


### [87] [Universal Analytical Description of Magnetoexcitons in Two-Dimensional Semiconducting Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19415)
*Dang-Khoa D. Le,Dai-Nam Le,Ngoc-Loan Phan,Van-Hoang Le*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出单层激子 s 态的普适零场谱公式，并用共享参数统一描述光学与磁光性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要在 Rytova–Keldysh 模型中统一 Coulomb 与 logarithmic 极限，准确刻画筛查依赖的激子谱及磁光响应。

Method: 通过匹配 Rytova–Keldysh 模型的 Coulomb 与 logarithmic 极限，构造 E_n≈-Ry*/[n-1/2+f_n(ξ)]^2 的通用 s 态零场谱；由解析激子半径确定抗磁系数和磁 crossover 尺度。

Result: 在 0≤ξ≤100 全筛查范围内零场偏差峰值约 1%；对采样的 hBN 封装单层，磁激子能量在至 100 T 时与数值谱达到 sub-meV rms 一致，并重现测量的轨道位移。

Conclusion: 共享参数可连接光学与磁光谱，为单层激子磁光性质提供准确且统一的描述。

Abstract: Matching the Coulomb and logarithmic limits of the Rytova--Keldysh model gives a universal zero-field spectrum for monolayer exciton $s$ states, $E_n\simeq-\mathrm{Ry}^*/[n-1/2+f_n(\xi)]^2$, where $\xi$ is the screening parameter. Analytical radii determine diamagnetic coefficients and the magnetic crossover scale. Zero-field deviations peak near $1\%$ across the full screening range $0\leq\xi\leq100$. For the sampled hBN-encapsulated monolayers, magnetoexciton energies agree with numerical spectra to sub-meV rms up to $100\T$ and capture the measured orbital shifts. Shared parameters link optical and magneto-optical spectra.

</details>


### [88] [Ultrastrong coupling between magnetoplasmons and higher cyclotron-resonance harmonics induced by spatially inhomogeneous electromagnetic fields in two-dimensional electron systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19425)
*Kazuyuki Kuroyama,Shimpei Nakamura,Motoaki Bamba*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文理论研究垂直磁场下二维电子系统在空间非均匀电磁场中的非局域磁等离子体响应，并建立非局域磁电导、Bernstein模物理与有效集体模耦合之间的定量联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限波矢电磁场可探测二维电子系统中的非局域集体动力学，但非局域磁电导如何决定磁等离子体与高阶回旋共振谐波的杂化及耦合强度仍需定量刻画。

Method: 结合Maxwell方程与Kubo公式，推导非局域磁电导张量，获得集体模的自洽色散关系；并将Maxwell-Kubo色散关系映射为Hopfield型哈密顿量，解析给出磁等离子体与第n阶回旋共振谐波之间的有效耦合强度。

Result: 有限面内波矢允许高阶回旋跃迁，使回旋共振高次谐波参与非局域电导；这些谐波与磁等离子体模杂化，在集体模谱中产生Bernstein模特征的避免交叉。归一化耦合强度随面内波长非单调变化并达到最大值，且在高密度区该最大值几乎与电子密度无关。

Conclusion: 研究建立了非局域磁电导、Bernstein模物理与有效集体模耦合之间的定量联系，为太赫兹磁等离子体系统中实现超强耦合提供设计准则。

Abstract: Finite-wave-vector electromagnetic fields provide a route to accessing nonlocal collective dynamics in two-dimensional electron systems. Here, we theoretically investigate the magnetoplasmon response of a two-dimensional electron system subjected to a spatially nonuniform electromagnetic field in a perpendicular magnetic field. By combining Maxwell's equations with the Kubo formula, we derive the nonlocal magnetoconductivity tensor and obtain the self-consistent dispersion relation for collective modes. We show that a finite in-plane wave vector enables higher-order cyclotron transitions, allowing higher harmonics of the cyclotron resonance to contribute to the nonlocal conductivity. These harmonics hybridize with the magnetoplasmon mode, producing avoided crossings in the collective-mode spectrum characteristic of Bernstein modes. To quantify this hybridization, we establish a correspondence between the Maxwell-Kubo dispersion relation and a Hopfield-type Hamiltonian and derive an analytical expression for the effective coupling strength between the magnetoplasmon and the n-th cyclotron-resonance harmonic. Our analysis reveals that the normalized coupling strength exhibits a nonmonotonic dependence on the in-plane wavelength, reaching a maximum that becomes nearly independent of electron density in the high-density regime. These results establish a quantitative connection between nonlocal magnetoconductivity, Bernstein-mode physics, and effective collective-mode coupling, providing design guidelines for realizing ultrastrong coupling in terahertz magnetoplasmonic systems.

</details>


### [89] [Current-driven chiral RKKY interaction in altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19574)
*Anirban Kundu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在无自旋轨道耦合的自旋劈裂金属中，电荷电流可通过漂移费米海诱导局域自旋间的Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya（DM）相互作用；本文以二维d波交错磁体中的RKKY耦合为例给出解析结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DM相互作用通常需要自旋轨道耦合和反演对称性破缺。作者旨在证明仅靠自旋劈裂金属中的电荷电流也能产生DM型交换耦合，并揭示交错磁体中该效应的对称性特征。

Method: 使用Keldysh格林函数并引入漂移费米海，计算二维d波交错磁体中两个磁性杂质间的RKKY相互作用，体系还包含交换劈裂；在电流线性阶提取费米面修正，并对任意两个独立自旋劈裂参数β和J⊥以Bessel函数给出闭式结果。

Result: 电流诱导的DM矢量沿面外自旋量子化轴ẑ，长距离按1/R衰减，比平衡态耦合慢一阶。它在节点方向键上消失（两自旋带简并）；对对角电流，沿两个晶轴的键上符号相反。该d波特征随Néel矢量和电流反向，可区别于铁磁体。电流还产生对杂质间距为奇函数的对称耦合；在低密度二维导体中DM耦合可达平衡交换的百分之几。

Conclusion: 无需自旋轨道耦合，电荷电流即可在自旋劈裂金属中产生DM相互作用；在d波交错磁体中其DM响应具有可切换、各向异性的d波指纹，为电流调控磁性杂质耦合提供了新机制。

Abstract: The Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya (DM) interaction between magnetic moments usually requires spin--orbit coupling and broken inversion symmetry. We show that in a spin-split metal without spin--orbit coupling a charge current generates a DM interaction between localized spins. Using Keldysh Green's functions (GFs) with a drifting Fermi sea, we compute the Ruderman--Kittel--Kasuya--Yosida (RKKY) interaction between two magnetic impurities in a two-dimensional $d$-wave altermagnet with an additional exchange splitting. To linear order in the current, the correction is a Fermi-surface term, and all its components are obtained in closed form in terms of Bessel functions, valid for arbitrary values of the two independent spin splittings, $\beta$ and $J_\perp$. The induced DM vector points along the spin-quantization axis, which in the present model is the out-of-plane direction $\hat{\mathbf{z}}$. At long distance it decays as $1/R$, one power more slowly than the equilibrium coupling. In the altermagnet it vanishes for bonds along the nodal directions, where the two spin bands are degenerate, and, for a diagonal current, has opposite signs on bonds along the two crystal axes. This $d$-wave signature reverses with the N\'eel vector and with the current, and distinguishes the altermagnet from a ferromagnet. The current also generates symmetric couplings that are odd in the impurity separation. In low-density two-dimensional conductors, the DM coupling can reach a few percent of the equilibrium exchange.

</details>


### [90] [Field-Effect Magnon Transistor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19582)
*Qian Song,Sergei Prokhorenko,Thomas Cleveland,Ashish Omar,Zhi Yao,Sajid Husain,Peter Meisenheimer,Bowen Hao,Ziye Zhu,Pushpendra Gupta,Shashank Ojha,Nicholas Reiterer,Maya Ramesh,Sergio Puebla,Darrell Schlom,Tong Zhou,Laurent Bellaiche,Lane W. Martin,Ramamoorthy Ramesh*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 基于铁电反铁磁体BiFeO3，利用门电场重构畴壁，实现可电编程、非易失的磁振子晶体管，可放大和调制磁振子输运并切换非局域逆自旋霍尔响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁磁振子有望用于高速、低能耗信息处理；补偿磁体中的回旋磁序本征奇宇称自旋劈裂可产生非零磁振子输运，但需要可重构的输运调控手段。

Method: 提出类似晶体管的磁振子场效应器件，以BiFeO3为平台，通过门电场重构畴壁；结合源漏反转、磁场测量、对称性分析和第一性原理计算研究非互易磁振子输运。

Result: 畴壁可在低信号与高信号态之间切换非局域逆自旋霍尔响应，且对磁振子传播方向具有显著依赖性；源漏反转、磁场测量、对称性分析和第一性原理计算揭示该非互易输运与跨畴壁的自旋角动量反转相关。

Conclusion: 结果确立了电可编程、非易失的磁振子晶体管，为反铁磁磁振子输运调控和信息器件提供可行路径。

Abstract: Antiferromagnetic magnons offer a promising platform for highspeed, energy-efficient information processing. Intrinsic odd-parity spin splitting in a cycloidal magnetic order offers a route to nonvanishing magnon transport in compensated magnets. Here, we propose a magnonic field-effect device (akin to a transistor) based on a ferroelectric antiferromagnet BiFeO3 in which a gate electric field reconfigures domain boundaries to amplify/modulate magnon transport. These boundaries switch the nonlocal inverse-spin-Hall response between low- and high-signal states, with a pronounced dependence on the direction of magnon propagation. Source-drain reversal, magnetic-field measurements, symmetry analysis, and first-principles calculations reveal nonreciprocal magnon transport associated with the reversal of spin angular momentum across the domain boundaries. Our results establish an electrically programmable, non-volatile magnon transistor.

</details>


### [91] [Quadrupole Origin of the Orbital Hall Effect in an $sp^3$ Centrosymmetric Lattice: Robustness and Tunability under Disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19651)
*Kevin J. U. Vidarte,Henrique P. Veiga,Aur\'elien Manchon,Tatiana G. Rappoport*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 无序对中心对称无自旋 sp^3 方形晶格模型轨道霍尔输运的影响被系统研究：空位和 Anderson 无序提升轨道霍尔角并保持可观响应，选择性 s-p 杂化无序可非单调增强轨道霍尔电导率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该 sp^3 紧束缚模型是轨道霍尔效应的常用最小模型，但无序如何影响其轨道霍尔输运尚需澄清。

Method: 在中心对称无自旋 sp^3 方形晶格模型中，采用 Chebyshev 多项式展开的 Kubo-Bastin 形式，比较空位、轨道依赖 Anderson 在位无序和选择性 V_{spσ} 键无序对轨道输运的影响。

Result: Anderson 无序和空位使纵向电荷电导率比轨道霍尔电导率下降更快，从而提高轨道霍尔角，且 Anderson 情形在强无序下饱和；选择性 V_{spσ} 键无序仅改变 s-p 杂化通道而保留其余跃迁网络，可导致非单调轨道霍尔响应，并在中等稀释时增强轨道霍尔电导率。

Conclusion: 轨道霍尔响应在空位和在位无序下仍保持可观；选择性调控 s-p 杂化提供了额外调节轨道霍尔电导率的途径。

Abstract: We investigate how disorder affects orbital Hall transport in the centrosymmetric spinless $sp^3$ tight-binding model on a square lattice, widely used as a minimal model for the orbital Hall effect [1]. In the pristine system, the intrinsic orbital Hall response is related to the quadrupolar orbital texture generated by $s$-$p$ hybridization. Using the Kubo-Bastin formalism expanded in Chebyshev polynomials, we analyze the effect of vacancies, orbital-dependent Anderson on-site disorder, and selective $V_{sp\sigma}$-bond disorder on the orbital transport. For Anderson disorder and vacancies, the longitudinal charge conductivity decreases faster than the orbital Hall conductivity over the range studied, leading to an increase of the orbital Hall angle and, in the Anderson case, saturation at stronger disorder. Selective $V_{sp\sigma}$-bond disorder behaves differently: by modifying only the $s$-$p$ hybridization channel while leaving the remaining hopping network intact, it can produce a non-monotonic orbital Hall response and enhance the orbital Hall conductivity at intermediate dilution. Overall, our results show that the orbital Hall response remains sizable under vacancy and on-site disorder, while selective modification of the $s$-$p$ hybridization provides an additional route to tune the orbital Hall conductivity.

</details>

*Katsunori Wakabayashi,Souren Adhikary,Tomoaki Kameda,Kazuhito Tsukagoshi*

TL;DR: R 堆叠双层 WSe2 的对称性使 K 谷层间隧穿严格为零，而 Γ 谷耦合很强；铁电极化通过能谷劈裂和位移场下的跨导奇偶性实现符号反转式读出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层 WSe2 中 K 与 Γ 价带顶能量接近，栅压可在轻/重空穴谷间重新分配载流子；已有图像通常假定势能景观固定。本文关注 R 堆叠如何改变该景观本身，并厘清层间隧穿、铁电极化与输运读出之间的关系。

Method: 结合 R 堆叠双层 WSe2 的对称性分析与第一性原理/DFT 拟合，将 K、Γ 层间耦合作为自由参数处理，并分析位移场下极化势阶、K 双重态劈裂及微分跨导的奇偶对称性；绝对能带偏移依赖交换关联泛函。

Result: K 谷耦合自由拟合上限为 0.017 meV，比室温 k_BT 低约三个数量级；Γ 谷耦合为 361.8 meV。极化势阶劈裂 K 双重态，每个 K 态局域于单层，使 K 边移动半劈裂值 41.7 meV，而 Γ 几乎不变。两种相反铁电极化畴沿位移场轴互为精确镜像；零场下微分跨导等大反号，理想非极性参考响应为零，因此存储极化表现为符号反转而非电流差。扫描位移场可在 1.07 V/nm 处使跨导过零。堆叠会重整化能谷景观，绝对偏移依赖泛函，但对称关系不依赖。

Conclusion: R 堆叠对称性保证 K 谷层间隧穿严格为零，并给出由器件对称性主导、而非能带细节主导的铁电极化读出方案：通过跨导符号反转或过零检测极化，而不是测量电流差；同时堆叠会改变能谷景观，极性与非极性结构并非简单平移关系。

Abstract: In bilayer WSe2 the valence-band maxima at K and {\Gamma} lie within a few thermal energies of one another, and gating redistributes holes between a light and a heavy valley of different mobilities on a landscape that stays fixed. We show that rhombohedral (R) stacking moves the landscape itself. The three-fold symmetry of R stacking forbids interlayer tunneling at K but not at {\Gamma}. The K coupling is therefore exactly zero, not merely small. A fit that leaves the coupling free returns an upper bound of 0.017 meV, three orders of magnitude under k_BT at room temperature, while the {\Gamma} coupling is 361.8 meV. The polarization creates a potential step between the layers and splits the K doublet. Each K state stays on a single layer, so the K edge shifts by half of that splitting, 41.7 meV, which any tunneling would reduce. {\Gamma} barely responds. Two consequences follow from symmetry alone, whatever the size of that shift. The two registries, carrying opposite out-of-plane ferroelectric polarizations, are exact mirror images along the displacement-field axis, and reversing the polarization reverses the displacement of the landscape. Their differential-mode transconductances are equal and opposite at zero field, where the response vanishes for an ideal nonpolar reference. The stored polarization is read out as a reversal of sign and not as a difference of current. Sweeping the field is a second route to the same parity. In the ideal symmetric limit the transconductance passes through zero at the field that cancels the polarization, 1.07 V/nm, at a single temperature. Both routes are limited by the symmetry of the device more than by the band structure. Stacking also renormalizes the landscape, so the polar one is not a translated copy of the nonpolar reference. Absolute offsets follow the exchange-correlation functional. The symmetry relations do not.

</details>

Method: 测量磁场及直流/脉冲偏置下的准静态状态图，结合宏自旋模拟复现相图，并对Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert（LLG）方程进行稳定性分析，推导包括风车阈值电压在内的临界线解析描述。

Result: 观察到平行、反平行、双稳态及动态风车区；风车区中一层翻转触发另一层翻转，产生类脉冲神经元信号。模拟复现相图；比较发现自旋转移矩电压依赖中的二次项无法由焦耳热解释。模型表明可通过接近自旋重取向转变（但牺牲热稳定性）或增强自旋转移矩二次贡献来降低阈值电压。

Conclusion: 结果为优化面向人工脉冲神经元及更广泛神经形态与自旋电子应用的多层MTJ堆栈提供了设计准则。

Abstract: The performance of perpendicular magnetic tunnel junctions (MTJs) depends critically on interfacial properties and layer thicknesses, small variations of which can substantially modify their magnetic and transport characteristics. This work investigates, experimentally and numerically, a perpendicular MTJ with two free layers, a configuration that preserves the precessional dynamics of the device while simplifying its fabrication. Quasi-static state diagrams measured under magnetic fields and DC or pulsed bias reveal static parallel, antiparallel, and bistable regimes, as well as a dynamical windmill regime in which the reversal of one layer triggers that of the other, giving rise to spiking neuron-like signals. Macrospin simulations reproduce these diagrams, and a stability analysis of the Landau-Lifchitz-Gilbert (LLG) equation yields an analytical description of the critical lines, including the windmill threshold voltage. The comparison also reveals a quadratic term in the voltage dependence of the spin-transfer torque that Joule heating cannot explain. The analytical model shows that the threshold voltage can be lowered either by approaching the spin-reorientation transition, at the cost of thermal stability, or by enhancing the quadratic contribution to the spin-transfer torque. These results establish guidelines for optimizing multilayer MTJ stacks for artificial spiking neurons and a broader range of neuromorphic and spintronic applications.

</details>


### [94] [Electrical transport of surface-bound electrons on annealed solid neon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19853)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态氖表面电子是有潜力的表面探针，但传统Sommer-Tanner方法用于此类毫米级固体样品时存在电容串扰和模型适用性问题，需厘清其输运特性与实验可靠性。

Method: 采用常规Sommer-Tanner方法测量固态氖表面束缚电子的直流输运；考察样品厚度约1 mm时的电容串扰随温度和退火的变化，并从同相响应中提取电子系统电阻，分析1.4–20 K范围内的温度依赖。

Result: 电容串扰随温度显著变化且受退火强烈影响；即使退火样品，电子响应也明显不同于液氦表面电子且不能被常规模型满意描述。高温16–20 K电阻行为与蒸气原子散射机制吻合，低温则与温度依赖的表面粗糙度散射一致；分析表明固态氖表面电子迁移率较高，但密度比预期低两个数量级。

Conclusion: 固态氖表面电子可作为研究氖表面形貌的合适探针，但现有实验方法在该体系中的适用性仍需更好理解。

Abstract: We study the dc transport of electrons bound to the surface of solid neon using the conventional Sommer-Tanner method. First, we find that the capacitive cross-talk for the thickness of solid samples on the order of a millimeter varies substantially with the temperature and is strongly affected by the sample annealing. Second, we find that, even for the annealed samples, the observed response of electrons differs significantly from that of the surface-bound electrons on liquid helium and cannot be satisfactorily described by conventional models. Despite this, we managed to extract the resistance of the electron system from its in-phase response and investigate the temperature dependence in a wide range between 1.4 and 20 K. In a high-temperature range between 16 and 20 K, the resistance behavior is found to agree very well with the vapor-atom scattering mechanism, while at lower temperatures it is consistent with temperature-dependent scattering by surface roughness. Our analysis indicates a high electron mobility of electrons on solid neon but two orders of magnitude lower density than expected. These results suggest surface electrons as a suitable probe for studying the morphology of the neon surface but call for a better understanding of the experimental method applicable to such systems.

</details>


### [95] [Theory of anisotropic magnetotransport beyond the constant relaxation-time approximation in multi-Weyl semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19869)
*Azaz Ahmad,Shantanu Mukherjee,Mohd Hashim Raza,Gautham Varma K.,Takami Tohyama*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文建立适用于任意单极荷 N 的多-Weyl 半金属广义半经典玻尔兹曼框架，研究轨道磁矩与杂质散射对纵向磁导和平面霍尔导的影响，发现强谷间散射下可出现磁导符号反转，且高单极荷体系更易出现负纵向磁导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Weyl半金属的手征反常磁输运图像可能不足以描述携带更高单极荷的多-Weyl体系；需要厘清贝里曲率、轨道磁矩、单极荷与内/谷间杂质散射共同作用下的弱场磁输运响应。

Method: 构建任意单极荷 N 的广义半经典玻尔兹曼理论，将轨道磁矩纳入准粒子色散和贝里曲率修正的动力学，并采用动量依赖碰撞积分描述谷内与谷间散射；对 N=1、2、3 以及非磁性和磁性杂质散射计算纵向磁导率 LMC 与平面霍尔导率 PHC。

Result: 无轨道磁矩时，LMC 在所研究散射区间内保持正值且随磁场二次方变化，符合手征反常辅助输运。引入轨道磁矩后，在足够强的谷间散射下，σ0 与 σz 杂质通道的 LMC 发生符号反转；临界谷间散射强度随单极荷 N 增大而系统降低。PHC 在 N=2、3 的相同杂质通道中也发生符号反转，而 N=1 在参数范围内保持正值。发生反转时，LMC 与 PHC 仍分别保持类 sin^2γ 和 sin2γ 的角度依赖。

Conclusion: 贝里曲率、轨道磁矩、单极荷和杂质散射的协同作用可在弱场下产生区别于传统手征反常图像的定性不同磁输运响应，尤其表现为高单极荷体系中更易出现的负纵向磁导和平面霍尔导符号反转。

Abstract: Multi-Weyl semimetals, characterized by Weyl nodes carrying higher monopole charges, provide a platform for exploring topological magnetotransport beyond the conventional Weyl paradigm. Here, we develop a generalized semiclassical Boltzmann framework for multi-Weyl semimetals with arbitrary monopole charge $N$, incorporating the orbital magnetic moment (OMM) into the quasiparticle dispersion and Berry-curvature-modified dynamics, together with a momentum-dependent collision integral accounting for intra- and intervalley scattering. We investigate the longitudinal magnetoconductivity (LMC) and planar Hall conductivity (PHC) for $N=1$, $2$, and $3$, considering both nonmagnetic and magnetic impurity scattering. In the absence of OMM, the LMC remains positive and quadratic in the magnetic field throughout the investigated scattering regime, consistent with conventional chiral-anomaly-assisted transport. Including OMM, however, leads to a sign reversal of the LMC under sufficiently strong intervalley scattering for the $\sigma_0$ and $\sigma_z$ impurity channels. Remarkably, the critical intervalley scattering strength decreases systematically with increasing monopole charge, indicating an enhanced tendency toward negative LMC in higher-charge Weyl systems. Furthermore, we find that the PHC also undergoes a sign reversal for $N=2$ and $N=3$ in the same impurity channels, while remaining positive for $N=1$ within the investigated parameter range. The angular dependences retain their characteristic $\sin^2\gamma$-like and $\sin2\gamma$ profiles for LMC and PHC, respectively, even across the sign reversal. Our results demonstrate that the interplay of Berry curvature, OMM, monopole charge, and impurity scattering can produce qualitatively distinct weak-field magnetotransport responses beyond the conventional chiral-anomaly picture.

</details>


### [96] [Exciton-polarons in monolayer semiconductors with drifting electrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19879)
*Shadman Iqbal,Jesper Levinsen,Meera M. Parish,Dmitry K. Efimkin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出利用外加偏置电流使二维半导体中掺杂费米海在动量空间发生位移，从而让光学吸收探测到有限动量的激子-极化子，并建立电流偏置下谱学性质与平衡态有限动量准粒子性质之间的精确关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统光学测量因光子面内动量近似为零，只能探测零动量附近的激子-极化子，难以研究其有限动量物理；作者希望找到可实验实现的方法来访问这一区域。

Method: 作者考虑在存在电场偏置电流时测量吸收：漂移电子使费米海在动量空间位移，使静止的光学活性激子-极化子相对载流子获得有限动量；并采用激子-极化子问题的图论/图解表述，推导电流偏置下激子-极化子的谱学与准粒子性质同平衡费米海中有限动量对应量之间的精确关系。

Result: 这些关系表明，利用漂移电子的吸收测量可以重建吸引极化子和排斥极化子两个分支的准粒子性质随动量的依赖；作者估计实验可及的电流能够覆盖较宽动量范围，同时仍处于线性响应区。

Conclusion: 电流偏置吸收为探测激子-极化子的有限动量物理提供了一种可行方案，并可将非平衡电流偏置测量与平衡态有限动量激子-极化子性质精确联系起来。

Abstract: Recent work has demonstrated that the optical absorption of moderately doped two-dimensional semiconductors can be described in terms of exciton-polarons. In this picture, interactions between a photoexcited exciton and a Fermi sea of excess charge carriers split the exciton spectrum into a lower-energy attractive polaron branch and a higher-energy repulsive polaron branch. Due to the negligible in-plane momentum of photons, however, optical measurements probe only exciton-polarons near zero momentum, leaving their finite-momentum physics largely unexplored. Here we propose accessing this regime by measuring the absorption in the presence of an electric bias current. In effect, this displaces the electronic Fermi sea in momentum space, such that an optically active exciton-polaron at rest acquires finite momentum relative to the charge carriers. Using a diagrammatic formulation of the exciton-polaron problem, we derive exact relations between the spectral and quasiparticle properties of current-biased exciton-polarons and their finite-momentum counterparts in an equilibrium Fermi sea. These relations show that absorption measurements with drifting electrons can reconstruct the momentum dependence of the quasiparticle properties for both branches. We estimate that experimentally accessible currents can probe a broad momentum range while remaining within the linear-response regime.

</details>


### [97] [Optical spin Edelstein response of d-wave altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20235)
*M. A. Mojarro,R. Carrillo-Bastos,Jes\'us A. Maytorena*
