TL;DR: 研究了二维d波交变磁体在Rashba自旋轨道耦合下的频率域自旋Edelstein效应，揭示带间纵向与横向响应分别由Zeeman量子度规和Zeeman贝里曲率主导，并受费米面拓扑转变、驱动频率和电场方向调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁体中动量依赖的自旋劈裂与补偿磁序为实现自旋极化的电学控制提供了机会，因此需要理解频率域和带间机制下的自旋Edelstein响应及其与费米面拓扑的关系。

Method: 构建二维d波交变磁体加Rashba自旋轨道耦合模型，计算超出带内响应范围的自旋-速度磁化率响应函数，分析带间纵向/横向Edelstein响应、态密度van Hove奇异性、费米能级三个区域以及自旋磁化对驱动频率和面内电场取向的依赖。

Result: 发现带间纵向和横向Edelstein响应分别由Zeeman量子度规和Zeeman贝里曲率决定；态密度在特定负能处呈对数van Hove奇异性，低于该能量出现四个分离费米轮廓；费米能级三个区域使Edelstein磁化率张量呈现特征谱结构，反映费米面拓扑变化；自旋磁化强烈依赖驱动频率和面内电场方向。

Conclusion: 结果为交变磁体中自旋Edelstein效应的电学和光学调控提供了新思路，并可用于自旋电子学应用。

Abstract: The momentum-dependent spin splitting and compensated magnetic order in altermagnets offer opportunities for electrical control of spin polarization. We explore the spin Edelstein effect in the frequency domain of a two-dimensional d-wave altermagnet with Rashba spin-orbit coupling. We calculate the spin-velocity susceptibility response function beyond the intraband response regime and find that the interband longitudinal and transverse Edelstein responses are governed by the Zeeman quantum metric and Zeeman Berry curvature, respectively. The density of states presents a logarithmic van Hove singularity at exactly the negative value of the energy below which four disconnected Fermi contours emerge. This defines three distinctive regions for the position of the Fermi level, through which the Edelstein susceptibility tensor displays characteristic spectral features, reflecting the topological change of the Fermi surface. The resulting spin magnetization shows a strong dependence on the driving frequency and on the orientation of the in-plane external electric field. Our results highlight new possibilities for electrical and optical control of the spin Edelstein effect in altermagnets that could find spintronic applications.

</details>


### [98] [Strain-Engineering Giant Pseudo-Electric Fields in Multilayer Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20323)
*Yiwen Liu,Ambikesh Gupta,Adri\'an Ceferino,Youngjoon Choi,Pierre A. Pantale\'on,Zhen Zhan,Suman Sanki,Hari Stoyanov,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,Francisco Guinea,Andrea Young,Haim Beidenkopf,Nurit Avraham*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作在多层石墨烯中通过原子弛豫形成的Bernal堆垛畴与一维菱方通道，首次实验揭示由拉伸晶格形变产生的巨赝电场及其对电子结构的深刻调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固体中的晶格畸变可产生电磁标量/矢量势；赝矢量势对应赝磁场并已用于零场量子霍尔效应，而赝标量势对应赝电场，虽理论上对许多电子系统有本征影响，却尚未被实验探索。

Method: 利用多层石墨烯体系，原子弛豫将其重构为被一维菱方通道分隔的大尺度Bernal堆垛畴；结合原子尺度成像表征应变下的晶格形变，并进行谱学研究以探测菱方通道中的电子结构。

Result: 在菱方通道中观察到拉伸晶格形变诱导巨赝电场，产生超平色散、发散态密度和 large energy gap；增强的电子关联导致平带不稳定及态密度条纹调制，而无需强外部位移场；赝标量势打开的能隙还保护了无外场下持续存在的拓扑边界态。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了石墨烯弹性的原子尺度表征和晶格畸变产生非平庸赝电场的直接光谱证据，并给出一种本征、可调谐地实现新量子态的途径。

Abstract: Electromagnetic scalar and vector potentials can be engineered in solids through lattice distortions, providing a versatile means of tuning electronic band structures. The pseudovector component, which generates pseudo-magnetic fields, has received much attention for its ability to realize a zero-field quantum Hall effect. However, the pseudoscalar component, giving rise to pseudo-electric fields to which many electronic systems are intrinsically susceptible, has not yet been experimentally explored. Here, we demonstrate the emergence and profound consequences of the pseudoscalar potential in a multilayer graphene system. Atomic relaxation reconstructs the system into large Bernalstacked domains separated by one-dimensional rhombohedral channels. Within this inter-dimensional system, we achieve the first direct atomic-scale imaging of distinct lattice deformations in graphene under strain. In rhombohedral channels, we observe that the tensile lattice deformation induces giant pseudo-electric fields, producing ultra-flat dispersion with divergent density of states and a large energy gap. This results in enhanced electronic correlations, leading to flat-band instabilities and striped modulation in the density of states that would otherwise require strong external displacement fields. Moreover, the gap opened by the pseudoscalar potential protects topological boundary states that persist in the absence of applied fields. Our results provide a comprehensive atomic-scale characterization of graphene elasticity and direct spectroscopic evidence for the nontrivial pseudo-electric field arising from lattice distortion. More broadly, it provides an intrinsic and tunable pathway to new quantum states

</details>


### [99] [Optical writing of Dzyaloshinskii N\'eel-type skyrmions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20878)
*Dinar Khusyainov,Lukas K\"orber,Sheng Li,Rein Liefferink,Thibaud Denneulin,Andr\'as Kov\'acs,Fabian Kammerbauer,Robert Fr\"omter,Mathias Kl\"aui,Dmytro Afanasiev,Alexey Kimel,Johan H. Mentink,Rafal E. Dunin-Borkowski,Theo Rasing*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在超薄Co/Pt薄膜中，利用皮秒圆偏振激光脉冲序列可确定性写入Néel型、固定手性的磁斯格明子，其稳定机制仅为Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超快激光有望写入纳米尺度拓扑磁结构用于信息处理，但磁斯格明子的光学生成在位置、类型尤其手性控制方面仍具挑战；常驱动斯格明子的磁偶极相互作用无法打破相反手性的简并。

Method: 采用皮秒圆偏振激光脉冲序列在超薄Co/Pt薄膜中写入斯格明子，并结合洛伦兹透射电子显微镜与磁光显微镜在纳米至微米多尺度重建其内部自旋结构；通过非对称斯格明子膨胀测量确定DMI的符号与大小并识别所写手性。

Result: 实现了仅由Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用稳定的Néel型、固定手性斯格明子的光学写入，并完整重构其多尺度自旋结构，测定了DMI符号、大小及对应手性。

Conclusion: 该工作将确定性光学写入与可控的斯格明子自旋结构相联系，推动了拓扑磁结构的光学调控及其在光自旋电子学中的应用。

Abstract: Ultrashort laser pulses offer a route to write nanoscale topological magnetic textures for information processing. Magnetic skyrmions are prototypical examples, yet optically generating them with controlled position, type, and, in particular, chirality remains challenging. This challenge arises because magnetic dipolar interactions, which often drive skyrmion formation, preserve the degeneracy between opposite chiralities, leaving no uniquely selected spin configuration. Here, we show that in an ultrathin Co/Pt film, sequences of picosecond circularly polarized laser pulses can write N\'eel-type, fixed-chirality skyrmions stabilized solely by the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction. Combining Lorentz transmission electron microscopy and magneto-optical microscopy, we comprehensively reconstruct their internal spin structure across multiple length scales, ranging from nanometres to micrometers. Using asymmetric skyrmion-expansion measurements, we determined the sign and magnitude of the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction and identified the written chirality. Our findings connect deterministic optical writing with the controlled spin structure of skyrmions, advancing optical control of topological textures for opto-spintronic applications.

</details>


### [100] [Altermagnetism in moir\'e quasicrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20952)
*Zi-Ming Wang,Rui Chen,Dong-Hui Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出通过扭转共线磁性层实现准晶交错磁性的对称性方案，并预言30°扭转蜂窝双层中的十二重准晶i波交错磁相及45°扭转双层中的镜面驱动l波相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统交错磁性局限于周期晶体；准晶的非晶体学对称性可支持高阶自旋极化图案，因此需要将其拓展到准晶体系。

Method: 采用基于对称性的扭转工程，对共线磁性双层进行不同转角堆叠，分析其准晶对称性与交错磁序；并用平面波投影的自旋分辨谱函数和自旋分辨电导表征自旋极化模式。

Result: 30°扭转蜂窝晶格双层形成十二重准晶并支持i波交错磁相；45°扭转双层保持四重旋转对称性并支持具有八条节线的镜面驱动l波相。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了通过扭转工程系统实现高角阶准晶交错磁性的通用路径。

Abstract: Altermagnetism is a collinear compensated magnetic phase with zero net magnetization and momentum-dependent spin polarization. Quasicrystalline altermagnetism extends altermagnetism beyond periodic crystals, with noncrystallographic symmetries allowing high-order spin polarization patterns. Here, we propose a symmetry-based approach to realizing quasicrystalline altermagnetism by twisting collinear magnetic layers. Specifically, we demonstrate that the $30^{\circ}$-twisted honeycomb lattice bilayers form the dodecagonal quasicrystals supporting an $i$-wave altermagnetic phase. We further find that $45^{\circ}$-twisted bilayers preserving fourfold rotational symmetry support a mirror-driven $l$-wave phase with eight nodal lines. We characterize these altermagnetic patterns through plane-wave-projected spin-resolved spectral functions and spin-resolved conductances. Our work provides a systematic route to high-angular-order quasicrystalline altermagnetism through twist engineering.

</details>


### [101] [Global bound-state spectrum and spin- and angular-momentum-dependent confinement in an inverse-$3/2$ magnetic quantum dot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20963)
*Luis B. Castro*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究了二维电子在反 3/2 径向磁场中由 Schrödinger–Pauli 方程描述的束缚态谱与束缚阈值，指出准精确解只是更广泛 biconfluent Heun 谱中的孤立精确点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破仅依赖准精确多项式扇区的传统处理，以全局方式刻画非多项式束缚态分支及自旋和角动量相关的连续谱边缘。

Method: 将径向问题化为 biconfluent Heun 方程；不限制在准精确多项式解，而通过端点选取的 Frobenius 解与次主导渐近解之间的谱 Wronskian 施加全局束缚态条件；并独立精确分析连续谱边缘。

Result: 得到连续的非多项式束缚态分支，已知准精确解作为孤立精确点嵌入其中；多项式指标 N 仅是准精确扇区内的终止指标，不能唯一标记谱分支；连续谱边缘给出依赖自旋和角动量的临界耦合，可低于准精确限制 U0a>4。

Conclusion: 准精确可解性应视为更广泛 biconfluent-Heun 谱中的一组特殊解析点，该谱具有依赖自旋和角动量的束缚阈值。

Abstract: We investigate the bound-state spectrum and confinement thresholds of a two-dimensional electron governed by the Schr\"{o}dinger--Pauli equation in an inverse-$3/2$ radial magnetic-field profile. The radial problem reduces to the biconfluent Heun equation, but instead of restricting the solution to the quasi-exact polynomial sector, we impose the bound-state condition globally through a spectral Wronskian between the endpoint-selected Frobenius solution and the subdominant asymptotic solution. The resulting spectrum contains continuous nonpolynomial bound-state branches on which the known quasi-exact solutions appear as isolated exact points. In this global description, the polynomial index $N$ remains a termination index within the quasi-exact sector but does not uniquely label the spectral branches. An independent exact analysis of the continuum edge yields spin- and angular-momentum-dependent critical couplings that can lie below the quasi-exact restriction $U_0a>4$, where $U_0$ is the Coulomb-strength parameter and $a$ is the magnetic-profile length scale. These results establish quasi-exact solvability as a set of special analytic points embedded in a broader biconfluent-Heun spectrum with spin- and angular-momentum-dependent confinement thresholds.

</details>


### [102] [A tunable multi-gated platform for superlattice engineering in bilayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20998)
*Saisab Bhowmik,Satyarth Srivastav,Aditya Mehta,Robin Bajaj,Suvronil Datta,Rohit S Bhandari,Nikhlesh S. Mehta,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Manish Jain,Hridis K. Pal,U. Chandni*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作在置于带三角孔阵列图案化石墨栅极上的高品质Bernal双层石墨烯中，实现了可由三个独立静电栅极调控的“栅极工程”超晶格，并观测到微带形成相关的绝缘态、激活能隙和载流子反型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小角扭转范德华异质结虽可产生长周期低能微带，但常受扭转角不均匀、应变和 disorder 限制，难以获得可调且高质量的相关相；作者希望无需机械扭转即可实现可重构的能带工程。

Method: 将高品质Bernal双层石墨烯放置在具有三角孔阵列的图案化石墨栅极上，形成周期势超晶格；利用三个独立静电栅极调控超晶格势，并通过变温输运、Hall测量和理论比较研究近电荷中性点行为。

Result: 在电荷中性点附近观察到表征微带形成的绝缘态；变温输运给出与理论一致的激活能隙，Hall测量显示载流子反型；不同栅极组合可静电重构超晶格，实现无需扭转的灵活能带结构设计。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种可静电重构的栅极工程超晶格平台，为能带工程提供新路径，并有望推广至天然材料难以实现的复杂周期结构。

Abstract: Periodic potentials with length scales much larger than crystal lattice constants can generate low-energy electronic minibands. While small-angle twisted van der Waals heterostructures have been widely used to realize such physics, correlated phases are often limited by twist-angle inhomogeneity, strain, and disorder. Here, we investigate high-quality Bernal bilayer graphene placed on a patterned graphite gate, featuring a triangular array of holes, forming a new class of "gate-engineered" superlattices tunable through three independent electrostatic gates. Near the charge neutrality point, we observe insulating states characteristic of miniband formation. Temperature-dependent transport reveals activated gaps consistent with theory, and Hall measurements indicate charge-carrier inversion. Notably, the superlattice can be reconfigured electrostatically using various gate combinations, enabling flexible band-structure design without mechanical twisting. The results highlight a new route to band engineering, potentially extendable to complex periodic structures that are untenable in natural materials.

</details>


### [103] [Large pseudo-orbital magnetic moment of magnons in CrI$_3$ nanotubes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21066)
*David Sanz (Universidad de Alicante,San Vicente del Raspeig,Spain),J. Fern\'andez-Rossier (Universidad de Alicante,San Vicente del Raspeig,Spain,International Iberian Nanotechnology Laboratory,Braga,Portugal)*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用线性自旋波理论预测，轴向磁场使CrI$_3$纳米管中的径向磁序转变为锥形磁序，并对 magnon 产生 Aharonov-Bohm 通量，从而赋予 magnon 约6个玻尔磁子的赝轨道磁矩，可作为实验探测该特殊磁序的特征信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新发现的CrI$_3$纳米管因二维母体材料强的面外磁各向异性，预期具有非常规径向磁序；但这些奇特磁态难以直接探测，因此需要寻找可用实验观测的独特特征。

Method: 采用线性自旋波理论分析CrI$_3$纳米管中的磁激发，研究轴向磁场如何将径向磁相倾斜为锥形磁相，并等效为穿过纳米管的 magnon Aharonov-Bohm 通量。

Result: 该Aharonov-Bohm通量使 magnon 获得赝轨道磁矩，其大小约为6个玻尔磁子，是常规 magnon 自旋磁矩的三倍，因而可通过 magnon 光谱探测。

Conclusion: 这种显著增强的赝轨道磁矩为CrI$_3$纳米管中径向与锥形磁序提供了可实验检验的“smoking gun”特征信号。

Abstract: Recently discovered CrI$_3$ nanotubes are expected to host unusual radial magnetic order states, on account of the strong out-of-plane magnetic anisotropy of the two-dimensional parent material. The detection of these peculiar magnetic states is challenging, and motivates the quest for unique features that can be probed experimentally. Using linear spin-wave theory, we show that an axial magnetic field tilts the radial phase into a conical one that threads the tube with an Aharonov-Bohm flux for magnons. This flux endows magnons with a pseudo-orbital magnetic moment, reaching approximately six Bohr magnetons, three times conventional magnon spin moment, and therefore detectable through magnon spectroscopy, providing a smoking gun for the radial and conical magnetic order in CrI$_3$ nanotubes.

</details>


### [104] [Interaction Landscapes of Structurally Distorted Toroidal Classical Spin Textures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21194)
*Henry Pommer,Arkamita Bandyopadhyay,Jamal Berakdar*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 建立以静态电流环表示局域磁矩的环状织构经典离散磁静模型，研究椭圆、偏心、交替和倾斜等结构形变对磁偶极-偶极相互作用、环状矩及双环状结构间相互作用能的影响，并用第一性原理分子体系作定性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分几何形变与磁形变对环状磁织构相互作用的影响，理解分子磁体和相关纳米磁体中环状序所导致的取向依赖相互作用。

Method: 将局域磁矩视为静态电流环，构建环状织构的离散模型；引入椭圆、偏心/不对称、交替和倾斜等形变，计算形变对环状矩和成对环状结构间相互作用能的影响；并对分子自旋环状体系进行第一性原理概念验证比较。

Result: 形变显著改变环状矩和相互作用能景观，产生特征对称性与各向异性，且对具体形变高度敏感；第一性原理计算显示定性相似的取向依赖行为。计算所得相互作用本质上仍是局域磁矩间的常规成对磁偶极-偶极作用，环状矩仅表征集体织构，并非额外的基本相互作用项。

Conclusion: 环状矩是描述集体自旋织构的量，相互作用仍由常规磁偶极-偶极作用主导；该模型为研究分子磁体和相关纳米磁体中环状序的取向依赖相互作用提供了平台。

Abstract: We develop a classical discrete magnetostatic model of localized magnetic moments arranged in a toroidal texture, where the moments are represented by static current loops and study the behavior of the magnetic dipole-dipole interaction under several types of structural deformations. The purpose is to disentangle effects due to geometric and magnetic deformations, including elliptical, lopsided, alternating, and tilted configurations. In particular, we investigate how distortions modify the toroidal moment and the interaction energy between pairs of toroidal structures. The resulting interaction landscapes exhibit characteristic symmetry patterns and anisotropies that are highly sensitive to the underlying deformation. As a separate proof-of-concept comparison, first-principles calculations on a molecular spin-toroidal system reveal qualitatively similar orientation-dependent behavior. The calculated interaction is microscopically the conventional pairwise magnetic dipole-dipole interaction between localized moments arranged in a spin-toroidal texture; the toroidal moment characterizes the collective texture and should not be interpreted as introducing an additional elementary interaction term. Our results provide a platform for exploring orientation-dependent interactions of spin-toroidal textures in molecular magnets and related nanoscale magnetic systems that are essential to understand the role of toroidal order.

</details>


### [105] [Torsional Hall Viscosity of Massive Chern Insulators: Finite Temperature and Chemical Potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21217)
*Weizhen Jia,Ioannis Matthaiakakis,David Rodr\'iguez Fern\'andez,Zhuo-Yu Xian,Johanna Erdmenger,Ewelina M. Hankiewicz,Ren\'e Meyer*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限温度和化学势下陈绝缘体的扭转霍尔黏度 ζ_H，涵盖有质量狄拉克费米子、恒定垂直磁场以及单块 BHZ 模型中的动量相关质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将扭转霍尔黏度理论推广到实验相关条件，并为其未来探测提供依据。

Method: 采用连续模型分析有质量狄拉克费米子与单块 BHZ 哈密顿量，考虑有限温度、化学势、恒定面外磁场以及由 BHZ 形变产生的动量依赖质量。

Result: 有质量狄拉克理论中温度和化学势给出紫外有限修正；化学势位于体能隙外时即使在零温也改变 ζ_H。恒定磁场下响应由相对论朗道能级决定，零温呈平台结构并在有限温被平滑，跳变正比于狄拉克质量、反比于磁长度平方和新占据朗道能级能量。对 (Hg,Mn)Te 量子阱相关参数，单块 BHZ 连续模型显示跨质量畴壁的跳变在化学势足够深入带隙且远大于热能时仍保持 BHZ 形变带来的真空增强。

Conclusion: 结果将扭转霍尔黏度理论扩展至有限温度和化学势等实验相关情形，并可能促进其未来探测。

Abstract: We study the torsional Hall viscosity $\zeta_{\rm H}$ of Chern insulators at finite temperature and chemical potential. Our analysis considers the massive Dirac fermion and two deformations: a constant out-of-plane magnetic field and a momentum-dependent mass described by the single-block Bernevig-Hughes-Zhang (BHZ) Hamiltonian. For the massive Dirac theory, we show that temperature and chemical potential lead to UV-finite corrections and that a chemical potential outside the bulk gap modifies $\zeta_{\rm H}$ even at zero temperature. In a constant magnetic field, the response of massive Dirac fermions is dictated by the relativistic Landau-level spectrum, leading to a plateau structure at zero temperature that is smoothed at finite temperature. The jumps are proportional to the Dirac mass and inversely proportional to the squared magnetic length and the newly occupied Landau-level energy. Finally, we present a detailed study of the correction to $\zeta_{\rm H}$ in the single-block BHZ continuum model due to temperature and chemical potential. For parameters relevant to (Hg,Mn)Te quantum wells, the magnitude of the jump across a mass domain wall retains its vacuum enhancement due to the BHZ deformation as long as the chemical potential lies sufficiently deep inside the gap compared with the thermal energy. Our results extend the theory of torsional Hall viscosity to experimentally relevant conditions and may facilitate its future detection.

</details>


### [106] [Low-energy states of a Su-Schrieffer-Heeger chain connected perpendicularly to a metallic lead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21227)
*A. A. Aligia*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究了单端垂直耦合金属引线的拓扑SSH链，解析纳入有限尺寸端模分裂，并给出低能零温电导与谱密度表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展Martínez等关于拓扑SSH链与金属引线耦合的工作，明确孤立SSH链端模有限尺寸分裂对低能输运的影响。

Method: 解析高精度确定孤立SSH链端模分裂；在低能区推导零温下通过引线的电导及相谱密度的简洁解析表达式。

Result: 当化学势与半满SSH链一致时，电导达到最大值，等于无SSH链时引线的电导；当化学势偏移半个端模分裂量时，电导消失。

Conclusion: 有限尺寸端模分裂主导SSH链-金属引线耦合体系的低能输运，所给解析结果提供了该体系的简明描述。

Abstract: Recently, Su-Schrieffer-Heeger chains (SSHCs) coupled to metallic leads have attracted attention. In particular Mart\'{\i}nez {\it et al.} [Phys. Rev. Res. {\bf 8}, 043118 (2026)] studied a topological SSHC connected at one of its ends perpendicularly to a metallic lead modeled by a tight-binding chain. Here, we extend their results by incorporating the finite-size splitting of the end modes of the isolated SSHC, which are determined analytically with high accuracy. We also derive simple analytical expressions, valid in the low-energy regime, for the zero-temperature conductance through the lead and the relevant spectral densities. We find that, when the chemical potential coincides with that of the half-filled SSHC, the conductance reaches its maximum value, equal to that of the lead in the absence of the SSHC. By contrast, the conductance vanishes when the chemical potential is shifted by an amount equal to half the end-mode splitting.

</details>


### [107] [Vortex states in submicron titanomagnetite particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21354)
*Olga N. Serebryakova,Nikolai A. Usov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过数值模拟研究椭球形钛磁铁矿颗粒的微磁状态与剩磁，揭示钛含量、长宽比和粒径对涡旋态及单畴尺寸的控制作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 明确钛磁铁矿颗粒在不同成分、形貌和尺寸下的磁畴状态、单畴临界尺寸及稀释集合体的剩磁行为。

Method: 对Fe(3-x)TixO4（0.4<x<0.6）、半轴比0.5–1.5、横向直径100–480 nm的椭球形颗粒进行微磁学数值模拟，并利用均匀态与涡旋态的能量图确定单畴粒径。

Result: 颗粒中可出现多种涡旋微磁状态，其类型取决于钛浓度、长宽比和粒径；计算了不同钛含量稀释集合体的剩磁随横向直径D的变化，发现亚微米颗粒在D约500 nm时归一化剩磁极低，M/Ms<0.01。

Conclusion: 钛磁铁矿颗粒的磁状态和剩磁强烈依赖成分、形状和尺寸；亚微米椭球形钛磁铁矿颗粒可具有极低剩磁，这对解释其磁性记录与岩石磁学行为具有重要意义。

Abstract: Numerical simulation is used to study the magnetic properties of spheroidal titanomagnetite particles Fe(3-x)TixO4 (0.4 < x < 0.6) with semi-axes ratio ranging from 0.5 to 1.5 and transverse diameters D = 100 - 480 nm. It is shown that various vortex micromagnetic states can emerge in the titanomagnetite particles depending on the titanium concentration, aspect ratio and particle diameter. The single-domain particle diameters are determined using the energy diagrams for stationary uniform and vortex states. The remanent magnetization of dilute assemblies of titanomagnetite particles with varying titanium content are calculated as a function of the transverse particle diameter D. It is demonstrated that for submicron-sized titanomagnetite particles, D ~ 500 nm, the normalized remanent magnetization of the assembly reaches very low values, M/Ms < 0.01, where Ms is the saturation magnetization of the particles.

</details>


### [108] [Energy spectrum of trions in semiconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21475)
*Pavel A. Belov,Lucas Happ,Stefan Scheel,Evgeny A. Yarevsky*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文用有限元法和高斯展开法（GEM）计算体半导体中trion（三体带电激子）的能级，覆盖宽空穴-电子质量比范围，并验证了数值精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体半导体中trion三体能级对理解多体库仑束缚态很重要，但此前计算在精度和普适质量比范围上仍有限，需要可靠且高精度的数值基准。

Method: 在总角动量表象中构造Schrödinger方程的有限元解；同时对波函数的三个Faddeev分量分别应用GEM。计算总角动量J=0和J=1的trion能级，覆盖大多数现有半导体化合物对应的空穴-电子质量比，并与一维量子线简化模型、其他公开三体程序及分子氢离子振动能级比较。

Result: 两种方法给出的trion能量随质量比的变化与一维量子线模型定性一致；与其他三体代码及分子氢离子振动能级数据吻合良好，确认了数值精度。GEM相较以往计算显著提高了trion能量精度。

Conclusion: 对于大多数半导体化合物，只存在一个总角动量为零的trion束缚态；该工作为体半导体trion能级提供了高精度数值结果和基准。

Abstract: Energy levels of trions in a bulk semiconductor crystal are calculated by two different numerical methods. On the one hand, we construct the finite-element solution of the Schr\"{o}dinger equation in the total angular momentum representation. On the other hand, we use the Gaussian expansion method (GEM), applied to each of the three Faddeev components of the wave function. The results for the total angular momenta $J=0$ and $J=1$ of the trion were obtained for a wide range of the hole-to-electron mass ratios covering most existing semiconductor compounds. The dependencies of the energies on the mass ratio for trions in a bulk crystal qualitatively agree with the results for a simplified model of a trion in a 1D quantum wire, for which the three-body energies can be calculated with arbitrary precision as well as easily approximated using Born-Oppenheimer potential-energy curves. A comparison of our results with data from other publicly available three-body codes as well as with the energies of well-known atomic counterparts, in particular, with vibrational energy levels of the molecular hydrogen ion, shows a good agreement and confirms the accuracy of the numerical data. We found that GEM significantly improves the precision of the obtained trion energies compared to previous calculations. Our calculations demonstrate that, for most semiconductor compounds, only one trion state with zero total angular momentum exists.

</details>


### [109] [Electrostatic and magnetic control of the anomalous Josephson phase in ferromagnetic hybrid junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21593)
*D. Rana,O. S. Kurapati,N. Vanjarapu,J. Zhao,J. B. F. Wolters,Y. Liu,P. Krogstrup,A. L. R. Manesco,S. D. Escribano,E. Prada,S. Vaitiek\.{e}nas*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在InAs纳米线外延EuS铁磁壳与Al超导壳形成的约瑟夫森结中，相位敏感测量观测到随轴向磁场扫描连续且滞回的反常约瑟夫森相位，并在零场下保留依赖磁历史的剩余相位偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究铁磁/超导复合纳米线中反常约瑟夫森相位的产生、记忆效应及其与纳米线横向电子结构和Rashba自旋轨道耦合的关系。

Method: 采用超导干涉仪进行相位敏感测量，在轴向磁场扫描和静电栅压下表征约瑟夫森结；结合自洽静电模拟提取有效Rashba自旋轨道耦合随栅压的变化。

Result: 观察到连续、滞回的反常相位偏移；零场下存在剩余相位偏置且依赖磁场历史；栅压强烈调控相位响应幅度，模拟得到的有效Rashba自旋轨道耦合与栅压依赖的相位响应一致。

Conclusion: Rashba自旋轨道耦合在反常约瑟夫森相位的静电调控中起核心作用，并支持通过磁历史与栅压实现可调、非易失的相位偏置。

Abstract: We report phase-sensitive measurements of Josephson junctions formed in InAs nanowires with epitaxial ferromagnetic EuS and superconducting Al shells. Using a superconducting interferometer, we observe a continuous, hysteretic anomalous phase shift under axial magnetic-field sweeps. A remanent phase bias persists at zero applied field and depends on magnetic history. Electrostatic gating strongly modifies the magnitude of the phase response, revealing its sensitivity to the transverse electronic structure of the nanowire. Self-consistent electrostatic simulations show that the effective Rashba spin-orbit coupling follows the measured gate-dependent phase response, suggesting its central role in the electrostatic control of the anomalous Josephson phase.

</details>

### [110] [Stabilization of LaH$_{10}$ with Thorium: Superconductivity in (La,Th)H$_{10}$ up to 232 K near 1 Mbar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20170)
*Dmitrii V. Semenok,Mikhail A. Volkov,Andrey V. Sadakov,Alexander T. Daniyarkhodzhaev,Alexey S. Usoltsev,Vladimir M. Pudalov,Mikhail I. Volgin,Iurii M. Nevolin,Toni Helm,Di Zhou,Viktor V. Struzhkin,Ivan A. Troyan*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: Th 替代 La 可在约 1 Mbar 稳定 (La,Th)H10 笼状结构，并将超导起始 Tc 提高到 232 K（134 GPa），同时 La-Th-H 体系呈现多种氢化物相和可测量的 s-wave 超导能隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三元 La 基氢化物是高温超导体的重要候选；作者希望通过 Th 替代 La 稳定 LaH10 型笼状结构、尽量减少超导抑制，并系统研究 La-Th-H 体系的相组成与超导临界行为。

Method: 由较空气稳定的 La2Th、La3Th、La5Th 合金合成 (La,Th)H10；采用接触电阻测量确定 Tc，并用电阻和无线射频测量在磁场及不同压力下验证超导；利用 380 mA 脉冲电流研究 P63/mmc-(La,Th)H9 的临界现象，并按 s-wave 模型拟合能隙。

Result: 立方 (La,Th)H10 在 134 GPa 达到起始 Tc = 232 K。P63/mmc-(La,Th)H9 在 173 GPa 得到单超导能隙 Δ(0)=37±4.5 meV，2Δ(0)/kTc=4.9±0.6。体系还鉴定出纳米晶 I4/mmm-(La,Th)H4（5-6 nm）、(La,Th)H6、hP13/cF80/A15 型 (La,Th)H5.75-6.25（70-80 GPa 下 Tc≈83 K）以及可能的 (La,Th)H16 多氢化物。

Conclusion: Th 替代可稳定高性能 (La,Th)H10 超导相并仅轻微抑制超导性；La-Th-H 体系化学相丰富，为研究高压氢化物超导机理、能隙行为和相稳定性提供了新平台。

Abstract: Ternary lanthanum-based hydrides are among the best high-temperature superconductors. Here we show that substituting La with thorium stabilizes La(Th)H$_{10}$ clathrate structure at $\sim$1 Mbar while only minimally suppressing superconductivity. The resulting cubic (La,Th)H$_{10}$ phase, synthesized from relatively air-stable La$_2$Th, La$_3$Th and La$_5$Th alloys, reaches an onset $\textit{T$_c$}$ = 232 K at 134 GPa, as confirmed by contact resistance measurements. Superconductivity was verified by two independent methods: electrical resistance and contactless radio-frequency measurements under applied magnetic fields at various pressures. Using a record-high pulsed current of 380 mA, we investigated critical phenomena in P6$_3$/mmc-(La,Th)H$_9$ at 173 GPa, which allowed for the experimental determination of a single superconducting gap within the s-wave model: $\Delta(0) = 37 \pm 4.5$ meV and $2\Delta(0)/kT_c = 4.9 \pm 0.6$. The La-Th-H system is chemically rich: in addition to the decahydride and nonahydride mentioned above, we also identified nanocrystalline I4/mmm-(La,Th)H$_4$ (5-6 nm grain size), (La,Th)H$_6$, as well as $hP13$, $cF80$ and $A15$ phases with compositions of (La,Th)H$_{5.75-6.25}$ and $\textit{T$_c$}$ $\approx$ 83 K at 70-80 GPa, and a possible (La,Th)H$_{16}$ polyhydride.

</details>


### [111] [High Characteristic Voltage YBCO Josephson Junctions Written With a Focused Helium Ion Beam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19718)
*Rochelle Y. Qu,Aravind R. Sreeja,Adhilsha Parachikunnumal,Alexander Brooks,Nicole Y. Chin,Jay C. LeFebvre,Roger K. Lake,Shane A. Cybart*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 利用聚焦氦离子束在YBCO薄膜中直写面内约瑟夫森结，获得1.6 K下IcR约929 µV，显著高于本实验室此前200–400 µV的典型值，并证实其约瑟夫森行为及在高温超导逻辑/SQUID应用中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约瑟夫森结的特征电压IcR决定电路工作频率和灵敏度；此前YBCO结的IcR通常仅200–400 µV，因此需要提高该指标以推进RSFQ、AQFP逻辑和高温工作SQUID。

Method: 用聚焦氦离子束对4 µm宽YBCO薄膜电极进行单线辐照，剂量500 ions/nm，直写面内约瑟夫森结；测量温度依赖电阻、电流-电压特性、临界电流磁場调制及20.5 GHz微波下的Shapiro台阶。

Result: 1.6 K下IcR达929 µV；器件表现出约瑟夫森行为，过剩电流低于临界电流的5%；临界电流在结转变附近随温度陡降并在低温饱和。作者将其归因于金属-绝缘体转变处的势垒：有限温度下通过局域态的跳跃导电，冷却时冻结。

Conclusion: 该尺寸下获得更高特征电压对RSFQ、绝热量子通量参变器逻辑及高温工作的SQUID是积极结果；但当前数据尚不能区分表面制备、样品选择、束聚焦和样品存储等改进各自的贡献。

Abstract: We report in-plane Josephson junctions written directly into YBa$_2$Cu$_3$O$_{7-\delta}$ (YBCO) thin films with a focused helium ion beam (He FIB). The characteristic voltage $I_{\mathrm{C}}R$ of these junctions, where $I_{\mathrm{C}}$ is the Josephson critical current and $R$ is the junction resistance, sets the operating frequency and the sensitivity of the circuits built from them. A single line irradiation with a dose of \SI{500}{ions/nm} across a \SI{4}{\micro\metre} wide electrode produced a junction with $I_{\mathrm{C}}R$ of 929 \si{\micro\volt} at \SI{1.6}{K}, which is `higher than the 200 to 400 \si{\micro\volt} typical of junctions fabricated previously in our laboratory. Temperature dependent resistance, current-voltage characteristics, magnetic field modulation of the critical current, and Shapiro steps under 20.5~GHz microwave irradiation show Josephson behavior with an excess current below 5 percent of the critical current. The critical current falls steeply with temperature near the junction transition and saturates at low temperature. We attribute this behavior to a barrier at the metal insulator transition, where hopping conduction through localized states is active at finite temperature and freezes out on cooling. We describe the changes in surface preparation, sample selection, beam focusing, and sample storage that accompanied the improvement, although the present data do not separate their individual contributions. Larger characteristic voltages at these dimensions are an encouraging result for rapid single flux quantum and adiabatic quantum flux parametron logic and for superconducting quantum interference devices operating at elevated temperature.

</details>


### [112] [Phase diagram and lattice structure of interlayer vortices in multilayer Ising superconductors in parallel magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20469)
*Subhradeep Mahata,Uddalok Nag,J. K. Jain*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 利用自洽Bogoliubov-de Gennes方程研究面内磁场下多层s波Ising超导体，揭示层数和层间跃迁如何调控相图与涡旋结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Ginzburg-Landau处理适用范围有限且需预设序参量形式，需在更广参数区间自洽解析多层Ising超导体中面内磁场诱导的层间涡旋、BCS相与FFLO相演化。

Method: 自洽求解微观Bogoliubov-de Gennes方程，比较双层、四层和无限层体系，在不预设能隙形式下解析其空间与层依赖，并考察层间跃迁对涡旋排布和表面层能隙调制的影响。

Result: 随层数增加，无层间涡旋的BCS相可承受的磁场范围减小，无限层体系在非零磁通下基本消失，与多层2H-NbSe2厚度依赖实验一致；无限层极限下含层间涡旋的FFLO相在低温下延伸至更大面内场，解释上临界场低温增强。四层中增大层间跃迁可使涡旋由外层间结重排至内层间结，且内部涡旋引起、可延伸至表面层的能隙抑制增强；六层计算表明内部涡旋对表面层能隙有有限但较弱的调制。

Conclusion: 层数和层间隧穿是决定多层Ising超导体相图、涡旋排布及表面能隙响应的关键参数，微观自洽BdG方法能捕捉超越GL描述的多层涡旋物理。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate layered $s$-wave Ising superconductors under an in-plane magnetic field using self-consistent solutions of the microscopic Bogoliubov-de Gennes equations. Comparing bilayer, four-layer, and infinite-layer systems, we determine how the phase diagram and vortex structure evolve with layer number. Compared to standard Ginzburg-Landau treatments, our approach applies over a broader parameter regime and self-consistently resolves the spatial and layer dependence of the gap amplitude without prescribing its form. We find that the field range supporting the conventional Bardeen-Cooper-Schrieffer (BCS) phase (defined as having no interlayer vortices) is reduced with increasing layer number and is essentially absent at nonzero magnetic flux in the infinite-layer system, consistent with recent thickness-dependent experiments on multilayer 2H-NbSe$_2$. In the infinite-layer limit, the Fulde-Ferrell-Larkin-Ovchinnikov (FFLO) phase containing interlayer vortices persists to progressively larger in-plane fields as temperature is lowered below the zero-field critical temperature, consistent with the experimentally observed low-temperature enhancement of the upper critical field. The vortex structure also evolves with interlayer hopping: in four-layer systems, for the fluxes and system sizes considered, increasing hopping reorganizes vortices from outer to inner interlayer junctions. At larger hopping, the gap distortion associated with an interior vortex extends to the surface layers, producing gap suppression comparable to that in the interior layers. Representative six-layer calculations show that interior vortices likewise produce a finite, although weaker, modulation of the surface-layer gap. These results highlight layer number and interlayer tunneling as key parameters governing the phase diagram and vortex arrangement in multilayer Ising superconductors.

</details>


### [113] [Sharp bounds on the Eliashberg critical temperature: a comparison principle, convexity in the spectrum, and the Kiessling-Altshuler-Yuzbashyan conjectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20496)
*Davis Rens*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文独立证明了Eliashberg理论中KAY关于临界温度Tc的上界与全耦合唯一性猜想，并给出Einstein模最大化、矩界、第三猜想证明及Lean 4形式化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无库仑赝势的声子介导超导Eliashberg理论中，Tc是正常态线性失稳点；KAY用Matsubara频率序列上紧算子最大特征值刻画Tc，证明强耦合唯一性并猜想Tc<C(λ⟨ω²⟩)^(1/2)，C≈0.1827且尖锐，此即Leavens 1977年部分基于数值提出的界。Kondratev未同行评审预印本给出证明；本文寻求独立证明并推广。

Method: 基于比较原理：在每个Matsubara转移上增强配对相互作用、且在最小转移严格增强时，最大特征值严格增大；该原理由Bergmann和Rainer一阶证明，也是Kondratev核心步骤。对任意有限二阶矩声子谱证明；固定λ和⟨ω²⟩时单个Einstein模最大化Tc；利用最大特征值对谱的凸性，给出由矩∫ω^n α²F dω（0≤n≤1）控制的尖锐Tc界；用非增向量前缀分解证明第三KAY猜想；有限维核心含该猜想在Lean 4中形式化，并用两个独立于比较原理的计算机辅助证明确认Einstein界。

Result: 证明Tc<C(λ⟨ω²⟩)^(1/2)（C≈0.1827尖锐）及全耦合唯一性；固定λ与⟨ω²⟩时Einstein模最大化Tc；得到包括Leavens零阶矩界Tc≤c0∫α²F dω（c0≈0.231，中等耦合最优）在内的矩界；所有界在带对称截断的库仑赝势下仍成立；证明第三KAY猜想；完成Lean 4形式化并由两个计算机辅助证明确认Einstein界。

Conclusion: 本文给出独立且适用范围广的证明，强化并扩展KAY/Leavens结果至库仑赝势、矩界和第三猜想，同时通过形式化验证有限维核心与计算机辅助证明提高可靠性。

Abstract: In Eliashberg theory of phonon-mediated superconductivity with vanishing Coulomb pseudopotential, the critical temperature $T_c$ marks the linear instability of the normal state. Kiessling, Altshuler and Yuzbashyan (KAY) characterized it through the top eigenvalue of a compact operator on Matsubara-frequency sequences. They proved that $T_c$ is unique at strong coupling and conjectured $T_c<C(\lambda\langle\omega^2\rangle)^{1/2}$ for every coupling $\lambda$, with $\langle\omega^2\rangle$ the second moment of the normalized phonon spectrum and $C\approx0.1827$ sharp, a bound Leavens stated in 1977 on partly numerical grounds. A preprint by Kondratev, not yet peer reviewed, gives a proof of this bound and of all-coupling uniqueness. We give an independent proof, for every spectrum with finite second moment, based on a comparison principle that is also Kondratev's central step and was proved to first order by Bergmann and Rainer: strengthening the pairing interaction at every Matsubara transfer, strictly at the smallest one, strictly raises the top eigenvalue. At fixed $\lambda$ and $\langle\omega^2\rangle$ a single Einstein mode maximizes $T_c$. Convexity of the top eigenvalue in the spectrum gives sharp bounds on $T_c$ by the moments $\int\omega^n\alpha^2F d\omega$, $0\le n\le1$, including Leavens's zeroth-moment bound $T_c\le c_0\int\alpha^2F d\omega$, $c_0\approx0.231$ numerically, optimal at moderate coupling. All bounds persist under a Coulomb pseudopotential with symmetric cutoff. A prefix decomposition of non-increasing vectors proves a third KAY conjecture, not treated in the preprint. The finite-dimensional core, including that conjecture, is formalized in Lean 4, and two computer-assisted proofs independent of the comparison principle confirm the Einstein bound.

</details>


### [114] [Inversion of magnetization anisotropy in low magnetic fields for textured Bi2223 superconductor due to magnetic responses from superconducting crystallites and intercrystalline boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20560)
*D. A. Balaev,S. V. Semenov,D. M. Gokhfeld,M. I. Petrov*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究了织构Bi2223高温超导体的磁各向异性，发现低场下磁滞回线分支差ΔM出现反常各向异性反转，并用晶间边界磁通俘获与晶粒片状屏蔽效应解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 织构高温超导体通常表现为H||NTP时ΔM更大，但作者关注小最大外加场下是否仍保持该规律，并揭示晶间边界子系统在低场磁滞中的主导作用。

Method: 对块体织构Bi2223进行不同取向（H||NTP与H⊥NTP）和不同最大外场Hmax下的磁化磁滞回线测量，提取上下分支差ΔM，并结合第一穿透场约30 Oe（T=78 K）分析低场磁滞来源。

Result: 在中高场（至1 kOe）ΔM(H||NTP)>ΔM(H⊥NTP)，符合织构超导体常规各向异性；但在T=78 K、Hmax至15 Oe时出现反常反转ΔM(H||NTP)<ΔM(H⊥NTP)。低场至约30 Oe的磁滞仅由晶间边界子系统俘获磁通决定；片状Bi2223晶粒在H||NTP时对内部外场产生部分屏蔽，使晶间边界有效场较小，因此需要更大外场才能使磁通穿透样品内部。

Conclusion: Bi2223织构超导体在小最大场下出现磁各向异性反转，源于晶间边界磁通俘获及片状晶粒对外场的取向相关屏蔽；该效应解释了H||NTP需要更高外场才发生磁通穿透的现象。

Abstract: A detailed study of the magnetic anisotropy of a bulk textured high-temperature superconductor Bi2223 has been carried out. In the range of moderate and high fields (up to 1 kOe), the difference {\Delta}M between the upper and lower branches of the magnetization hysteresis loop is larger when the external magnetic field H is parallel to the normal of the texturing plane (H||NTP) than when H is parallel to the texturing plane (H {\perp} NTP). This magnetic anisotropy is typical for such textured superconductors. However, at small values of the maximum applied field Hmax (up to 15 Oe at a temperature T = 78 K), an anomalous inversion of anisotropy has been found: {\Delta}M(H||NTP) < {\Delta}M(H {\perp} NTP). It has been established that in the field range up to ~30 Oe (the first penetration field into Bi2223 crystallites is about 30 Oe at T = 78 K), the magnetic hysteresis is determined only by the magnetic flux trapped in the intercrystallite boundary subsystem. The pronounced platelet shape of the Bi2223 crystallites leads to partial screening of the external field inside the sample when the field is oriented parallel to the crystallite c-axis. The observed inverse anisotropy {\Delta}M(H||NTP) < {\Delta}M(H {\perp} NTP) has been explained by the fact that the effective magnetic field into the intercrystallite boundaries is smaller for the H||NTP orientation than for H {\perp} NTP. Therefore, when the field is oriented H||NTP, a larger external field is required for magnetic flux to penetrate into the sample inner than for the H {\perp} NTP orientation.

</details>


### [115] [Failure of Mean-Field Critical Scaling and Two Distinct Dissipation Regimes in La$_2$SmNi$_2$O$_{7-\delta}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21536)
*Yongze Ye,Feiyu Li,Di Peng,Junjie Zhang,Hai-Hu Wen*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 对加压下La2SmNi2O7−δ单晶（Tc onset=92.5 K）的临界标度与涡旋动力学分析表明，Ullah–Dorsey框架下2D和3D标度均不能在Tc^MF附近良好坍缩；电阻率对数两步下降对应两个耗散区，低温符合TAFF模型，高温偏离超导涨落相关的涡旋动力学，指向多带复合序参量及轨道依赖涡旋核心演化的重要作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高压镍酸盐超导为研究超导机制提供新平台，尤其La2−xSm1+xNi2O7−δ体系；需要厘清其临界涨落和涡旋耗散行为，并检验常规临界标度是否适用于双层镍酸盐的多带复合序参量。

Method: 对Tc onset=92.5 K的La2SmNi2O7−δ单晶进行系统临界标度和涡旋动力学分析；在Ullah–Dorsey框架下检验二维和三维标度坍缩；分析对数电阻率的两步下降，并用热助磁通流（TAFF）模型描述低温耗散区，比较高温度区与超导涨落相关涡旋动力学。

Result: 二维和三维标度均不能在Tc^MF附近给出良好坍缩；对数电阻率呈现两步下降，揭示两个不同耗散区。低温区可由TAFF模型很好描述，而高温区不能用涉及超导涨落的涡旋动力学解释；两区交叉可能与轨道依赖的涡旋核心演化有关。

Conclusion: 临界标度失效和涡旋耗散行为表明复合序参量在镍酸盐中起重要作用：配对主要由dz2轨道的反铁磁交换驱动，而超流刚度主要由dx2−y2轨道决定；该多带复合序参量显著影响临界涨落和涡旋运动耗散。

Abstract: Recently, superconductivity with an onset transition temperature ($T_{\mathrm{c}}^{\mathrm{onset}}$) up to 96 K has been observed in the $\mathrm{La}_{2-x}\mathrm{Sm}_{1+x}\mathrm{Ni}_2\mathrm{O}_{7-\delta}$ system under pressure, providing an attractive platform for investigating superconducting mechanisms in nickelates. Here, we systematically analyzed the critical scaling and vortex dynamics for the $\mathrm{La}_2\mathrm{Sm}\mathrm{Ni}_2\mathrm{O}_{7-\delta}$ single crystal with $T_{\mathrm{c}}^{\mathrm{onset}}=92.5$ K under pressure. Within the Ullah--Dorsey framework, neither the two-dimensional nor three-dimensional scaling provides a good collapse near $T_{\mathrm{c}}^{\mathrm{MF}}$. This failure may be associated with the multiband composite order parameter of bilayer nickelates, in which pairing is mainly driven by antiferromagnetic exchange in the $d_{z^2}$ orbital, whereas the superfluid stiffness is mainly governed by the $d_{x^2-y^2}$ orbital. The two-step decrease in the logarithmic resistivity reveals two distinct dissipative regimes. The low-temperature regime is well described by the thermally assisted flux flow (TAFF) model, whereas the high-temperature regime cannot be explained by the vortex dynamics involved with the superconducting fluctuation. The crossover between the two regimes may be associated with the orbital-dependent evolution of vortex cores. These results suggest the significant role of the composite order parameter in critical fluctuations and vortex-motion dissipation in nickelates.

</details>
