### [116] [MagBerry: A Julia package for modeling magnon and phonon topology and transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18731)
*Xiaonong Shen,Wei Ren*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: MagBerry 是一个 Julia 软件包，用于统一研究磁振子、声子及其杂化激发的拓扑性质与输运响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将磁振子与声子的拓扑、角动量及热输运分开处理；需要一个统一框架，在共同玻色子理论下计算杂化激发的能谱、拓扑不变量、角动量分辨响应和非线性输运。

Method: 该包将 Sunny.jl 的磁模型与线性自旋波哈密顿量同原生谐波声子及磁振子—声子耦合模块结合，采用玻色子 Bogoliubov–de Gennes 表述；计算体态与边界谱、Berry 曲率、Chern 数及各模式的 spin/phonon 角动量，并用弛豫时间 Boltzmann 理论计算热霍尔、spin/orbital Nernst、声子角动量 Hall 及扩展 Berry 曲率偶极贡献的非线性磁振子热霍尔输运。

Result: 示例复现了拓扑磁振子边界谱，并展示了声子几何与非线性输运；在交错磁杂化模型中，作者扩展了已发表的 d 波声子角动量织构研究，计算其 Boltzmann 输运系数，得到纵向与横向角动量电流随温度和温度梯度方向变化的张量描述。

Conclusion: MagBerry 提供了研究磁振子、声子及杂化激发拓扑与热/角动量输运的统一可扩展计算工具。

Abstract: We present MagBerry, a Julia package for studying the topology and transport of magnons, phonons, and their hybrid excitations. It combines magnetic models and linear spin-wave Hamiltonians from Sunny.jl with native modules for harmonic phonons and magnon--phonon coupling. These excitations are treated within a common bosonic Bogoliubov--de Gennes formulation. The package calculates bulk and boundary spectra, Berry curvature, and Chern numbers, and resolves the spin and phonon angular momentum carried by each mode. Transport calculations include thermal Hall, spin and orbital Nernst, and phonon angular momentum Hall responses. The package also evaluates the extended Berry curvature dipole contribution to nonlinear magnon thermal Hall transport. Relaxation-time Boltzmann theory gives heat and angular momentum currents. Examples based on published models reproduce topological magnon boundary spectra and illustrate phonon geometry and nonlinear transport. For an altermagnetic hybrid model, we extend a published study of the d-wave phonon angular momentum texture by calculating its Boltzmann transport coefficients. The resulting tensor describes how the longitudinal and transverse angular momentum currents vary with temperature and the direction of the temperature gradient.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.str-el'></div>

# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Gapless Nature of Spin Liquid in the $S=1/2$ Kagome Heisenberg Antiferromagnet with Neural-Network Quantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18840)
*Andreas Raikos,Alessandro Sinibaldi,Riccardo Rende,Giuseppe Carleo,Federico Becca,Luciano Loris Viteritti*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文用不预设自旋液体结构的神经网络量子态直接计算自旋-1/2 kagome海森堡反铁磁体的三重态能隙，发现磁序参量和三重态能隙在热力学极限下均趋于零，支持无能隙U(1) Dirac自旋液体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋-1/2 kagome海森堡反铁磁体究竟具有带隙的Z2自旋液体还是无能隙的U(1) Dirac自旋液体，是阻挫磁性中的核心未解问题；需要不依赖预设自旋液体结构的直接数值证据。

Method: 采用时间反演对称的基于Transformer的神经网络量子态拟设，在周期性边界条件、最大768格点的二维kagome团簇上进行变分计算，获得最先进的变分能量，并对磁序参量和三重态激发能隙做有限尺寸标度。

Result: 热力学极限下每格点能量估计为-0.43873(12)；磁序参量和三重态激发能隙的有限尺寸标度均外推为零，直接表明基态无能隙，并与U(1) Dirac自旋液体一致。

Conclusion: 结果提供了kagome海森堡反铁磁体基态为无能隙自旋液体的独立直接证据，并说明神经网络量子态可有效解决传统数值方法难以处理的阻挫量子磁体基态性质问题。

Abstract: Whether the spin-$1/2$ kagome Heisenberg antiferromagnet hosts a gapped $\mathbb{Z}_2$ or a gapless $U(1)$ Dirac spin liquid remains a central open question in frustrated magnetism. Here, we address this question by directly computing the triplet gap with a neural-network quantum state that does not presuppose any specific spin-liquid structure. Applying a time-reversal symmetric transformer-based ansatz to clusters of up to $768$ sites, we obtain state-of-the-art variational energies on two-dimensional lattices with periodic boundary conditions and estimate an energy per site of $-0.43873(12)$ in the thermodynamic limit. Finite-size scaling of the magnetic order parameter and triplet excitation gap shows that both extrapolate to zero in the thermodynamic limit. Together, these results provide independent and direct evidence of a gapless spin-liquid ground state, consistent with the $U(1)$ Dirac spin liquid. More broadly, they demonstrate that neural-network quantum states can resolve ground-state properties of frustrated quantum magnets that remain challenging for conventional numerical methods.

</details>


### [118] [Chiral Majorana Charge Kondo Criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19595)
*Xiao Xiao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出手性Majorana电荷Kondo（CMCK）临界点作为区分手性Majorana边缘模的多体判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手性Majorana边缘模的输运信号可由常规机制产生，导致识别不明确，因此需要更具区分度的探测方案。

Method: 考虑承载单一手性Majorana边缘模的带电岛：Majorana自共轭锁定正常隧穿与反常隧穿振幅，使每个物理电极自然提供两个Kondo屏蔽通道；普通亚带隙态会破坏该锁定并趋向常规电荷Kondo（CK）。分析两端及N端几何下的临界电导、领头温度修正、残余熵以及栅压失谐和接触不对称下的交叉指数。

Result: 两端情形下CMCK与常规CK共享临界电导G*=e^2/2h，但领头电导修正分别按T^{1/3}和T标度；残余熵及交叉指数也不同。N端时CMCK电导修正按T^{1/(N+1)}标度，且N≥3时临界电导与同端数常规CK不同。

Conclusion: 这些普适特征可用现有电荷Kondo测量技术检验，从而为手性Majorana边缘模提供更明确的实验判据。

Abstract: Unambiguous identification of chiral Majorana edge modes remains challenging because the transport signatures proposed for them can also arise from conventional mechanisms. Here we identify the chiral Majorana charge Kondo (CMCK) critical point as a more discriminating many-body route to detect chiral Majorana edge modes. This criticality arises in a charged island hosting a single chiral Majorana edge mode, where Majorana self-conjugacy locks normal and anomalous tunneling amplitudes, so that each physical lead provides two Kondo screening channels without fine tuning. Ordinary subgap states generically break this locking and drive the system toward conventional charge Kondo (CK) behavior. In the simplest two-terminal geometry, CMCK and conventional CK share the critical conductance $G^*=e^2/2h$, but their leading conductance corrections scale as $T^{1/3}$ and $T$, respectively. Their residual entropies also differ, as do the crossover exponents under gate detuning and contact asymmetry. For $N$ leads, CMCK conductance corrections scale as $T^{1/(N+1)}$, and for $N\geq3$ even the critical conductance differs from that of conventional CK with the same number of leads. These universal signatures are testable using established charge-Kondo measurement techniques.

</details>


### [119] [Angular dependence of the low-temperature skyrmion state in Cu$_2$OSeO$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19993)
*Christian Oberleitner,Andreas Bauer,Lukas Heindl,Lin Chen,Aisha Aqeel,Christian H. Back,Hans Huebl,Christian Pfleiderer*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在Cu2OSeO3中，沿〈100〉方向的低温斯格明子态（LTS）和亚稳倾斜锥态随磁场方向朝〈110〉旋转而逐渐消失：LTS仅在〈100〉附近约20°内可观测，倾斜锥态可延续至约40°。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已知沿〈100〉方向时，磁晶各向异性可稳定LTS和倾斜锥态，但缺乏对磁场方向旋转时这些态如何演化及其相互关系的系统研究。

Method: 对同一长方体样品在5 K下结合磁化强度测量、交流磁化率测量和宽带微波谱；利用微波谱中的GHz激发模式（尤其斯格明子的逆时针旋进模式）作为磁态特征和体积分数的定性指标。

Result: 随磁场从〈100〉经〈111〉转向〈110〉，LTS特征减弱且仅在距〈100〉约20°内出现，其斯格明子解绕场值基本不变；倾斜锥态特征可保持到约40°，超过后恢复常规的螺旋—锥—场极化态序列。

Conclusion: 倾斜锥态虽是场扫掠下LTS形核的前提条件，但并非其形成的充分条件；LTS的稳定还受磁场方向及磁晶各向异性等其他因素限制。

Abstract: In the cubic chiral magnet Cu$_2$OSeO$_3$, a low-temperature skyrmion state (LTS) and a metastable tilted conical state emerge for magnetic field along $\langle 100\rangle$, both stabilized by magnetocrystalline anisotropies. We report how these states evolve when the magnetic field is rotated from $\langle 100\rangle$ via $\langle 111\rangle$ to $\langle 110\rangle$. Measurements of the magnetization and the AC susceptibility are combined with broadband microwave spectroscopy on the same cuboid sample at 5\,K. In the microwave spectra, the characteristic gigahertz excitations, notably the counterclockwise gyration mode of the skyrmions, provide a direct indicator of the character of the magnetic states and a qualitative measure of their volume fractions. Both techniques consistently show that signatures of the LTS decrease in strength under rotation and are observed only for field directions within ${\sim}20^{\circ}$ of $\langle 100\rangle$. In this range, the field values at which the skyrmions unwind remain essentially unchanged. In contrast, signatures of the tilted conical state persist up to ${\sim}40^{\circ}$, beyond which the conventional sequence of helical, conical, and field-polarized states is recovered. Thus, the tilted conical state, while being a prerequisite for the nucleation of the LTS under field sweeps, is not sufficient for its formation.

</details>


### [120] [Two channels of magnetic renormalization in the terahertz lattice response of FePS$_{3}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20102)
*Eduardo D. Stefanato,Nicolas M. Kawahala,Ana Carolina F. de Brito,Jan Plutnar,Zdenek Sofer,Bernardo R. A. Neves,Ingrid D. Barcelos,Felix G. G. Hernandez*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文用太赫兹时域光谱研究FePS3的层间剪切与磁性耦合，发现自旋-剪切耦合各向异性，并在奈尔温度以上通过短程自旋关联持续存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有超快实验表明FePS3层间剪切与磁序强耦合，但磁序如何重整化剪切刚度和阻尼尚不清楚。

Method: 测量FePS3在1.6–300 K、垂直层方向最高7 T磁场下的各向异性电导率，采用太赫兹时域光谱识别0.6–0.8 THz和1.3–2.4 THz吸收带。

Result: 低频带对应层间剪切共振；磁序使一个面内分量变硬、另一个变软，并在奈尔温度处反转劈裂。频率移动限于有序相，而线宽和多数振子强度遵循持续至转变温度以上的短程自旋关联。磁场主要作用于某一取向的高频共振，约3.2 T处软化加速且线宽出现尖点状极小，符合场致序参量的临界慢化。

Conclusion: 研究给出了自旋-剪切耦合的平衡图像：该耦合具有各向异性，并可通过短程自旋关联在奈尔温度以上持续存在。

Abstract: Interlayer vibrations probe how magnetic van der Waals layers are stacked and coupled. In the zigzag antiferromagnet FePS$_3$, ultrafast experiments show that the interlayer shear couples strongly to the magnetic order, but not how magnetism renormalizes the shear stiffness and damping. Here, using terahertz time-domain spectroscopy, we measure the anisotropic conductivity of FePS$_3$ from 1.6 to 300 K and in fields up to 7 T normal to the layers. We observe absorption bands at 0.6-0.8 and 1.3-2.4 THz that persist into the paramagnetic phase. The lower one is consistent with an interlayer shear resonance; the magnetic order stiffens one of its in-plane components and softens the other, reversing their splitting at the N\'eel temperature. We identify two channels of magnetic renormalization: frequency shifts are confined to the ordered phase, whereas the linewidths of this resonance and most oscillator strengths follow short-range spin correlations persisting well above the transition. The field acts mainly on the higher resonance in one orientation: near 3.2 T, its softening accelerates and its linewidth has a cusp-like minimum, consistent with critical slowing down of a field-induced order parameter. These results give an equilibrium view of spin-shear coupling, which is anisotropic and persists above the N\'eel temperature through short-range correlations.

</details>


### [121] [Measurement of Magnonic Quantum Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18897)
*S. Kim,M. Stone,S. E. Nagler,A. D. Christianson,Jiabin Yu,S. Okamoto,H. Miao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文提出并验证了利用非弹性中子散射（INS）实验刻画磁振子量子几何的方案，可从能量-动量依赖的INS强度中提取量子几何张量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子几何编码于波函数而非能谱，直接实验表征困难，尤其对量子材料中的准粒子而言，其波函数通常只能由低能有效模型推断。

Method: 建立磁振子量子几何的实验框架：证明INS截面可测量铁磁体自旋波函数的几何参数；通过追踪能量与动量依赖的INS强度提取量子几何张量，且无需详细掌握微观自旋-自旋相互作用。

Result: 实现了对磁振子量子几何的实验表征，表明磁振子是实验探测量子几何的理想平台。

Conclusion: 该工作为磁振子量子几何的实验研究开辟新途径，并有望推动几何驱动的磁振子输运与磁性材料中量子Fisher信息等方向的发展。

Abstract: Quantum geometry plays a fundamental role in macroscopic quantum phenomena, including quantum Hall effects and flat-band superconductivity. However, since quantum geometry is encoded in wavefunctions rather than energy spectra, direct experimental characterization remains a formidable challenge. This challenge is particularly acute for quasiparticles in quantum materials, whose wavefunctions are generally inferred from effective low-energy models rather than measured directly. Here, we establish an experimental framework for characterizing the quantum geometry of magnons, a ubiquitous Bosonic quasiparticle of magnetically ordered states. We show that the inelastic neutron scattering (INS) cross-section measures geometric parameters of the spinor wavefunctions of ferromagnets. By tracking the energy- and momentum-dependent INS intensity, we extract the quantum geometric tensor without requiring detailed knowledge of microscopic spin-spin interactions. Our results establish magnons as an ideal arena for experimentally probing quantum geometry and open new avenues for exploring geometry-driven magnon transport and quantum Fisher information in magnetic materials.

</details>


### [122] [From RuP$_3$SiO$_{11}$ to Ideal Geometry: Parallel-Face Octahedra for Kitaev-Dominated Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18920)
*Do Hoon Kiem,Satoshi Okamoto,Tom Berlijn*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过第一性原理计算评估 RuP3SiO11 作为 Kitaev 自旋液体候选材料的磁性相互作用，并扩展到 MT3QO11 家族，提出平行面八面体构型是实现 Kitaev 主导区域的新结构途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判断 RuP3SiO11 距离 Kitaev 极限有多近，并探索该材料家族中磁性相互作用的可调性及结构设计原则。

Method: 对 RuP3SiO11 进行 ab initio 磁性相互作用计算，并比较八个 MT3QO11（M=Ru,Os; T=P,As; Q=Si,Ge）替代类似物；同时对比理想化几何构型。

Result: Kitaev 与 off-diagonal 交换与实验吻合较好，但 Heisenberg 交换为铁磁而非实验拟合的反铁磁；发现此前分析未包含的显著面外反铁磁交换；八个类似物的磁性相互作用变化显著；平行面八面体构型导致 Kitaev 主导区域。

Conclusion: MT3QO11 材料家族具有可调的磁性相互作用，平行面八面体排列提供了一条通往 Kitaev 极限的新结构路线。

Abstract: RuP$_3$SiO$_{11}$ has recently been identified as a Kitaev spin liquid candidate via neutron scattering. To assess its proximity to the Kitaev limit, we perform \textit{ab initio} calculations of magnetic interactions, finding good agreement for the Kitaev and off-diagonal exchanges, while the Heisenberg exchange is ferromagnetic rather than the experimentally fitted antiferromagnetic one. We also identify a sizable antiferromagnetic out-of-plane exchange not captured in previous analyses. Extending the study to eight substitutional analogs in the $MT_3Q$O$_{11}$ family ($M$ = Ru, Os; $T$ = P, As; $Q$= Si, Ge), we observe strong variations in magnetic interactions, highlighting the tunability of this materials class. Finally, inspired by RuP$_3$SiO$_{11}$, which exhibits a near parallel-face octahedral arrangement, we compare idealized geometries and show that the parallel-face configuration yields a Kitaev-dominated regime, suggesting a new structural route to the Kitaev limit.

</details>


### [123] [Spectroscopic evidence for unconventional surface magnetism in the Kondo insulator FeSb$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19804)
*Ke-Jun Xu,Erin E. Fleck Jonathan A. Sobota,Nhat Huy M. Tran,Shuyi Li,Youzhe Chen,Soonsang Huh,Takeshi Kondo,Makoto Hashimoto,Dong-Hui Lu,Zhi-Xun Shen,Dung-Hai Lee,Chunjing Jia,Robert J. Birgeneau*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用ARPES研究Kondo绝缘体FeSb2(010)表面，发现表面态存在各向异性劈裂，可能源于Kondo击穿后涌现的表面交替磁序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FeSb2体态因Kondo屏蔽呈非磁性，但表面 itinerant 电子密度降低可能发生Kondo击穿并恢复局域磁矩；且无Kondo屏蔽时体态被预测具有d波交替磁性，因此值得探究表面是否出现磁序。

Method: 对FeSb2(010)表面进行角分辨光电子能谱测量，分析表面态劈裂的动量各向异性，并通过变温和Te掺杂检验其来源。

Result: 表面态劈裂在k_z方向最大、k_x方向最小；随温度升高或Te掺杂而明显受抑，表明其更可能来自磁序而非Rashba效应。结合体态近d波交替磁性与表面晶体对称性，推测观测结果与涌现的表面交替磁序一致。

Conclusion: 该工作提出FeSb2表面可能存在由Kondo击穿诱导的二维交替磁序，为在绝缘非磁性体衬底上实现非常规二维磁性及多功能应用提供候选平台。

Abstract: Kondo insulators have a non-magnetic bulk due to many-body screening of local moment by conduction electrons. However, the surface of these systems has reduced density of itinerant electrons and may experience a Kondo breakdown, leading to recovery of non-zero local moments and possible magnetic order. Here we use angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy to investigate the (010) surface of a $d$-$p$ electron Kondo insulator FeSb$_2$, which is predicted to host a bulk $d$-wave altermagnetic state in the absence of Kondo screening. We find anisotropic splitting on the surface states with a maximum in the $k_z$ direction and a minimum in the $k_x$ direction. The splitting is apparently suppressed with increasing temperature or with Te doping, suggesting that the splitting arises from a magnetic order rather than Rashba effects. Considering the proximity of the bulk to a $d$-wave altermagnetic state and considering the symmetry of the crystal on the surface, we postulate that our observations may be consistent with an emergent surface altermagnetic order. Such an unconventional 2-dimensional magnetic order naturally hosted on an insulating, non-magnet bulk substrate may offer a promising platform for versatile applications.

</details>


### [124] [Unconventional Magneto-Optical Kerr Effect beyond Magnetization Scaling Driven by Pulse-Field-Induced Metamagnetism in Fe$_2$Mo$_3$O$_8$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20697)
*Soichiro Yamane,Sota Nakamura,Shuya Suzuki,Atsutoshi Ikeda,Masaki Kondo,Takashi Kurumaji,Kentaro Ino,Keisuke Matsuura,Fumitaka Kagawa,Yoshinori Tokura,Masashi Tokunaga,Shingo Yonezawa*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文利用脉冲强磁场磁光克尔效应（MOKE）在最高60 T、低至1.4 K条件下研究Fe2Mo3O8中磁场诱导的反铁磁—亚铁磁相变，发现跨越相变时出现巨大且非单调的瞬态MOKE异常，其不随体磁化强度标度，揭示出源于AFM-FRM相界面的非常规非对角光学响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一阶磁相变通常伴随亚稳态和相共存，但这些非平衡过程在体测量中常被平均掩盖；需要局域、非对角光学探针来揭示隐藏的局域磁动力学和相界面效应。

Method: 采用脉冲强磁场MOKE测量，将测量范围扩展至60 T并降至1.4 K，研究极性绝缘体Fe2Mo3O8中磁场诱导的AFM-FRM转变，并比较MOKE响应与体磁化强度、温度、磁场历史和脉冲协议的关系。

Result: 跨越metamagnetic相变时MOKE响应表现出意外且巨大的非单调瞬态行为，且与体磁化强度不呈标度关系，类似于非常规反常霍尔效应的MOKE对应物；低温下MOKE细节高度可重复，而较高温度下强烈依赖磁场历史和脉冲协议，表明相变驱动机制发生变化；异常 excess MOKE 被认为来自实空间中的AFM-FRM相界面。

Conclusion: 研究将MOKE拓展至60 T区间，确立脉冲场磁光测量作为一种可探测瞬态和隐藏场诱导现象的新探针，并揭示竞争磁序边界处一类新的非对角光学响应。

Abstract: First-order phase transitions often proceed through metastable states and complicated phase coexistence, yet such nonequilibrium processes are obscured in bulk measurements. The magneto-optical Kerr effect (MOKE) probes the local off-diagonal optical response, offering access to local magnetic dynamics beyond bulk measurements. Here, we investigate the magnetic-field-induced antiferromagnetic (AFM)-ferrimagnetic (FRM) transition in the polar insulator Fe$_2$Mo$_3$O$_8$ using pulsed-field MOKE measurements extended for the first time up to 60 T and down to 1.4 K. The MOKE response exhibits unexpected and huge nonmonotonic transient behavior across the metamagnetic phase transition. The MOKE behavior does not show any scaling with the bulk magnetization, pointing to an MOKE analogue of the unconventional anomalous Hall effect. This is a new class of off-diagonal response emerging exclusively at the boundary between competing magnetic orders. At low temperatures, MOKE profiles are highly reproducible in detail, whereas at elevated temperatures they depend strongly on magnetic-field history and pulse protocol, suggesting changes in the driving mechanism of the phase transition. We infer that the excess MOKE anomaly originates from AFM-FRM phase interfaces in real space. Our results, extending MOKE into the 60 T regime, establish pulsed-field magneto-optics as a novel probe capable of uncovering transient and hidden field-induced phenomena through unconventional off-diagonal optical responses.

</details>


### [125] [Self-attention is a Dyson equation for many-body machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21398)
*Jarvist Moore Frost*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 论文解释了一个经验矛盾：对称 sum-pooling（DeepSets）能以 sub-meV 精度预测基态势能，却在相关波函数和激发态上失败。作者指出 DeepSets 等价于 Hartree 平均场，会抹去非局域两体关联；而 self-attention 等价于实空间 Dyson 迭代，其 softmax 核充当两体自能，因此能恢复相关能学和分形节点结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是回答：为何 DeepSets 的对称 sum-pooling 对近邻基态能量高度有效，却不能处理相关波函数与激发态？论文试图给出这一适用边界的物理图像，并解释 self-attention 在量子多体建模中为何有效。

Method: 作者将 DeepSets 解释为平均场 Hartree 模型：其 O(1/N) 非对角 Jacobian 足以描述近邻基态能量，但会擦除非局域两体关联。进一步将 self-attention 视为实空间 Dyson 迭代，其中 Boltzmann/softmax 核对应两体自能；并借助物理哈密顿量的成对作用、Löwdin 的 2-RDM 定理和 Galitskii--Migdal 关系，论证迭代两体传播子可捕捉相关物质的完整能量学。最后用分形 backflow 行列式拟合比较两种架构对节点面的表示。

Result: 在分形 backflow 行列式拟合中，DeepSets 将节点面模糊为平均场包络，而 self-attention 能恢复分形吸引子及其非局域节点结构。这表明 sum-pooling 的平均场性质限制了其对相关波函数和激发态的表达能力，而两体注意力机制可保留关键的两体关联。

Conclusion: self-attention 的“不合理有效性”并非偶然：在由成对相互作用构成的世界中，两体注意力机制足以捕捉相关物质的能量学；因此可概括为“two-body attention is all you need”。

Abstract: Why does symmetric sum-pooling (DeepSets) predict ground-state potentials to sub-meV accuracy, yet fail for correlated wavefunctions and excited states? Sum-pooling is a mean-field (Hartree) model: its $O(1/N)$ off-diagonal Jacobian suffices for nearsighted ground-state energies, but erases nonlocal two-body correlations. We show that self-attention is a real-space Dyson iteration whose Boltzmann (softmax) kernel acts as a two-body self-energy. Because physical Hamiltonians act pairwise, L\"owdin's 2-RDM theorem and the Galitskii--Migdal relation guarantee that iterating a two-body propagator captures the full energetics of correlated matter. When fitting a fractal backflow determinant, DeepSets blurs the nodal surface onto a mean-field envelope, whereas self-attention recovers the fractal attractor. Seen through this lens, the unreasonable effectiveness of attention is not an accident; in a universe built from pairwise interactions, two-body attention is all you need.

</details>


### [126] [Hyperkagome antiferromagnetism in the iron garnet Almandine Fe$_3$Al$_2$Si$_3$O$_{12}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21610)
*Minnong Zhang,Harald O. Jeschke,Daniel Guterding*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文将铁铝榴石 almandine（Fe3Al2Si3O12）确定为自旋-2 超烧绿石反铁磁体，并通过第一性原理与蒙特卡洛给出与比热实验一致的约 9.8 K 反铁磁转变及磁环八极序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超烧绿石晶格可提供三维强磁阻挫，但具有明确微观哈密顿量且实验可实现的材料很少；石榴石中磁性离子可形成两套互穿超烧绿石晶格，是研究自旋玻璃、自旋液体和隐藏序的重要平台。

Method: 用密度泛函理论能量映射构建高自旋 Fe2+ 矩的 Heisenberg 哈密顿量，得到主导最近邻反铁磁交换及显著长程相互作用；结合经典蒙特卡洛与量子-经典自旋长度重整，并计算中子衍射振幅及半经典静态/动态结构因子。

Result: 模拟得到非共线反铁磁序，转变温度 TN≈9.8 K，与比热实验吻合；该磁态可由磁环八极序自然描述，并与 Gd3Ga5O12 的已知结果相联系；同时给出可供中子散射检验的定量预测。

Conclusion: Almandine 被确立为石榴石家族中化学可及的超烧绿石阻挫磁性实现，为后续中子散射实验和阻挫磁体研究提供明确候选与理论预测。

Abstract: Antiferromagnets on the hyperkagome lattice provide a three-dimensional route to strong magnetic frustration, but experimentally accessible realizations with well-established microscopic Hamiltonians remain scarce. Garnets are a broad family of materials in which magnetic ions can form two interpenetrating hyperkagome lattices, giving rise to spin glass, spin-liquid and hidden-order physics. Here we identify the iron aluminosilicate garnet almandine, Fe$_3$Al$_2$Si$_3$O$_{12}$, as a spin-$2$ hyperkagome antiferromagnet. Using density functional theory energy mapping, we determine a Heisenberg Hamiltonian for the high-spin Fe$^{2+}$ moments and find dominant nearest-neighbor antiferromagnetic exchange, as well as significant longer-range interactions. Classical Monte Carlo simulations of this Hamiltonian, combined with quantum-to-classical rescaling of the spin length, show a transition to antiferromagnetic order at $T_{\rm N}\approx9.8$\~K, in excellent agreement with specific heat experiments. The resulting non-collinear magnetic state is naturally described by magnetic toroidal octupolar order, which we connect to known results for the highly frustrated hyperkagome antiferromagnet gadolinium gallium garnet, Gd$_3$Ga$_5$O$_{12}$. We also calculate magnetic neutron diffraction amplitudes as well as static and dynamic structure factors using semiclassical dynamics, providing quantitative predictions for future neutron scattering experiments. Our results establish almandine as a chemically accessible realization of frustrated hyperkagome magnetism in the garnet family.

</details>


### [127] [Quantum Cooling by Classical Shadows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18834)
*Xuepeng Wang,Debanjan Chowdhury*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: Shadow chain 表明，单次随机测量快照即可在平均意义上驱动量子多体系统沿虚时冷却方向演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚时演化可冷却至多体哈密顿量基态，但其非幺正性无法直接在量子计算机上实现；现有方法需借助多拷贝参数估计或递归编译等外部结构。

Method: 该算法用随机测量将量子态塌缩为经典记录（shadow），并据此确定下一步幺正更新；跨步快照形成辅助场路径积分的量子可执行采样轨迹，利用 Born 规则的内禀随机性自采样，无需外部接受-拒绝步骤。

Result: 单次随机快照足以在平均意义上沿冷却方向引导状态；轨迹间涨落随系统尺寸指数抑制；算法无拟设，连续极限外推下精确，指数成本仅随系统尺寸而非温度增长。

Conclusion: Shadow chain 提供了一种无需外部编译或接受-拒绝、由测量随机性驱动的量子可执行虚时冷却方案，其温度相关成本不再指数增长。

Abstract: Cooling to ground states of many-body Hamiltonians by imaginary-time evolution is central to quantum simulation, but its non-unitary aspect prevents direct implementation on a quantum computer. Existing routes restore it by importing structure from outside the intrinsic quantum dynamics, e.g. by parameter estimation on many copies or recursive compilation. Here, we show that a single randomized snapshot of the quantum many-body system is sufficient to steer the state along the cooling direction on average. Our algorithm, shadow chain, uses randomized measurements to collapse the state to its classical record, which fixes the next unitary update. Snapshots across steps form a trajectory that is a quantum-executable sample of an auxiliary-field path integral; fluctuations among trajectories are exponentially suppressed in system size. With the randomness supplied intrinsically by the Born measurement rule, the trajectories sample themselves rather than going through external accept-reject steps. The resulting algorithm is ansatz-free and exact upon extrapolation to the continuous limit, with the exponential cost confined only to system size rather than temperature.

</details>


### [128] [Finite-time quenches across first-, second-, and infinite-order transitions in XXZ spin chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19022)
*Luiz Felipe Santos Dittberner,Luigi Berber,Maria C. O. Aguiar,Krissia Zawadzki,Helena Bragan\c{c}a*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究了量子淬火驱动下小尺寸XXZ自旋链中平衡量子相变如何在非平衡动力学中显现，重点关注相变阶数、对称性和 ramp 时长对瞬时本征基分解的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子模拟器可在有限小系统中实现淬火动力学，但不同阶数平衡相变在非平衡过程中的签名尚需系统理解，尤其是第一阶、第二阶和BKT相变在小系统中的差异。

Method: 对自旋-1/2和自旋-1 XXZ链的各向异性进行线性 ramp，遍历第一阶、第二阶Haldane-Néel和无限阶BKT相变；选取当前量子模拟器可达到的系统尺寸，分析时间演化态在瞬时本征基中的分解，并考察相变阶数、对称性和 ramp 时长的作用。

Result: 相变阶数决定动力学：跨BKT相变时，中等慢速 ramp 已使系统接近基态并呈绝热演化；跨第二阶Haldane-Néel相变需约三倍更慢的 ramp 才能达到类似区域。跨第一阶相变时，由于动力学保持初始磁化，形成铁磁畴；慢速 ramp 产生单个畴壁，快速 ramp 产生多个畴壁。相变后不久，态表现为畴壁态之间的相干拍频，可由新提出的 quantum-state texture 描述。

Conclusion: 有限小系统中淬火动力学的特征强烈依赖平衡相变阶数和对称性；BKT、第二阶与第一阶相变表现出不同的绝热需求和畴结构动力学，畴壁态相干拍频提供了第一阶相变的非平衡签名。

Abstract: We investigate how equilibrium quantum phase transitions manifest themselves in small systems driven out of equilibrium by quantum quenches. We consider linear ramps of the anisotropy in spin-1/2 and spin-1 XXZ chains, which host transitions of first, second, and infinite (Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless, BKT) order. We consider system sizes available in current quantum simulators and investigate how the order of the transition, the symmetries, and the ramp duration affect the decomposition of the time-evolved state into the instantaneous eigenbasis. The order of the transition determines the dynamics: across the BKT transition, moderately slow ramps already keep the system close to its ground state, corresponding to an adiabatic evolution, whereas for the second-order Haldane-N\'eel transition a ramp roughly three times slower is required to reach the same regime. Across the first-order transition, since the dynamics conserves the magnetization of the initial state, we observe the formation of ferromagnetic domains, separated by a single domain wall for slow ramps and by multiple walls for faster ones. Interestingly, shortly after the transition, the state evolves as a coherent beating between domain-wall states, a behavior captured by the recently proposed quantum-state texture.

</details>


### [129] [Quasi-particle sector hybridization and the onset of chaos in spin ladders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19053)
*Arnav Pushkar,Abhik Kumar Saha*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文研究自旋-1/2 XXZ梯形模型中三连子（triplon）准粒子数对量子混沌出现的影响，发现谱混沌可在三连子数扇区尚未完全杂化时出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互多体系统中，准粒子数如何影响量子混沌的出现是基础问题；强rung极限下本征态可近似按triplon数扇区组织，因此适合检验扇区杂化与谱统计之间的关系。

Method: 在自旋-1/2 XXZ梯形模型中，随leg耦合增强，结合能级谱统计、基于triplon的可观测量以及von Neumann纠缠熵，刻画triplon数扇区的微观杂化及向GOE统计的过渡。

Result: 向高斯正交系综（GOE）统计的交叉随系统尺寸增大移至更弱耦合；而与triplon扇区杂化相关的特征交叉尺度对尺寸依赖较弱。此外，即使强rung结构残余仍存在，本征态间涨落仍表现出与ETH一致的标度行为。

Conclusion: 谱混沌并不必然要求triplon数扇区完全杂化；在扇区结构部分保留时，本征态统计仍可呈现与ETH一致的混沌特征。

Abstract: How quasiparticle numbers influence the emergence of quantum chaos is a fundamental question in interacting many-body systems. We investigate this interplay in a spin-$1/2$ XXZ ladder, where the strong-rung regime supports an approximate organization of many-body eigenstates into quasi-particle(triplon) number sectors. Using spectral statistics, triplon-based observables, and von Neumann entanglement entropy, we characterize the microscopic hybridization of triplon-number sectors as the leg coupling increases. We find that the crossover toward Gaussian orthogonal ensemble statistics shifts to weaker couplings with increasing system size, whereas the characteristic crossover scale associated with triplon-sector hybridization exhibits a much weaker size dependence. Furthermore, eigenstate-to-eigenstate fluctuations exhibit scaling consistent with the eigenstate thermalization hypothesis even while remnants of the strong-rung structure persist, suggesting that spectral chaos does not necessarily require complete sector hybridization.

</details>


### [130] [Optical response of quantum metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19195)
*Erik van Heumen,Martin Dressel,Simone Fratini*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文指出，在强关联量子材料中，用弛豫率描述电导在无长寿命载流子的奇异金属区失效；输运由快速衰减的电流涨落与量子细致平衡决定，从而自然给出Planckian耗散。文中主张改用电流-电流关联函数分析实验，并在铜氧化物中发现奇异金属响应贯穿所有掺杂，而最佳掺杂以上出现第二个经典分量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联量子系统的电输运性质长期存在争议。既有理论多假设耗散来自载流子总动量的弛豫，或将其归因于准粒子自能修正、流体力学集体激发；但当载流子寿命极短、经典描述失效时，需要新的框架解释奇异金属中观察到的Planckian耗散。

Method: 作者从量子输运出发，论证在奇异金属区仅用弛豫率描述电导不再充分，转而以快速衰减的电流涨落和量子细致平衡刻画电导。并提出用电流-电流关联函数而非光学电导率分析实验数据，以区分经典与量子金属行为；随后将该方法应用于铜氧化物超导体。

Result: 分析表明，奇异金属输运由快速衰减的电流涨落主导，结合量子细致平衡可产生广泛观察到的Planckian耗散。对铜氧化物的分析揭示奇异金属响应存在于所有掺杂水平，并在最佳掺杂以上出现第二个经典输运分量；该方法还凸显超导、赝能隙等相关特征。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了统一理解奇异金属电输运的量子框架：电导不应仅由弛豫率解释，而应由电流涨落和量子细致平衡描述。电流-电流关联函数分析是区分经典与量子金属的有力工具，并为超导、赝能隙及金属性质研究提供新视角，同时指出理论发展的关键方向。

Abstract: Strongly interacting quantum systems display puzzling electrical transport properties whose interpretation remains controversial. Many theoretical and phenomenological analyses assume that the complex behaviour observed in such `quantum materials' originates either from quasi-particles dressed with an anomalous electron self-energy or from collective excitations associated with hydrodynamic flow. Despite their distinctly different starting points, the underlying assumption is that dissipation of electrical current arises from the relaxation of the total momentum of charge carriers. This classical description breaks down in the absence of long-lived charge carriers. In such strange metals dissipation seems to be governed by the rate at which equilibrium is thermally restored, and it has been suggested that this constitutes an upper bound, known as the `Planckian limit'. Here we show that in this quantum regime a description of the conductivity in terms of relaxation rates no longer suffices: strange metal transport is carried instead by fast-decaying current fluctuations, which together with quantum detailed balance give rise to the widely observed Planckian dissipation. The distinction between classical and quantum metals becomes transparent when experimental data is analysed in terms of the current-current correlation function instead of the optical conductivity. When applied to the cuprate superconductors, such analysis uncovers that the strange metal response is present at all doping levels, and that a second, classical component develops above optimal doping. This simple, but powerful, approach further highlights essential features associated with superconductivity, (pseudo-)gaps, provides fresh insight in the properties of metals, and identifies key areas where progress in our theoretical understanding is needed.

</details>


### [131] [Fragmentation in Non-Hermitian Many-Body Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19205)
*Stefano Longhi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究随机虚规范场非厄米费米链中单粒子反常非厄米趋肤局域与有限填充下多体密度碎裂的联系，并揭示其动力学涌现和Coulomb气体描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非厄米趋肤效应与Anderson局域化均不能描述随机虚规范场下强涨落、亚指数衰减的单粒子局域行为；同时有限填充时Pauli排斥如何改变非厄米趋肤积累尚不清楚。

Method: 采用精确自由费米子方法研究空间随机虚规范场非厄米费米链，分析单粒子本征态、有限填充密度分布及其非厄米时间演化，并用随机势中的Coulomb气体模型进行描述。

Result: 单粒子本征态呈不规则非厄米趋肤局域，具强涨落和亚指数衰减；有限填充下Pauli排斥抑制宏观积累，使粒子密度碎裂为高/低占据的分离区域，形成空间分开的Fermi畴，其数目N_FS随系统尺寸L亚扩展。该碎裂不仅在平衡态出现，也在非厄米演化中动力学涌现，而厄米系统不会动力学选择此类结构。

Conclusion: 随机虚规范场诱导的反常非厄米趋肤局域与多体实空间密度碎裂直接相关，可通过随机势Coulomb气体统一描述，并揭示非厄米动力学可选择性产生厄米系统中不出现的碎片化结构。

Abstract: In non-Hermitian (NH) fermionic chains with spatially random imaginary gauge fields, single-particle eigenstates exhibit erratic NH skin localization with strongly fluctuating, subexponential decay, distinct from both conventional skin accumulation and Anderson localization. At finite filling, Pauli exclusion suppresses macroscopic accumulation and instead leads to fragmentation of the particle density, in sharp contrast to Hermitian disordered systems. Using exact free-fermion methods, we show that the density organizes into disconnected regions of high and low occupancy, forming spatially separated Fermi domains whose number $N_{\mathrm{FS}}$ scales subextensively with system size $L$. Remarkably, fragmentation is not only an equilibrium property but also emerges dynamically in the NH evolution, unlike in Hermitian systems where such structures are not dynamically selected. This behavior admits a Coulomb-gas description in a stochastic potential, establishing a direct link between erratic NH skin localization and real-space fragmentation of many-body states.

</details>


### [132] [Charge-Transfer Energy Governed by $f$-$d$ Coulomb Interaction in Rare-Earth Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19781)
*Norimasa Sasabe,Shinji Watanabe,Akira Yasui,Naomi Kawamura,Kojiro Mimura,Takayuki Uozumi,Masaichiro Mizumaki,Hitoshi Sato*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文建立了一个将f-d库仑相互作用与电荷转移能联系起来的谱学框架，并用YbInCu4的价态转变验证了该框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: f-d库仑相互作用在稀土体系价态涨落和量子价态临界性中很重要，但核心能级谱通常用唯象电荷转移能Δ分析，两者关系不清。

Method: 作者提出Δ中包含由d电子占据数n_d决定的f-d库仑贡献U_fd n_d，从而定义修正后的电荷转移能Δ'；并对YbInCu4联合分析核心能级光电子谱和L边X射线吸收谱。

Result: 联合谱学分析表明，YbInCu4在一阶价态转变前后观测到的谱学演化可由Δ'的变化一致解释。

Conclusion: 该工作澄清了电荷转移能与f-d库仑相互作用之间的关系，为从核心能级谱理解稀土价态涨落提供了统一框架。

Abstract: The $f$-$d$ Coulomb interaction ($U_{\rm fd}$) plays a central role in low-energy theories describing valence fluctuations and quantum valence criticality in rare-earth systems. In contrast, core-level spectroscopies have conventionally been analyzed using the phenomenological charge-transfer energy ($\Delta$), and the relationship between these two descriptions has remained unclear. Here, we develop a spectroscopic framework demonstrating that the charge-transfer energy contains an $f$-$d$ Coulomb contribution $U_{\rm fd}n_d$ arising from the $d$-electron occupation ($n_d$), leading to the charge-transfer energy $\Delta'$. We apply this framework to the prototypical valence-transition compound YbInCu$_4$ and demonstrate, from combined analyses of core-level photoemission and $L$-edge x-ray absorption spectroscopy, that the observed spectroscopic evolution across the first-order valence transition is consistently interpreted in terms of the variation of $\Delta'$.

</details>


### [133] [Compact Projected Pfaffian States for Rydberg-Blockaded Quantum Spin Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19790)
*Ryui Kaneko*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 紧凑的投影Pfaffian变分波函数可高精度描述非可积PXP模型基态，并适用于一维和二维阻塞约束系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PXP模型非可积且阻塞约束使希尔伯特空间指数增大，需要参数少、精度高的变分波函数来刻画其基态，并检验投影Pfaffian构造在约束自旋体系中的适用性。

Method: 将Gutzwiller投影和Rydberg-blockade投影分别作用于一般费米子配对态，使每个允许阻塞构型的振幅由配置选择的配对矩阵Pfaffian给出；以已知无阻挫PXP变形的精确阻塞投影乘积态为初态，通过变分蒙特卡洛优化O(N)个对称允许配对参数。

Result: 一维N格点体系优化后得到近精确基态，归一化能量方差V-score约10^{-9}；二维方格上相同振幅结构的O(N)参数波函数在精确对角化可达团簇上相对能量误差低于0.2%，磁化与现有基准一致。

Conclusion: 同一投影Pfaffian构造可统一用于一维和二维阻塞约束系统，以紧凑参数化实现高精度基态描述。

Abstract: We numerically demonstrate that a compact projected Pfaffian ansatz effectively describes the ground state of the nonintegrable PXP model. The variational wave function is obtained by applying separate Gutzwiller and Rydberg-blockade projectors to a general fermionic paired state, and each blockade-allowed configuration has an amplitude given by the Pfaffian of a configuration-selected pairing matrix. The exact blockade-projected product state of a known frustration-free deformation of the PXP model lies within the variational manifold and provides a physically motivated initial state for the variational Monte Carlo optimization toward the ground state of the pure PXP model. For a one-dimensional system of $N$ sites, optimizing all symmetry-allowed pairings in an $O(N)$-parameter wave function yields a near-exact ground state with a normalized energy variance (V-score) of order $10^{-9}$ despite the exponentially large blockade-constrained Hilbert space. On the square lattice, an $O(N)$-parameter wave function with the same amplitude structure yields relative energy errors below $0.2\%$ for clusters accessible to exact diagonalization and produces magnetizations consistent with available benchmarks. These results show that the same projected Pfaffian construction can be applied to blockade-constrained systems in both one and two dimensions.

</details>


### [134] [Exact environment checkpointing for memory-limited DMRG and TDVP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19801)
*Nihal Krishna Shrivastava,Rudranil Basu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作将检查点（checkpointing）引入有限 MPS 扫描算法（DMRG/TDVP），只保留每 k 个环境张量并在需要时重建中间环境，从而显著降低环境栈内存，同时保持确定性收缩下的逐位可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DMRG/TDVP 等有限矩阵乘积态扫描通常逐位存储一个环境张量；在阻挫和二维系统所需的大键维下，环境栈而非 MPS 本身会成为显存/内存瓶颈。

Method: 每隔 k 个环境保留一个检查点，取 k≈√N（N 为格点数）；扫描返回时通过重复原始收缩重建中间环境块。由于重建的是相同确定性收缩，能量与态可逐位复现。方法可与 offloading 结合，且无需主机内存或磁盘。

Result: 在 96–384 位点柱面上环境内存降低 4.6–10 倍。144 位点三角 J1-J2 柱面、141 GB 单 GPU、八收缩块下，半扫描重建检查点的键维搜索获得 1.77（单层）和 2.07（双层）加速；跨半扫描保留检查点时单层开销为 6–15%，取决于局域本征求解器。D=1536 时，八块双层调度扫描时间为同分块存储基线的 1.42 倍、未分块存储基线的 1.79 倍。简单内存与运行时间模型可在几个百分点内复现测量。

Conclusion: 检查点式环境管理能有效缓解大键维有限 MPS 扫描的内存瓶颈，性能开销可控且结果精确可复现，并已为 ITensorMPS.jl、TeNPy、MPSKit.jl 和 quimb 提供实现。

Abstract: Finite matrix-product-state sweep algorithms, such as the density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) and the time-dependent variational principle (TDVP), store one environment tensor per site. At the bond dimensions needed for frustrated and two-dimensional systems this stack, rather than the state itself, exhausts the memory of the device. We adapt checkpointing to these sweeps: every $k$-th environment is kept, with $k\approx\sqrt{N}$ for $N$ sites, and the blocks in between are rebuilt when the sweep returns to them. The rebuild repeats the original contractions, so energies and states are reproduced bit for bit when the contractions are deterministic. Environment memory falls by 4.6-10x on cylinders of 96-384 sites. Bond-dimension searches with an implementation that rebuilds checkpoints at each half-sweep give gains of 1.77 for one level and 2.07 for two levels with eight contraction chunks on a single 141 GB GPU, using a 144-site triangular $J_1$-$J_2$ cylinder and stored baselines with matching chunking. Separate timings with checkpoints retained across half-sweeps give a one-level overhead of 6-15%, depending on the local eigensolver. At $D=1536$ the two-level schedule with eight chunks takes 1.42 times the sweep time of the equally chunked stored baseline, or 1.79 times that of the unchunked stored baseline. Simple models of the memory and the run time reproduce the measurements to within a few per cent. Unlike offloading, the method needs no host memory or disk, and the two can be combined. Implementations are provided for ITensorMPS.jl, TeNPy, MPSKit.jl and quimb.

</details>


### [135] [Spectral Moments of the Projected Hubbard Configuration Graph: Connectivity, Exchange, and Virtual Doublons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.19929)
*D. Souza*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究强排斥 Hubbard 模型最低带 H∞ 的谱矩结构，给出有限图/方晶格少费米子的精确 μ2、μ4 公式，并用 Schrieffer-Wolff、精确对角化和 Lean 4 验证解释能带方差及 Peierls 通量下束缚对的 2e 相位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强 U 下投影掉双占位后的低能带决定交换与配对动力学；需要超越一阶交换的精确矩和通量响应，尤其区分单电荷与束缚对相位。

Method: 以 H∞=P0 Ht P0 为对象，用置换/图组合计数计算少费米子谱矩；大 U 下做二阶 Schrieffer-Wolff 并检验 Hilbert-Schmidt 正交性；在 L×L 环面加 Peierls 相位；用精确对角化数值校验，并用 Lean 4 机器验证部分结论。

Result: (1) 任意 N≥2 图、自旋无关 Hermitian hopping、任意自旋组成，μ2=n(N-n)/(N(N-1)) Σ_{i≠j}|tij|^2。(2) μ4 精确线性依赖于同自旋配对计数；均匀简单图 N≥4 的斜率 α(N,n)=-8C4(N-n)(8N-11n-2)/[N(N-1)(N-2)(N-3)]，由 4-环数 C4 决定。(3) 大有限 U 下二阶 SW 修正在无三角图上与 H∞ Hilbert-Schmidt 正交，故带方差无 1/U 项，U^-2 系数为负（低填充 ED 检查）。(4) Peierls 通量下交换斜率 α(N,2,Φ)=-64/(N-1)cosΦ□（L=3 或 L≥5 的 L×L 环面）含普通电荷 e 谐波，而 doublon hopping 为 t_d e^{2iAij}；单-doublon 二阶 Hamiltonian 在边界 twist 下严格 π 周期（h/2e），两电子 U-band 数值符合至 O((2λ)^L/U^{L-1})。部分结果 Lean 4 机器验证。

Conclusion: 新结果是精确矩结构和 doubled phase 推导；排斥束缚与配对电荷 2e 已知；不对多对相刚度、ODLRO 或超导性作断言。

Abstract: At strong on-site repulsion, the lowest band of $n$ lattice fermions is the spectrum of the hopping projected onto configurations without double occupancy, $H_\infty=P_0H_tP_0$. We study its moments, for few fermions in the square-lattice Hubbard model and, where the arguments allow, on any finite graph. (1) For spin-independent Hermitian hopping (no on-site terms) on any $N\geq2$-site graph, $\mu_2=\frac{n(N-n)}{N(N-1)}\sum_{i\neq j}|t_{ij}|^2$, for any spin composition. (2) On any finite graph, $\mu_4$ is exactly linear in the same-spin pair count, as four hops permute particles by at most a transposition or 3-cycle; for uniform hopping on a simple graph with $N\geq4$ the slope is $\alpha(N,n)=-8C_4(N-n)(8N-11n-2)/[N(N-1)(N-2)(N-3)]$, fixed by the number $C_4$ of 4-cycles. (3) At large finite $U$, the second-order Schrieffer-Wolff correction is Hilbert-Schmidt orthogonal to $H_\infty$ on every triangle-free graph, so the band variance has no $1/U$ term; its $U^{-2}$ coefficient, an exact trace checked by exact diagonalization, is negative at the low fillings computed. (4) Under a Peierls flux, the exchange slope $\alpha(N,2,\Phi)=-\frac{64}{N-1}\cos\Phi_\square$ ($L\times L$ tori, $L=3$ or $L\geq5$) carries the ordinary charge-$e$ harmonic, while the doublon (bound-pair) hopping acquires a doubled phase, $t_de^{2iA_{ij}}$: the second-order single-doublon Hamiltonian is exactly $\pi$-periodic ($h/2e$) under a boundary twist, and the exact two-electron U-band follows numerically, up to $O((2\lambda)^L/U^{L-1})$. Result (1), the permutation bound, the orthogonality in (3) and the doubled phase are machine-checked in Lean 4. Repulsive binding and the pair charge $2e$ are known; new are the exact moment structure and the derivation of the doubled phase. We make no claim about many-pair phase stiffness, off-diagonal long-range order or superconductivity.

</details>


### [136] [Multi-symmetry projected coupled cluster theory with triples corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20108)
*Ruiheng Song,Thomas M. Henderson,Gustavo E. Scuseria*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文为自旋与点群投影耦合簇PCCSD提出两种微扰三体修正 coherent (T) 与 +(T)，并在N2/C2解离、3d过渡金属单氧化物和Cr2上评估其恢复静态与动态相关平衡的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投影与耦合簇分别擅长描述静态相关和动态相关；PCCSD虽改善单双激发描述，但缺少三体相关，需引入微扰三体修正以兼顾两类相关并检验其系统适用性。

Method: 在投影CCSD框架下构造两种近似微扰三体修正：coherent (T)在投影网格上组合三体中间量，+(T)则用PCCSD振幅计算常规(T)表达式；无投影时二者均退化为常规(T)。

Result: 对六个单氧化物基准子集，coherent (T)将PCCSD平均绝对误差由13.4降至3.0 mEh，接近常规CCSD(T)；两种修正均恢复Cr2短键极小，coherent (T)的井深更接近外推DMRG，而+(T)给出更平衡的N2与C2解离曲线。

Conclusion: 微扰三体修正确实能提升PCCSD表现，但其效果依赖体系，说明仍需发展更系统的三体处理方案。

Abstract: Symmetry projection and coupled cluster theory provide complementary descriptions of static and dynamic correlation. We introduce two approximate perturbative triples corrections to spin- and point-group-projected coupled cluster with single and double excitations (PCCSD). The coherent $(T)$ correction combines triples intermediates over the projection grid, whereas $+(T)$ evaluates the conventional triples expression using PCCSD amplitudes. Both recover conventional $(T)$ in the absence of projection. We assess these corrections for N$_2$ and C$_2$ dissociation, $3d$ transition-metal monoxides, and Cr$_2$. Projection improves the singles-and-doubles description and distinguishes selected low-lying symmetry sectors. For the six-monoxide benchmark subset, coherent $(T)$ reduces the PCCSD mean absolute error from $13.4$ to $3.0~\mEh$, achieving accuracy comparable to conventional CCSD(T). Both triples corrections restore the short-bond minimum missing from the PCCSD curves of Cr$_2$, with coherent $(T)$ giving a well depth closer to extrapolated DMRG. In contrast, $+(T)$ provides more balanced N$_2$ and C$_2$ dissociation curves. These results demonstrate the benefits of perturbative triples in PCC, while their system-dependent performance highlights the need for a more systematic treatment.

</details>


### [137] [Self-doped correlated adatom lattices on cubic boron nitride](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20263)
*Niklas Enderlein,Tim Kullick,Henri Menke,Roland Gillen,Janina Maultzsch,Philipp Hansmann*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 综述cBN(111)表面三角吸附原子晶格，利用DFT+cRPA+电荷自洽DMFT筛选出12个强关联二维电子体系候选，多数为掺杂Mott绝缘体，并处于预言三角晶格非常规超导的参数区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在立方氮化硼(cBN)(111)表面构建三角吸附原子晶格，寻找具有强电子关联的二维电子系统候选材料。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论(DFT)结合约束随机相位近似(cRPA)和电荷自洽动力学平均场理论(DMFT)进行系统筛选。

Result: 识别出12个候选体系：N终止面上的III族吸附原子及B终止面上的V族吸附原子形成干净单带Hubbard系统；B终止面上的III族和过渡金属(V、Cr、Mn)吸附原子形成双带Hubbard系统。cBN的极性III-V特征导致偏离整数填充±1/4电子的自掺杂；强Hubbard/Hund相互作用与小跃迁使多数体系成为掺杂Mott绝缘体，准电子质量重整化可达4，局域磁矩可达2.5 μB。

Conclusion: 这些体系具有强关联、重准电子和显著局域磁矩，处于三角晶格上非常规超导已被预言的参数区域，是进一步研究强关联物理和超导的候选平台。

Abstract: We survey triangular adatom lattices on the (111) surfaces of cubic boron nitride (cBN) for strongly correlated two-dimensional electron systems. Using density functional theory combined with the constrained random phase approximation and charge self-consistent dynamical mean-field theory, we identify twelve promising candidates. Group-III (group-V) adatoms on the N- (B-)termination realize clean one-band Hubbard systems, while group-III and transition-metal (V, Cr, Mn) adatoms on the B-termination realize two-band Hubbard systems. Importantly, the polar III-V character of cBN induces self-doping by $\pm 1/4$ electron away from integer filling. With strong Hubbard (and Hund's) interactions and small hoppings, most systems are doped Mott insulators. Heavy quasielectrons with mass renormalizations up to 4 and strong local moments up to 2.5 $\mu_B$ put our systems into a regime where unconventional superconductivity on the triangular lattice has been predicted.

</details>


### [138] [Role of crystal-field degrees of freedom in square and rectangular skyrmion lattices in noncentrosymmetric tetragonal magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20661)
*Cong Guan,Yan S. Zha,Satoru Hayami*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在非中心对称方格子局域磁矩模型中，晶体场自由度可调控方形/矩形斯格明子晶格的稳定性、空间对称性与拓扑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究晶体场自由度在稳定方形和矩形斯格明子晶格中的作用，并揭示其微观机制。

Method: 采用自洽平均场计算；先考虑单一晶体场双重态，包含交换作用与极性型Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya相互作用，并进一步引入次近邻交换及第二晶体场双重态。

Result: 发现具有不同磁周期的方形斯格明子晶格，以及自发破缺面内四重旋转对称性的矩形斯格明子晶格；其稳定性和场致相序受晶体场波函数强烈控制，并出现矩形相的重入行为。次近邻交换改变这些态之间的竞争；引入第二双重态后出现具有不同调制模式的额外矩形斯格明子晶格、轴向单-Q态及多种双-Q态。

Conclusion: 晶体场自由度为调控多重-Q磁态的稳定性、空间对称性和拓扑性质提供了微观机制。

Abstract: We investigate the role of crystal-field degrees of freedom in stabilizing square and rectangular skyrmion lattices in a noncentrosymmetric localized-moment model on a square lattice. The magnetic phases are determined using self-consistent mean-field calculations. We first consider a single crystal-field doublet with exchange and polar-type Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya interactions. We find square skyrmion lattices with distinct magnetic periods, as well as a rectangular skyrmion lattice that spontaneously breaks the in-plane fourfold rotational symmetry. Their stability and field-induced phase sequences are strongly controlled by the crystal-field wave function, including reentrant behavior of the rectangular phase. Second-neighbor exchange further modifies the competition among these states. When a second crystal-field doublet is retained, additional rectangular skyrmion lattices with distinct modulation patterns emerge, together with axial single-$Q$ states and various double-$Q$ states. These results demonstrate that crystal-field degrees of freedom provide a microscopic mechanism for tuning the stability, spatial symmetry, and topology of multiple-$Q$ magnetic states.

</details>


### [139] [Excitonic magnetism and competing valence bond solid instabilities in $d^4$ ruthenium compounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.20682)
*Aleksandra Krajewska,Tomohiro Takayama,Hidenori Takagi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该综述以 W_t/Δ_eff 为统一参数轴，梳理四类结构连接的 d4 钌化合物，说明其从 J_eff=0 Mott 态到激子磁性的演化，以及近量子临界点处由晶格几何和化学键决定的竞争基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: d4 Mott 绝缘体中自旋轨道耦合产生 J_eff=0 单态与 J_eff=1 三重态，交换作用使三重态色散；当三重态带宽超过有效能隙时可形成激子磁性，因此需要理解不同结构连接下电子基态如何选择。

Method: 系统综述代表性 d4 钌化合物，包括孤立八面体 K2RuCl6、角共享钙钛矿 Ca2RuO4、边共享蜂窝结构和角共享烧绿石，并将其映射到 W_t/Δ_eff 的概念参数轴。

Result: 表明 d4 钌化合物不仅呈现从 J_eff=0 Mott 态到激子磁性的演化，还包含自旋轨道耦合、交换相互作用、电子跳跃、晶体场和键共价性在相近能标上的多相竞争；在 W_t~Δ_eff 的激子量子临界点附近，晶格几何与化学键决定电子基态，常形成价键固体并抢先抑制激子量子临界性。

Conclusion: d4 钌体系是研究激子磁性与多机制竞争的重要平台；W_t/Δ_eff 框架将结构与电子相联系起来，并突出几何和成键在量子临界附近的关键作用。

Abstract: Spin-orbit entangled $d^4$ Mott insulators serve as a rich playground for unconventional magnetism and competing quantum electronic ground states. Intra-ionic spin-orbit coupling $\lambda_{\rm{SOC}}$ generates a non-magnetic $J_{\rm{eff}} = 0$ singlet ground state separated from an excited $J_{\rm{eff}} = 1$ triplet. The effective intra-atomic gap ${\Delta}_{\rm{eff}}$ between the ground state and lowest excited state is determined by $\lambda_{\rm{SOC}}$ and non-cubic crystal field ${\Delta}_{\rm{cf}}$. The intersite magnetic exchange $J_{\rm{ex}}$ induces dispersion of the excited $J_{\rm{eff}} = 1$ triplet. When the triplet bandwidth $W_{\rm{t}}$ overcomes ${\Delta}_{\rm{eff}}$, the system undergoes long-range magnetic ordering which is viewed as a condensation of spin-orbit excitons and called excitonic magnetism. In this review, we systematically survey representative $d^4$ ruthenium compounds across four distinct structural connectivities of Ru-ligand octahedra, from K$_2$RuCl$_6$ with isolated octahedra, corner-sharing perovskite Ca$_2$RuO$_4$, edge-sharing honeycombs, to corner-sharing pyrochlores, and map them onto a conceptual parameter axis of $W_{\rm{t}} / {\Delta}_{\rm{eff}}$. We demonstrate that $d^4$ ruthenium compounds not only represent evolution from a $J_{\rm{eff}} = 0$ Mott state to excitonic magnetism but encompass a broader competition of diverse electronic phases arising from spin-orbit coupling, exchange interactions, electron hopping, crystal field and bond covalency inhabiting similar energy scales. Near excitonic quantum critical point, where $W_{\rm{t}} \sim {\Delta}_{\rm{eff}}$, lattice geometry and chemical bonds of constituent atoms play a decisive role in selecting the electronic ground state, frequently driving valence bond solid formation that pre-empts excitonic quantum criticality.

</details>


### [140] [Hund physics in the quarter-filled two-band Hubbard-Kanamori model: Crystal-field splitting and non-local Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21021)
*Diego Florez-Ablan,Carlos Mejuto-Zaera,Massimo Capone*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究四分之一填充双轨道Hubbard-Kanamori模型中晶体场劈裂与非局域关联对弱Hund金属零温性质的影响，并检验Hund耦合指纹在轨道简并解除及团簇DMFT下的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该填充下每个格点仅有一个电子，Hund耦合作用并不直观，但此前有限温研究已发现其可定义弱Hund金属；作者旨在澄清其零温边界、对晶体场劈裂的敏感性以及非局域关联的作用。

Method: 采用单格点动力学平均场理论（DMFT）和基于选定组态相互作用求解器的两格点团簇DMFT，计算四分之一填充双轨道Hubbard-Kanamori模型的零温性质。

Result: 单格点DMFT给出的弱Hund金属边界与有限温结果一致；Hund耦合指纹对轨道简并解除相当稳健，可存活至约四分之一带宽的劈裂，超过两级Kondo屏蔽消失的劈裂尺度。团簇DMFT表明单格点图像总体稳健，尽管Mott局域化在更小相互作用下发生；金属相中格点间关联较弱且对J不敏感，局域态中出现最近邻铁磁与反铁轨道关联，并体现在格点间互信息中。

Conclusion: 弱Hund金属的Hund耦合特征在零温、晶体场劈裂和非局域关联下均表现出显著稳健性；非局域关联主要修正Mott局域化尺度，而金属相仍可由单格点DMFT较好描述。

Abstract: We address the effect of crystal-field splitting and non-local correlations in the quarter-filled two-orbital
  Hubbard-Kanamori model. The role of the Hund's coupling in this configuration is not obvious since the atomic ground state
  has only one electron per site. Yet, previous works identified fingerprints of the Hund's coupling defining a weak Hund's
  metal at least at finite temperature.
  Using single-site dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT) and two-site cellular DMFT based on a selected
  configuration-interaction solver we investigate the zero-temperature properties of the weak Hund's metal. Within DMFT we draw
  the boundary for the weak Hund's metal, which is consistent with previous finite-temperature results.
  Interestingly, we find that the fingerprints of the Hund's coupling are quite robust to the lifting of the orbital
  degeneracy and they survive up to splittings of a quarter of the bandwidth, well beyond those at which a two-stage Kondo
  screening disappears.
  Including non-local correlations in cluster DMFT demonstrates the robustness of the single-site DMFT picture, even if Mott
  localization occurs at smaller interactions. In the metal the intersite correlations are weak and insensitive to $J$, while
  in the localized state we find nearest-neighbor ferromagnetic, antiferro-orbital correlations that are manifest also in the
  mutual information between sites.

</details>


### [141] [Orbital-dependent electronic correlations and single-band character in layered palladates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.21138)
*Jing-Xuan Wang,Jia-Ming Wang,Rong-Qiang He,Zhong-Yi Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 采用全电荷自洽DFT+DMFT系统比较五种层状钯酸盐的轨道与层分辨电子关联，发现其整体处于弱至中等关联区，且Pd-d_{x^2-y^2}轨道关联强于Pd-d_{z^2}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层状钯酸盐具有与铜氧化物相似的结构和轨道特征，可作为4d电子体系研究电子关联的平台，并与铜酸盐的单带物理进行对比。

Method: 使用全电荷自洽密度泛函理论加动力学平均场理论（DFT+DMFT），计算LaPdO2、RbSr2PdO3、BaLaPdO2Cl2、La4Pd3O8和La5Pd4O10的轨道分辨与层分辨电子结构。

Result: 所有体系均保持金属性，低能态主要由Pd-d_{x^2-y^2}轨道贡献，其质量重整化强于Pd-d_{z^2}。LaPdO2和BaLaPdO2Cl2为中等关联，RbSr2PdO3关联更弱；三层La4Pd3O8和四层La5Pd4O10均为弱关联，Pd-d_{x^2-y^2}质量增强相近，内层与外层Pd位点差异很小。BaLaPdO2Cl2最接近铜酸盐式单带描述，而RbSr2PdO3保留具有Pd-d_{z^2}特征的额外电子口袋。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了层状钯酸盐中弱至中等、轨道依赖电子关联的比较图像，并指出BaLaPdO2Cl2是最接近铜酸盐单带行为的候选体系。

Abstract: Layered palladates provide a $4d$ platform for examining electronic correlations in materials with cuprate-like structural and orbital characteristics. Using fully charge-self-consistent density functional theory plus dynamical mean-field theory, we investigate the orbital- and layer-resolved electronic structures of LaPdO$_2$, RbSr$_2$PdO$_3$, BaLaPdO$_2$Cl$_2$, La$_4$Pd$_3$O$_8$, and La$_5$Pd$_4$O$_{10}$. Under the conditions studied, all compounds remain metallic, with low-energy states dominated by the Pd-$d_{x^2-y^2}$ orbital. This orbital exhibits stronger mass renormalization than the Pd-$d_{z^2}$ orbital. LaPdO$_2$ and BaLaPdO$_2$Cl$_2$ show moderate correlations, whereas RbSr$_2$PdO$_3$ is more weakly correlated. Both the trilayer La$_4$Pd$_3$O$_8$ and quadlayer La$_5$Pd$_4$O$_{10}$ remain weakly correlated, with similar Pd-$d_{x^2-y^2}$ mass enhancements and little difference between inner- and outer-layer Pd sites. Among the structures studied, BaLaPdO$_2$Cl$_2$ is closest to a cuprate-like single-band description, whereas RbSr$_2$PdO$_3$ retains an additional electron pocket with Pd-$d_{z^2}$ character. These results establish a comparative picture of weak to moderate, orbital-dependent electronic correlations in layered palladates.

</details>
